# Harmonia 1.1.20 — Complete Business and Enterprise Capability Catalogue

> **PASS 3 / STEP 4 — ADJUDICATED OFFLINE OVERLAY (002).** This is an **unpublished candidate preview**, not a governed 1.1.20 Continuum incorporation or Solution Architect acceptance of new Feature wording. It preserves Candidate 017 and applies accepted wording/representation clarifications plus the agreed E07.02.08 CT3. FC-042, FC-059 and FC-101 remain outside the accepted coverage group. See the separate change-set report and FC register.

**Candidate 017 — Candidate 014–016 preserved; adds reusable E03.07 governed bulk-data interconnect coverage; not accepted or governed.**
**Source branch / pinned commit:** `Capability-Map-Consolidation` / `fecb465cab9c8c5f6e9a6035a1f7c6780e7ca374`. Original Candidate 006 population is retained unchanged; Candidate 012, 013 and 014 additions are labelled. New locators are not governed canonical identities.
**Architecture:** CT1 → CT2 → CT3 → measurable Features. Business and Enterprise populations separate. All business-to-enterprise contribution hypotheses require review.

# Business Capabilities
## B01 — Care Participation & Access
People can understand their health and care options, obtain access, and participate on appropriate terms.

### B01.01 — Health Needs & Service Navigation
Help people recognise needs and find suitable sources of assistance.

#### B01.01.01 — Health Need Recognition Support
People can describe a concern and understand whether they need clinical care, wellbeing support or other assistance.

- **`B01.01.01.F01` — UnderstoodNextStep:** proportion of sampled enquiries where the person can identify an appropriate next action. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.01.F02` — Clear Concern Expression:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed health need recognition support scenarios demonstrating clear concern expression against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.01.F03` — High-Risk Need Recognition:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing health need recognition support scenarios with correct high-risk need recognition, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.01.F04` — Appropriate Self-Help Guidance:** proportion of independently checked health need recognition support instances satisfying the specified appropriate self-help guidance quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.01.F05` — Supported Escalation Choice:** proportion of representative health need recognition support scenarios in which supported escalation choice is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.01.02 — Suitable Service Identification
People can discover services appropriate to their stated need and circumstances.

- **`B01.01.02.F01` — RelevantServiceOptions:** proportion of tested needs with correctly relevant, usable service options. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.02.F02` — Needs-Matched Service Options:** proportion of independently checked suitable service identification instances satisfying the specified needs-matched service options quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.02.F03` — Service Suitability Explanation:** proportion of applicable suitable service identification requests or situations with verified service suitability explanation. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.02.F04` — Available Alternative Services:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in suitable service identification for which available alternative services is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.02.F05` — Service Contact Usability:** proportion of applicable suitable service identification interactions where service contact usability reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.01.03 — Accessible Service Navigation
People with differing access or communication needs can obtain practical navigation assistance.

- **`B01.01.03.F01` — AccessibleNavigationCompletion:** proportion of representative access scenarios completed without an avoidable navigation barrier. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.03.F02` — Accessible Language Pathway:** proportion of representative accessible service navigation scenarios in which accessible language pathway is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.03.F03` — Assisted Navigation Option:** proportion of representative accessible service navigation scenarios in which assisted navigation option is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.03.F04` — Navigation Barrier Escalation:** proportion of representative accessible service navigation scenarios in which navigation barrier escalation is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.01.03.F05` — Successful Service Connection:** proportion of applicable accessible service navigation requests or situations with verified successful service connection. *PROPOSED*.

### B01.02 — Care Entry & Access Arrangement
Make it possible to request, arrange and enter appropriate care.

#### B01.02.01 — Care Request Reception
A person or referring party can initiate a request for care and receive a clear disposition.

- **`B01.02.01.F01` — AcknowledgedCareRequest:** proportion of valid requests with an attributable receipt and disposition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.01.F02` — Complete Request Capture:** proportion of independently checked care request reception instances satisfying the specified complete request capture quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.01.F03` — Appropriate Request Classification:** proportion of independently checked care request reception instances satisfying the specified appropriate request classification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.01.F04` — Request Status Visibility:** proportion of sampled care request reception cases with accessible, accurate evidence of request status visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.01.F05` — Redirected Request Accountability:** proportion of sampled care request reception cases with accessible, accurate evidence of redirected request accountability. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.02.02 — Care Appointment Arrangement
Appropriate attendance or contact can be scheduled and confirmed.

- **`B01.02.02.F01` — Confirmed Access Arrangement:** proportion of valid arrangements confirmed with essential time, location and preparation details. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.02.F02` — AccessibleArrangementChange:** proportion of sampled changes that can be completed and confirmed through an appropriate access route. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.02.F03` — Suitable Appointment Choice:** proportion of representative care appointment arrangement scenarios in which suitable appointment choice is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.02.F04` — Accessible Booking Confirmation:** proportion of representative care appointment arrangement scenarios in which accessible booking confirmation is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.02.F05` — Preparation Requirements Visibility:** proportion of sampled care appointment arrangement cases with accessible, accurate evidence of preparation requirements visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.02.F06` — Appointment Change Continuity:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in care appointment arrangement for which appointment change continuity is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.02.03 — Urgent Care Entry
A person needing urgent assessment can enter an appropriate care pathway without inappropriate administrative delay.

- **`B01.02.03.F01` — TimelyUrgentAccess:** proportion of qualified urgent requests reaching the specified clinical access point within the applicable interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.03.F02` — Correct Urgency Recognition:** proportion of independently checked urgent care entry instances satisfying the specified correct urgency recognition quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.03.F03` — Immediate Access Direction:** proportion of applicable urgent care entry requests or situations with verified immediate access direction. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.03.F04` — Escalation of Access Delays:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the escalation of access delays outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.02.03.F05` — Urgent Entry Handover:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in urgent care entry for which urgent entry handover is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

### B01.03 — Eligibility & Entitlement Determination
Establish a person's basis and conditions for receiving services.

#### B01.03.01 — Service Eligibility Assessment
A person can have eligibility for a particular service assessed against applicable criteria.

- **`B01.03.01.F01` — ExplainedEligibilityDecision:** proportion of sampled eligibility decisions with intelligible reasons and applicable criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.01.F02` — Criteria-Based Assessment:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed service eligibility assessment scenarios demonstrating criteria-based assessment against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.01.F03` — Eligibility Evidence Sufficiency:** proportion of positive and negative service eligibility assessment test cases meeting the applicable eligibility evidence sufficiency criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.01.F04` — Decision Reason Visibility:** proportion of sampled service eligibility assessment cases with accessible, accurate evidence of decision reason visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.01.F05` — Review Route Availability:** proportion of sampled service eligibility assessment cases with accessible, accurate evidence of review route availability. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.03.02 — Entitlement Verification
A valid entitlement can be established or questioned before a funding or access decision.

- **`B01.03.02.F01` — CurrentEntitlementConfirmation:** proportion of determinations using correct, current entitlement evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.02.F02` — Authoritative Entitlement Source:** proportion of positive and negative entitlement verification test cases meeting the applicable authoritative entitlement source criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.02.F03` — Entitlement Currency Verification:** proportion of positive and negative entitlement verification test cases meeting the applicable entitlement currency verification criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.02.F04` — Scope of Entitlement Clarity:** proportion of positive and negative entitlement verification test cases meeting the applicable scope of entitlement clarity criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.02.F05` — Contested Entitlement Referral:** proportion of positive and negative entitlement verification test cases meeting the applicable contested entitlement referral criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.03.03 — Eligibility Exception Resolution
Ambiguous or contested eligibility can be reviewed without silently denying access.

- **`B01.03.03.F01` — ReviewableEligibilityException:** proportion of exception cases with an assigned, traceable resolution. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.03.F02` — Exception Ownership:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed eligibility exception resolution scenarios demonstrating exception ownership against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.03.F03` — Evidence-Grounded Review:** proportion of sampled eligibility exception resolution cases with accessible, accurate evidence of evidence-grounded review. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.03.F04` — Timely Exception Disposition:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely exception disposition outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.03.03.F05` — Explained Resolution Outcome:** proportion of sampled eligibility exception resolution cases with accessible, accurate evidence of explained resolution outcome. *PROPOSED*.

### B01.04 — Informed Participation & Choice
Support people in understanding, expressing preferences and participating in decisions.

#### B01.04.01 — Care Option Explanation
People can understand materially different care options and relevant consequences.

- **`B01.04.01.F01` — DemonstratedOptionUnderstanding:** proportion of sampled cases with evidence of suitable explanation and understanding assessment. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.01.F02` — Material Alternative Visibility:** proportion of sampled care option explanation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of material alternative visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.01.F03` — Benefit and Risk Clarity:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing care option explanation scenarios with correct benefit and risk clarity, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.01.F04` — Accessible Option Communication:** proportion of representative care option explanation scenarios in which accessible option communication is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.01.F05` — Opportunity to Seek Clarification:** proportion of representative care option explanation scenarios in which opportunity to seek clarification is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.04.02 — Preference Expression Support
People can express priorities and preferences for their care in an accessible manner.

- **`B01.04.02.F01` — RecordedRelevantPreferences:** proportion of applicable care decisions with a current, usable preference record. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.02.F02` — Accessible Preference Capture:** proportion of representative preference expression support scenarios in which accessible preference capture is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.02.F03` — Preference Currency:** proportion of representative preference expression support scenarios in which preference currency is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.02.F04` — Context-Appropriate Preference Use:** proportion of representative preference expression support scenarios in which context-appropriate preference use is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.02.F05` — Changed Preference Recognition:** proportion of representative preference expression support scenarios in which changed preference recognition is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.04.03 — Shared Care Decision Participation
Care decisions can reflect informed participation and applicable clinical responsibilities.

- **`B01.04.03.F01` — DocumentedSharedDecision:** proportion of sampled decisions with evidenced participation appropriate to the context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.03.F02` — Participation Opportunity:** proportion of representative shared care decision participation scenarios in which participation opportunity is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.03.F03` — Relevant Decision Alternatives:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in shared care decision participation for which relevant decision alternatives is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.03.F04` — Decision Responsibility Clarity:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed shared care decision participation scenarios demonstrating decision responsibility clarity against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.04.03.F05` — Respect for Expressed Preference:** proportion of representative shared care decision participation scenarios in which respect for expressed preference is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

### B01.05 — Representation & Carer Participation
Enable duly authorised advocates, carers and related people to assist care.

#### B01.05.01 — Authorised Care Representation
Representatives can participate in decisions or arrangements within their established authority.

- **`B01.05.01.F01` — Recognised Representative Participation:** proportion of sampled representation scenarios applying verified scope and participation correctly. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.01.F02` — Representation Scope Verification:** proportion of positive and negative authorised care representation test cases meeting the applicable representation scope verification criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.01.F03` — Representation Limit Visibility:** proportion of sampled authorised care representation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of representation limit visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.01.F04` — Valid Representation Currency:** proportion of independently checked authorised care representation instances satisfying the specified valid representation currency quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.01.F05` — Representation Decision Record:** proportion of sampled authorised care representation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of representation decision record. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.05.02 — Family and Carer Involvement
Appropriate family or carers can contribute information and support without being assumed to hold decision authority.

- **`B01.05.02.F01` — AppropriateCarerInvolvement:** proportion of applicable cases recording involvement and any limits correctly. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.02.F02` — Consent-Appropriate Carer Input:** proportion of positive and negative family and carer involvement test cases meeting the applicable consent-appropriate carer input criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.02.F03` — Carer Contribution Recognition:** proportion of sampled family and carer involvement cases with accessible, accurate evidence of carer contribution recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.02.F04` — Participation Boundary Clarity:** proportion of representative family and carer involvement scenarios in which participation boundary clarity is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.02.F05` — Carer Communication Continuity:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in family and carer involvement for which carer communication continuity is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.05.03 — Representation Change Management
Changes to representation or participation arrangements can be recognised promptly.

- **`B01.05.03.F01` — CurrentRepresentativeStatus:** proportion of sampled interactions using the applicable current representation arrangement. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.03.F02` — Timely Representation Update:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely representation update outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.03.F03` — Withdrawn Authority Recognition:** proportion of positive and negative representation change management test cases meeting the applicable withdrawn authority recognition criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.03.F04` — Affected Parties Notification:** proportion of applicable representation change management interactions where affected parties notification reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.05.03.F05` — Historical Authority Trace:** proportion of positive and negative representation change management test cases meeting the applicable historical authority trace criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.05.04 — Representation Authority Accountability **[NEW C012]**
Establish and review what a carer, advocate or responsible person can legitimately decide or access, without replacing the person’s own voice.

*Enterprise contributions: E05.06, E05.02, E07.02; findings: R01, R11.*

- **`B01.05.04.F01` — RepresentationBasisKnown:** Authority basis is documented and current. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B01.05.04.F02` — ScopeOfAssistanceClear:** Permitted support differs explicitly from permitted decision-making. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B01.05.04.F03` — RevokedAuthorityHonoured:** Expired authority no longer permits representative action. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B01.05.04.F04` — PersonPreferenceRecorded:** Represented person’s wishes remain separately visible. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B01.05.04.F05` — DisputedAuthorityEscalated:** Disputed representation triggers a documented review path. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### B01.06 — Self-Management & Wellbeing Support
Enable ongoing health actions and participation outside formal encounters.

#### B01.06.01 — Individual Health Action Support
People can undertake agreed actions that help them manage ongoing health or wellbeing.

- **`B01.06.01.F01` — SupportedSelfManagementProgress:** proportion of agreed self-management actions reviewed for progress. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.01.F02` — Agreed Action Clarity:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed individual health action support scenarios demonstrating agreed action clarity against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.01.F03` — Practical Action Accessibility:** proportion of representative individual health action support scenarios in which practical action accessibility is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.01.F04` — Action Support Follow-Up:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed individual health action support scenarios demonstrating action support follow-up against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.01.F05` — Changed Need Adjustment:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in individual health action support for which changed need adjustment is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.06.02 — Health Information Understanding
People can obtain and understand information appropriate to managing their own health.

- **`B01.06.02.F01` — UsableHealthGuidance:** proportion of evaluated materials understood by intended audience groups. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.02.F02` — Appropriate Health Literacy:** proportion of representative health information understanding scenarios in which appropriate health literacy is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.02.F03` — Multilingual Information Availability:** proportion of applicable health information understanding requests or situations with verified multilingual information availability. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.02.F04` — Misunderstanding Identification:** proportion of representative health information understanding scenarios in which misunderstanding identification is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.02.F05` — Information Source Clarity:** proportion of sampled health information understanding cases with accessible, accurate evidence of information source clarity. *PROPOSED*.

#### B01.06.03 — Change and Escalation Guidance
People can recognise when their circumstances warrant additional advice or care.

- **`B01.06.03.F01` — RecognisedEscalationNeed:** proportion of representative change scenarios where an appropriate response is identified. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.03.F02` — Observable Warning Sign Guidance:** proportion of applicable change and escalation guidance interactions where observable warning sign guidance reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.03.F03` — Escalation Contact Availability:** proportion of applicable change and escalation guidance interactions where escalation contact availability reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.03.F04` — Clear Urgency Differentiation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed change and escalation guidance scenarios demonstrating clear urgency differentiation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B01.06.03.F05` — Follow-Up after Escalation:** proportion of applicable change and escalation guidance interactions where follow-up after escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

## B02 — Clinical Assessment & Determination
Clinicians and people can establish, assess and revise clinical understanding and appropriate care intent.

### B02.01 — Clinical Context Establishment
Obtain and interpret what is known about the person's relevant circumstances and history.

#### B02.01.01 — Relevant Clinical Context Assembly
Clinicians can establish the relevant history, current circumstances and care context for a decision.

- **`B02.01.01.F01` — Context Available at Decision:** proportion of sampled decisions with required context available when needed. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.01.F02` — Relevant History Coverage:** proportion of sampled relevant clinical context assembly cases with accessible, accurate evidence of relevant history coverage. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.01.F03` — Context Source Traceability:** proportion of sampled relevant clinical context assembly cases with accessible, accurate evidence of context source traceability. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.01.F04` — Currency of Clinical Context:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in relevant clinical context assembly for which currency of clinical context is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.01.F05` — Important Missing Context Disclosure:** proportion of sampled relevant clinical context assembly cases with accessible, accurate evidence of important missing context disclosure. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.01.02 — Clinical History Reconciliation
Material inconsistencies or omissions in a clinical history can be recognised and addressed.

- **`B02.01.02.F01` — ReconciledClinicalHistory:** proportion of selected inconsistencies resolved or appropriately qualified. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.02.F02` — Conflicting History Recognition:** proportion of sampled clinical history reconciliation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of conflicting history recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.02.F03` — Medication and Allergy Reconciliation:** proportion of independently checked clinical history reconciliation instances satisfying the specified medication and allergy reconciliation quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.02.F04` — Patient-Contributed History Inclusion:** proportion of sampled clinical history reconciliation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of patient-contributed history inclusion. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.02.F05` — Explained History Reconciliation:** proportion of independently checked clinical history reconciliation instances satisfying the specified explained history reconciliation quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.01.03 — Contextual Information Qualification
Clinicians can distinguish verified, reported, provisional and unavailable context.

- **`B02.01.03.F01` — QualifiedContextUse:** proportion of assessed decisions carrying appropriate context limitations. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.03.F02` — Source Reliability Qualification:** proportion of independently checked contextual information qualification instances satisfying the specified source reliability qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.03.F03` — Relevance to Current Decision:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in contextual information qualification for which relevance to current decision is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.03.F04` — Uncertainty Visibility:** proportion of sampled contextual information qualification cases with accessible, accurate evidence of uncertainty visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.01.03.F05` — Conflicting Context Flagging:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed contextual information qualification scenarios demonstrating conflicting context flagging against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.01.04 — Clinically Meaningful Information Currency **[NEW C012]**
Enable clinicians to identify whether information is current, historic, disputed, incomplete or unavailable when establishing clinical context.

*Enterprise contributions: E02.01, E07.05, E01.02; findings: R09, R11.*

- **`B02.01.04.F01` — HistoricalStateQualified:** Clinician distinguishes historic state from current presentation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B02.01.04.F02` — UnavailableNotAssumedAbsent:** Service outage is not mistaken for negative clinical history. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B02.01.04.F03` — DisputedFactsVisible:** Relevant unresolved corrections are clearly identified. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B02.01.04.F04` — SourceAndTimeKnown:** Material facts include source, occurrence and documentation time. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B02.01.04.F05` — CriticalContextIntegrity:** Missing critical context is flagged before consequential clinical decisions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### B02.02 — Health & Functional Assessment
Determine current clinical, functional and support needs.

#### B02.02.01 — Clinical Condition Assessment
The current clinical state and presenting concern can be assessed within appropriate expertise.

- **`B02.02.01.F01` — CompletedRelevantAssessment:** proportion of sampled assessments covering required domains for the clinical context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.01.F02` — Comprehensive Presenting Assessment:** proportion of representative clinical condition assessment scenarios in which comprehensive presenting assessment is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.01.F03` — Clinically Appropriate Observation:** proportion of independently checked clinical condition assessment instances satisfying the specified clinically appropriate observation quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.01.F04` — Assessment Finding Communication:** proportion of applicable clinical condition assessment interactions where assessment finding communication reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.01.F05` — Reassessment Trigger Recognition:** proportion of sampled clinical condition assessment cases with accessible, accurate evidence of reassessment trigger recognition. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.02.02 — Functional Ability Assessment
A person's functional capacities and limitations can be established where relevant.

- **`B02.02.02.F01` — AssessedFunctionalNeeds:** proportion of applicable care decisions informed by an appropriate functional assessment. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.02.F02` — Daily Function Coverage:** proportion of applicable functional ability assessment requests or situations with verified daily function coverage. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.02.F03` — Person-Centred Function Goals:** proportion of assessed functional ability assessment cases with observable person-centred function goals against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.02.F04` — Environmental Factors Inclusion:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed functional ability assessment scenarios demonstrating environmental factors inclusion against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.02.F05` — Function Change Visibility:** proportion of sampled functional ability assessment cases with accessible, accurate evidence of function change visibility. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.02.03 — Health Needs Reassessment
Assessments can be revised when a person's condition or circumstances change.

- **`B02.02.03.F01` — TimelyNeedsReassessment:** proportion of qualifying changes followed by appropriate reassessment. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.03.F02` — Meaningful Change Recognition:** proportion of sampled health needs reassessment cases with accessible, accurate evidence of meaningful change recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.03.F03` — Prior Assessment Comparison:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed health needs reassessment scenarios demonstrating prior assessment comparison against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.03.F04` — Updated Need Communication:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in health needs reassessment for which updated need communication is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.02.03.F05` — Timely Reassessment:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely reassessment outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.

### B02.03 — Clinical Investigation Direction
Decide what further evidence is needed and why.

#### B02.03.01 — Investigation Need Determination
Clinicians can decide what further investigation is justified by the clinical question.

- **`B02.03.01.F01` — JustifiedInvestigationIntent:** proportion of sampled requests with an explicit, relevant clinical indication. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.01.F02` — Investigative Purpose Clarity:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed investigation need determination scenarios demonstrating investigative purpose clarity against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.01.F03` — Proportionate Investigation Selection:** proportion of applicable investigation need determination requests or situations with verified proportionate investigation selection. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.01.F04` — Avoidance of Unnecessary Testing:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed investigation need determination scenarios demonstrating avoidance of unnecessary testing against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.01.F05` — Patient-Circumstance Consideration:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed investigation need determination scenarios demonstrating patient-circumstance consideration against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.03.02 — Investigation Prioritisation
Investigation urgency and intended timing can reflect clinical need.

- **`B02.03.02.F01` — CorrectInvestigationPriority:** proportion of tested requests assigned an appropriate priority. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.02.F02` — Clinical Priority Accuracy:** proportion of independently checked investigation prioritisation instances satisfying the specified clinical priority accuracy quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.02.F03` — Priority Change Recognition:** proportion of sampled investigation prioritisation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of priority change recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.02.F04` — Urgent Investigation Escalation:** proportion of applicable investigation prioritisation interactions where urgent investigation escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.02.F05` — Priority Rationale Visibility:** proportion of sampled investigation prioritisation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of priority rationale visibility. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.03.03 — Investigation Selection Review
Investigation choices can be reviewed to avoid unnecessary duplication or missed information.

- **`B02.03.03.F01` — AppropriateInvestigationSelection:** proportion of sampled investigation plans meeting documented appropriateness criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.03.F02` — Investigation Appropriateness Review:** proportion of independently checked investigation selection review instances satisfying the specified investigation appropriateness review quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.03.F03` — Contraindication Visibility:** proportion of sampled investigation selection review cases with accessible, accurate evidence of contraindication visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.03.F04` — Redundant Investigation Recognition:** proportion of sampled investigation selection review cases with accessible, accurate evidence of redundant investigation recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.03.03.F05` — Revised Investigation Plan:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed investigation selection review scenarios demonstrating revised investigation plan against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

### B02.04 — Diagnostic & Clinical Reasoning
Interpret findings and establish or revise clinical understanding.

#### B02.04.01 — Clinical Finding Interpretation
Clinicians can interpret findings in relevant patient and clinical context.

- **`B02.04.01.F01` — ContextualFindingInterpretation:** proportion of evaluated interpretations containing required context and caveats. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.01.F02` — Finding Context Adequacy:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed clinical finding interpretation scenarios demonstrating finding context adequacy against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.01.F03` — Uncertain Finding Identification:** proportion of independently checked clinical finding interpretation instances satisfying the specified uncertain finding identification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.01.F04` — Contradictory Finding Review:** proportion of sampled clinical finding interpretation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of contradictory finding review. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.01.F05` — Communicated Clinical Significance:** proportion of applicable clinical finding interpretation interactions where communicated clinical significance reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.04.02 — Diagnostic Understanding Revision
New evidence can appropriately confirm, refine or challenge the current clinical understanding.

- **`B02.04.02.F01` — EvidenceResponsiveDiagnosticRevision:** proportion of material new findings prompting documented diagnostic reconsideration. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.02.F02` — New-Evidence Integration:** proportion of sampled diagnostic understanding revision cases with accessible, accurate evidence of new-evidence integration. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.02.F03` — Diagnostic Alternative Review:** proportion of sampled diagnostic understanding revision cases with accessible, accurate evidence of diagnostic alternative review. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.02.F04` — Prior Reasoning Traceability:** proportion of sampled diagnostic understanding revision cases with accessible, accurate evidence of prior reasoning traceability. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.02.F05` — Revised Understanding Communication:** proportion of representative diagnostic understanding revision scenarios in which revised understanding communication is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.04.03 — Diagnostic Uncertainty Communication

- **`B02.04.03.F01` — TransparentDiagnosticUncertainty:** proportion of sampled uncertain cases with recorded uncertainty and follow-up intent. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.03.F02` — Uncertainty Explanation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed diagnostic uncertainty communication scenarios demonstrating uncertainty explanation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.03.F03` — Confidence Limitation Visibility:** proportion of sampled diagnostic uncertainty communication cases with accessible, accurate evidence of confidence limitation visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.03.F04` — Follow-Up Investigation Advice:** proportion of applicable diagnostic uncertainty communication interactions where follow-up investigation advice reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.04.03.F05` — Appropriate Stakeholder Notification:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic uncertainty communication instances satisfying the specified appropriate stakeholder notification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

### B02.05 — Clinical Risk & Priority Determination
Determine urgency, risk and priority of care.

#### B02.05.01 — Clinical Risk Appraisal
Clinical risks can be identified and assessed to inform decisions.

- **`B02.05.01.F01` — RecordedMaterialRisk:** proportion of applicable cases with relevant risk factors considered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.01.F02` — Relevant Risk Factor Coverage:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical risk appraisal scenarios with correct relevant risk factor coverage, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.

- **`B02.05.01.F04` — Mitigation Need Recognition:** proportion of sampled clinical risk appraisal cases with accessible, accurate evidence of mitigation need recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.01.F05` — Risk Change Sensitivity:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical risk appraisal scenarios with correct risk change sensitivity, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.05.02 — Urgency and Acuity Determination
The relative urgency of care can be determined from clinical presentation and circumstances.

- **`B02.05.02.F01` — AppropriateClinicalPriority:** proportion of tested presentations assigned an appropriate response priority. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.02.F02` — Clinically Consistent Acuity Assignment:** proportion of independently checked urgency and acuity determination instances satisfying the specified clinically consistent acuity assignment quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.02.F03` — Urgent Priority Notification:** proportion of applicable urgency and acuity determination interactions where urgent priority notification reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.02.F04` — Changed Acuity Recognition:** proportion of sampled urgency and acuity determination cases with accessible, accurate evidence of changed acuity recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.02.F05` — Appropriate Escalation Path:** proportion of independently checked urgency and acuity determination instances satisfying the specified appropriate escalation path quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### B02.05.03 — Risk Reassessment and Escalation
Changing risks can lead to appropriate reassessment and escalation.

- **`B02.05.03.F01` — ResponsiveRiskEscalation:** proportion of prescribed escalation scenarios managed within applicable clinical conditions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.03.F02` — Risk Trigger Recognition:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing risk reassessment and escalation scenarios with correct risk trigger recognition, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.03.F03` — Timely Clinical Escalation:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely clinical escalation outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.03.F04` — Escalation Receipt Confirmation:** proportion of applicable risk reassessment and escalation interactions where escalation receipt confirmation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B02.05.03.F05` — Reassessment Outcome Trace:** proportion of sampled risk reassessment and escalation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of reassessment outcome trace. *PROPOSED*.

## B03 — Clinical Care & Response
Indicated clinical care and direct assistance can be provided, observed, adapted and continued safely.

### B03.01 — Direct Clinical Intervention
Provide indicated clinical treatments or procedures.

#### B03.01.01 — Indicated Treatment Delivery
Appropriate treatment can be delivered to a person under the relevant clinical responsibility.

- **`B03.01.01.F01` — CompletedIndicatedIntervention:** proportion of prescribed interventions delivered or appropriately deferred with reason. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.01.F02` — Treatment Identity Confirmation:** proportion of independently checked indicated treatment delivery instances satisfying the specified treatment identity confirmation quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.01.F03` — Correct Treatment Execution:** proportion of independently checked indicated treatment delivery instances satisfying the specified correct treatment execution quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.01.F04` — Informed Treatment Readiness:** proportion of applicable indicated treatment delivery requests or situations with verified informed treatment readiness. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.01.F05` — Treatment Outcome Capture:** proportion of assessed indicated treatment delivery cases with observable treatment outcome capture against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.

#### B03.01.02 — Treatment Response Review
The effects and adverse responses of a treatment can be evaluated.

- **`B03.01.02.F01` — EvaluatedTreatmentResponse:** proportion of qualifying interventions with required response review. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.02.F02` — Expected Response Monitoring:** proportion of sampled treatment response review cases with accessible, accurate evidence of expected response monitoring. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.02.F03` — Adverse Response Recognition:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing treatment response review scenarios with correct adverse response recognition, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.02.F04` — Treatment Effect Interpretation:** proportion of assessed treatment response review cases with observable treatment effect interpretation against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.02.F05` — Response-Based Reassessment:** proportion of applicable treatment response review interactions where response-based reassessment reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B03.01.03 — Treatment Adaptation
Clinical interventions can be adjusted when evidence or the person's circumstances warrant it.

- **`B03.01.03.F01` — ResponsiveTreatmentAdjustment:** proportion of sampled changed-condition cases with a documented treatment disposition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.03.F02` — Indication for Change Clarity:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in treatment adaptation for which indication for change clarity is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.03.F03` — Adaptation Authority Confirmation:** proportion of positive and negative treatment adaptation test cases meeting the applicable adaptation authority confirmation criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.03.F04` — Changed Treatment Communication:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in treatment adaptation for which changed treatment communication is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B03.01.03.F05` — Adaptation Effect Follow-Up:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in treatment adaptation for which adaptation effect follow-up is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B03.02.02 — Nursing Care Planning

- **`B03.02.02.F02` — Individual Nursing Need Coverage:** proportion of applicable nursing care planning requests or situations with verified individual nursing need coverage. *PROPOSED*.

## B04 — Diagnostic Investigation
Reliable diagnostic evidence can be requested, generated, interpreted, verified and communicated.

### B04.01 — Diagnostic Investigation Commissioning
Make an appropriate investigation available against a justified and valid request, including preparation and allocation of responsibility.

#### B04.01.01 — Diagnostic Request Qualification

#### B04.01.02 — Investigation Preparation Coordination

#### B04.01.03 — Diagnostic Priority Allocation

- **`B04.01.03.F01` — AppropriateDiagnosticPriority:** proportion of sampled investigations receiving justified priority. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.01.03.F02` — Clinically Appropriate Priority:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic priority allocation instances satisfying the specified clinically appropriate priority quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.01.03.F03` — Priority Decision Visibility:** proportion of sampled diagnostic priority allocation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of priority decision visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.01.03.F04` — Priority Change Response:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in diagnostic priority allocation for which priority change response is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.01.03.F05` — Delay Risk Escalation:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the delay risk escalation outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.

### B04.02 — Diagnostic Evidence Production
Establish observations, measurements, images or other evidence suitable for the intended investigation.

#### B04.02.01 — Specimen and Subject Identification
Diagnostic material or the examined subject can be reliably associated with the correct investigation.

- **`B04.02.01.F01` — CorrectSubjectAssociation:** proportion of audited evidence items with verified subject and request linkage. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.01.F02` — Correct Subject Matching:** proportion of independently checked specimen and subject identification instances satisfying the specified correct subject matching quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.01.F03` — Specimen Chain Integrity:** proportion of independently checked specimen and subject identification instances satisfying the specified specimen chain integrity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.01.F04` — Misidentification Detection:** proportion of independently checked specimen and subject identification instances satisfying the specified misidentification detection quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.01.F05` — Specimen Provenance Availability:** proportion of sampled specimen and subject identification cases with accessible, accurate evidence of specimen provenance availability. *PROPOSED*.

#### B04.02.02 — Diagnostic Measurement Acquisition
Required images, measurements, specimens or observations can be obtained at adequate quality.

- **`B04.02.02.F01` — UsableDiagnosticEvidence:** proportion of investigations producing evidence fit for intended interpretation. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.02.F02` — Measurement Method Suitability:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed diagnostic measurement acquisition scenarios demonstrating measurement method suitability against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.02.F03` — Measurement Quality Completeness:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic measurement acquisition instances satisfying the specified measurement quality completeness quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.02.F04` — Repeat Measurement Availability:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in diagnostic measurement acquisition for which repeat measurement availability is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.02.F05` — Measurement Source Trace:** proportion of sampled diagnostic measurement acquisition cases with accessible, accurate evidence of measurement source trace. *PROPOSED*.

#### B04.02.03 — Evidence Quality Exception Handling
Unusable or incomplete evidence can be identified and appropriately corrected or qualified.

- **`B04.02.03.F01` — TransparentEvidenceExceptions:** proportion of injected quality exceptions recognised before unqualified reporting. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.03.F02` — Invalid Evidence Recognition:** proportion of independently checked evidence quality exception handling instances satisfying the specified invalid evidence recognition quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.03.F03` — Quality Exception Attribution:** proportion of independently checked evidence quality exception handling instances satisfying the specified quality exception attribution quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.03.F04` — Repeat Acquisition Direction:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in evidence quality exception handling for which repeat acquisition direction is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.02.03.F05` — Quality Disposition Visibility:** proportion of independently checked evidence quality exception handling instances satisfying the specified quality disposition visibility quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

### B04.03 — Diagnostic Findings Reporting
Produce, communicate and appropriately escalate clinically usable findings within relevant professional authority.

#### B04.03.01 — Diagnostic Findings Interpretation
Appropriate specialists can prepare intelligible findings from diagnostic evidence.

- **`B04.03.01.F01` — ClinicallyUsableFinding:** proportion of reviewed reports containing required interpretation and qualifiers. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.01.F02` — Appropriate Reference Context:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic findings interpretation instances satisfying the specified appropriate reference context quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.01.F03` — Interpretation Qualification:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic findings interpretation instances satisfying the specified interpretation qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.01.F04` — Clinically Significant Finding Recognition:** proportion of sampled diagnostic findings interpretation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of clinically significant finding recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.01.F05` — Reviewable Finding Rationale:** proportion of sampled diagnostic findings interpretation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of reviewable finding rationale. *PROPOSED*.

#### B04.03.02 — Diagnostic Result Communication
Findings can reach the responsible care context with their significance preserved.

- **`B04.03.02.F01` — ConfirmedDiagnosticDelivery:** proportion of reportable results reaching the intended responsible destination. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.02.F02` — Correct Recipient Delivery:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic result communication instances satisfying the specified correct recipient delivery quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.02.F03` — Result Receipt Visibility:** proportion of sampled diagnostic result communication cases with accessible, accurate evidence of result receipt visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.02.F04` — Result Interpretation Context:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed diagnostic result communication scenarios demonstrating result interpretation context against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.02.F05` — Unreceived Result Escalation:** proportion of applicable diagnostic result communication interactions where unreceived result escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B04.03.03 — Critical Finding Communication
Time-critical findings can be escalated to the responsible recipient.

- **`B04.03.03.F01` — Acknowledged Critical Result:** proportion of critical findings acknowledged by a responsible recipient within the prescribed interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.03.F02` — CorrectCriticalResultRecipient:** proportion of critical findings directed to an appropriately accountable recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.03.F03` — Critical Finding Classification:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing critical finding communication scenarios with correct critical finding classification, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.03.F04` — Rapid Appropriate Notification:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the rapid appropriate notification outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.03.F05` — Recipient Identity Assurance:** proportion of independently checked critical finding communication instances satisfying the specified recipient identity assurance quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.03.03.F06` — Unacknowledged Finding Escalation:** proportion of applicable critical finding communication interactions where unacknowledged finding escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B04.03.04 — Critical Result Receipt & Follow-Up Confirmation **[NEW C014]**
Confirm that critical clinical findings reached an accountable recipient and that required follow-up obligations remain visible.

- **`B04.03.04.F01` — CriticalResultRecipientKnown:** A critical-result notification has an identifiable intended responsible recipient. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B04.03.04.F02` — ReceiptNotClinicalAction:** Notification delivery is not reported as clinical response completion. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B04.03.04.F03` — RequiredFollowUpTracked:** The clinical response obligation remains open until suitably disposed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B04.03.04.F04` — UnacknowledgedResultEscalated:** Unacknowledged critical findings trigger the specified escalation pathway. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B04.03.04.F05` — CommunicationEvidencePreserved:** The result, notification, acknowledgement and follow-up record remain attributable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-HIE-GATE, C-CT2-ACT. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### B04.04 — Diagnostic Quality & Safety Assurance
Establish appropriate identification, traceability, technical validity and safety of investigations and findings.

#### B04.04.01 — Diagnostic Process Validity
Investigations can be conducted under required quality and safety controls.

- **`B04.04.01.F01` — VerifiedProcessConformance:** proportion of assessed investigations satisfying relevant controls. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.01.F02` — Required Control Completion:** proportion of applicable diagnostic process validity requests or situations with verified required control completion. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.01.F03` — Process Deviation Recognition:** proportion of sampled diagnostic process validity cases with accessible, accurate evidence of process deviation recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.01.F04` — Validity Evidence Availability:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic process validity instances satisfying the specified validity evidence availability quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.01.F05` — Remediation of Invalid Investigation:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic process validity instances satisfying the specified remediation of invalid investigation quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### B04.04.02 — Verified Diagnostic Result Release
Diagnostic reports can be released only after necessary verification of source and quality.

- **`B04.04.02.F01` — Result Released with Verified Identity and Quality:** proportion of released reports passing required identity and quality checks. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.02.F02` — Authorised Result Release:** proportion of positive and negative verified diagnostic result release test cases meeting the applicable authorised result release criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.02.F03` — Preliminary Versus Final Status:** proportion of sampled verified diagnostic result release cases with accessible, accurate evidence of preliminary versus final status. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.02.F04` — Corrected Result Communication:** proportion of independently checked verified diagnostic result release instances satisfying the specified corrected result communication quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.02.F05` — Release Auditability:** proportion of sampled verified diagnostic result release cases with accessible, accurate evidence of release auditability. *PROPOSED*.

#### B04.04.03 — Diagnostic Traceability
Investigation, evidence, interpretation and report can be traced together.

- **`B04.04.03.F01` — TraceableDiagnosticLineage:** proportion of sampled results with an unbroken required investigation history. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.03.F02` — Source-to-Finding Linkage:** proportion of independently checked diagnostic traceability instances satisfying the specified source-to-finding linkage quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.03.F03` — Investigation Event Chronology:** proportion of sampled diagnostic traceability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of investigation event chronology. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.03.F04` — Method and Equipment Attribution:** proportion of sampled diagnostic traceability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of method and equipment attribution. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B04.04.03.F05` — Retained Investigation Evidence:** proportion of sampled diagnostic traceability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of retained investigation evidence. *PROPOSED*.

#### B04.04.04 — Diagnostic Information Release Accountability **[NEW C012]**
Determine that diagnostic results are validly released and traceable without equating transport receipt with clinical responsibility transfer.

*Enterprise contributions: E03.01, E03.04, E08.07; findings: R16.*

- **`B04.04.04.F01` — ResultVerifiedBeforeRelease:** Published diagnostic result has required verification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B04.04.04.F02` — ReleaseIdentityKnown:** Accountable releaser and release instant can be established. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B04.04.04.F03` — DeliveryNotClinicalAcceptance:** Technical delivery is distinguished from clinician review. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B04.04.04.F04` — CorrectionHistoryPreserved:** Corrected results retain historical traceability. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B04.04.04.F05` — CriticalResultEscalationTracked:** Urgent findings have a traceable acknowledgement/escalation path. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

## B05 — Care Coordination & Continuity
Care intentions, contributions and responsibilities can remain coherent over time and across boundaries.

### B05.01 — Multidisciplinary Care Coordination
Enable several parties to plan and contribute coherently.

#### B05.01.01 — Shared Care Intent Alignment
Different contributors can work from a coherent understanding of intended care.

- **`B05.01.01.F01` — AlignedMultidisciplinaryIntent:** proportion of sampled shared-care cases with a consistent documented plan. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.01.F02` — Shared Outcome Understanding:** proportion of representative shared care intent alignment scenarios in which shared outcome understanding is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.01.F03` — Care Goal Disagreement Recognition:** proportion of sampled shared care intent alignment cases with accessible, accurate evidence of care goal disagreement recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.01.F04` — Agreed Priority Visibility:** proportion of sampled shared care intent alignment cases with accessible, accurate evidence of agreed priority visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.01.F05` — Intent Update Communication:** proportion of applicable shared care intent alignment interactions where intent update communication reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.01.02 — Care Contribution Coordination
Required clinical and supporting contributions can be arranged without avoidable conflict.

- **`B05.01.02.F01` — CoordinatedCareContributions:** proportion of reviewed cases where required participants were engaged at the appropriate point. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.02.F02` — Contributor Responsibility Clarity:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed care contribution coordination scenarios demonstrating contributor responsibility clarity against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.02.F03` — Timely Contribution Requests:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely contribution requests outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.02.F04` — Contribution Status Visibility:** proportion of sampled care contribution coordination cases with accessible, accurate evidence of contribution status visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.02.F05` — Unfulfilled Contribution Escalation:** proportion of applicable care contribution coordination interactions where unfulfilled contribution escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.01.03 — Multidisciplinary Decision Review
Material differences of clinical opinion can be surfaced and reconciled or qualified.

- **`B05.01.03.F01` — ReviewableSharedCareDecision:** proportion of sampled multidisciplinary decisions with accountable disposition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.03.F02` — Appropriate Discipline Inclusion:** proportion of independently checked multidisciplinary decision review instances satisfying the specified appropriate discipline inclusion quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.03.F03` — Review Finding Record:** proportion of sampled multidisciplinary decision review cases with accessible, accurate evidence of review finding record. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.03.F04` — Decision Conflict Resolution:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed multidisciplinary decision review scenarios demonstrating decision conflict resolution against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.01.03.F05` — Review Action Follow-Up:** proportion of sampled multidisciplinary decision review cases with accessible, accurate evidence of review action follow-up. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.01.04 — Team Task Participation & Handover **[NEW C014]**
Enable multidisciplinary teams to participate in delegated or shared work without losing the accountability for each contribution.

- **`B05.01.04.F01` — TeamContributionOwnership:** Each assigned contribution has a clear responsible party and review status. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.01.04.F02` — SharedTaskPolicyVisible:** Aggregate or assigned-to-finish requirements are known to relevant participants. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.01.04.F03` — HandoverOutstandingWork:** Transferred responsibilities retain context and unfinished obligations. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.01.04.F04` — UnfinishedContributionVisible:** One participant’s completion does not silently complete all required team contributions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.01.04.F05` — ClinicalTeamContextProtected:** Team collaboration does not bypass patient consent or confidentiality boundaries. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-CT2-ACT, DRP-PR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### B05.02 — Referral & Transfer Coordination
Arrange movement to an appropriate provider, service or care setting.

#### B05.02.01 — Appropriate Referral Destination
A request for another service can be directed to an appropriate provider or setting.

- **`B05.02.01.F01` — SuitableReferralDestination:** proportion of accepted referrals meeting relevant destination criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.02.01.F02` — Needs-Matched Referral Selection:** proportion of independently checked appropriate referral destination instances satisfying the specified needs-matched referral selection quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

- **`B05.02.01.F04` — Referral Acceptance Feasibility:** proportion of applicable appropriate referral destination interactions where referral acceptance feasibility reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.02.01.F05` — Alternative Destination Availability:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in appropriate referral destination for which alternative destination availability is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.02.02 — Referral Acceptance and Tracking

- **`B05.02.02.F03` — Referral Disposition Visibility:** proportion of sampled referral acceptance and tracking cases with accessible, accurate evidence of referral disposition visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.02.02.F04` — Unaccepted Referral Escalation:** proportion of applicable referral acceptance and tracking interactions where unaccepted referral escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.02.02.F05` — Referral Closure Evidence:** proportion of sampled referral acceptance and tracking cases with accessible, accurate evidence of referral closure evidence. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.02.03 — Transport and Transfer Preparation
Necessary readiness for safe transfer can be coordinated across parties.

- **`B05.02.03.F01` — ReadyCareTransfer:** proportion of sampled transfers meeting applicable pre-transfer requirements. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.02.03.F02` — Transport Need Suitability:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed transport and transfer preparation scenarios demonstrating transport need suitability against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.02.03.F03` — Transfer Readiness Confirmation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in transport and transfer preparation for which transfer readiness confirmation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.02.03.F04` — Clinical Escort Arrangements:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed transport and transfer preparation scenarios demonstrating clinical escort arrangements against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.02.03.F05` — Contingency for Transfer Delay:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the contingency for transfer delay outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.

### B05.03 — Care Responsibility Transfer
Establish and communicate who is responsible for continuing care.

#### B05.03.01 — Clinical Handover Acceptance
Receiving care responsibility can be explicitly acknowledged in the applicable context.

- **`B05.03.01.F02` — OutstandingCareContinuity:** proportion of accepted transfers with consequential outstanding obligations identified. *PROPOSED*.

- **`B05.03.01.F05` — Timely Handover Confirmation:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely handover confirmation outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.03.01.F06` — Rejected Handover Resolution:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in clinical handover acceptance for which rejected handover resolution is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.03.02 — Handover Information Sufficiency
The receiving party can obtain the information necessary to continue care appropriately.

- **`B05.03.02.F05` — Missing Information Escalation:** proportion of applicable handover information sufficiency interactions where missing information escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.03.03 — Outstanding Obligation Transfer
Unfinished actions and follow-up responsibilities can be identified and assigned.

- **`B05.03.03.F01` — AccountableOutstandingCare:** proportion of outstanding actions with acknowledged next responsibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.03.03.F02` — Outstanding Action Identification:** proportion of independently checked outstanding obligation transfer instances satisfying the specified outstanding action identification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.03.03.F03` — Responsible Recipient Assignment:** proportion of applicable outstanding obligation transfer interactions where responsible recipient assignment reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.03.03.F04` — Due-Time Communication:** proportion of applicable outstanding obligation transfer interactions where due-time communication reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.03.03.F05` — Unaccepted Obligation Escalation:** proportion of applicable outstanding obligation transfer interactions where unaccepted obligation escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.03.04 — Handover Acceptance & Evidence **[NEW C012]**
Establish whether receiving responsibility was actually accepted and whether outstanding obligations remain accounted for.

*Enterprise contributions: E06.07, E03.04, E08.07; findings: R07, R16.*

- **`B05.03.04.F01` — ReceivingPartyAgreed:** Handover identifies who accepted clinical responsibility. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.03.04.F02` — UnacceptedHandoverVisible:** No receipt notification is treated as acceptance without required evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.03.04.F03` — OutstandingWorkOwned:** Each unresolved obligation has accountable next owner. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.03.04.F04` — TransferredContextSufficient:** Required care context is available to receiver. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.03.04.F05` — AcceptanceTimeTraceable:** Timing of acceptance and any delay is independently evidenced. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### B05.04 — Longitudinal Care Coordination
Maintain coherent care intentions and activities over time.

#### B05.04.01 — Longitudinal Care Intent Continuity
Care plans and decisions can remain coherent over multiple episodes and settings.

- **`B05.04.01.F01` — CoherentLongitudinalCare:** proportion of reviewed longitudinal cases without unexplained contradictory care intent. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.04.01.F02` — Persistent Care Goal Context:** proportion of assessed longitudinal care intent continuity cases with observable persistent care goal context against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.04.03 — Continuity Exception Recognition
Interruptions or gaps in ongoing care can be recognised and addressed.

- **`B05.04.03.F01` — DetectedContinuityGap:** proportion of seeded care gaps detected and assigned for action. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.04.03.F02` — Interrupted Care Recognition:** proportion of sampled continuity exception recognition cases with accessible, accurate evidence of interrupted care recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.04.03.F03` — Unassigned Responsibility Detection:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed continuity exception recognition scenarios demonstrating unassigned responsibility detection against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.04.03.F04` — Continuity Risk Escalation:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing continuity exception recognition scenarios with correct continuity risk escalation, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B05.04.03.F05` — Restoration of Care Arrangements:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in continuity exception recognition for which restoration of care arrangements is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.04.04 — Longitudinal Care Context Continuity **[NEW C014]**
Preserve meaningful care history across organisation, encounter and clinical service transitions so participants can act on appropriate context.

- **`B05.04.04.F01` — LongitudinalEncounterContinuity:** Clinical teams can distinguish longitudinal events belonging to separate care episodes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.04.04.F02` — TransitionContextAvailability:** Relevant prior-care context can be accessed during authorised handover. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.04.04.F03` — StaleCareContextWarning:** Materially outdated care facts are distinguishable from current facts. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.04.04.F04` — CrossProviderEvidenceTraceability:** Record contributors and source organisations remain identifiable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.04.04.F05` — IncompleteJourneyQualification:** Gaps in longitudinal history are disclosed where clinically significant and permitted. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

- **`B05.05.03.F03` — Appropriate Escalation Recipient:** proportion of independently checked unattended follow-up escalation instances satisfying the specified appropriate escalation recipient quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

- **`B05.05.03.F05` — Escalation Outcome Visibility:** proportion of sampled unattended follow-up escalation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of escalation outcome visibility. *PROPOSED*.

#### B05.05.04 — Deferred Clinical Follow-Up Accountability **[NEW C014]**
Maintain continuity of care obligations that can only be fulfilled after prolonged human or external activity.

- **`B05.05.04.F01` — LongFollowUpIdentifiable:** A follow-up obligation remains identifiable over multi-day or multi-week periods. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.05.04.F02` — NoPrematureFulfilment:** Digital acknowledgement alone does not close a clinically required follow-up. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.05.04.F03` — EscalationAccountability:** Overdue clinical follow-up has an accountable escalation pathway. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.05.04.F04` — LateOutcomeIntegration:** Relevant completion reports can be reconciled after interrupted or offline periods. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B05.05.04.F05` — CrossCareTeamHandover:** Transferred follow-up preserves responsibility and its prior context. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-CT2-ACT, DRP-PR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.

## B06 — Therapies, Medicines & Functional Support
Medicines, rehabilitation and therapeutic or psychosocial support can be appropriately provided and reviewed.

### B06.01 — Medicines Therapy Management
Prescribe, reconcile, administer, review and optimise medicines as appropriate.

### B07.01 — Health Service Portfolio Management

- **`B08.01.04.F03` — PriorityResponsibilityClear:** Higher-priority tasks and overdue work are distinguishable from routine obligations. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### B08.03.04 — Effective Clinical Privilege Evidence **[NEW C012]**
Ensure professional privileges for a particular care context are valid and reviewable independently of technical authentication.

*Enterprise contributions: E05.02, E05.06, E07.05; findings: R22.*

- **`B08.03.04.F01` — PrivilegeForServiceValidated:** Actual service and setting privileges are checked. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B08.03.04.F02` — TechnicalLoginNotEnough:** Successful login alone does not imply clinical privilege. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B08.03.04.F03` — PrivilegeExpiryHonoured:** Expired/suspended authority cannot be used as current evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B08.03.04.F04` — ExceptionalAuthorisationLogged:** Emergency or exceptional authority is separately accountable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B08.03.04.F05` — PrivilegeSourceTraceable:** The organisation and acceptance basis of privileges can be reviewed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### B08.03.05 — Clinical Privilege Currency Stewardship **[NEW C014]**
Ensure provider directory consumers can distinguish current clinical privileges and practice restrictions from historical or merely claimed eligibility.

- **`B08.03.05.F01` — PrivilegeEffectivePeriod:** Clinical privileges are associated with the correct effective period and service setting. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B08.03.05.F02` — RestrictionPromptlyVisible:** A changed restriction is reflected to authorised downstream consumers with accountable currency. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B08.03.05.F03` — UnverifiedPrivilegeQualified:** Unverified privilege assertions cannot be used as established clinical authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B08.03.05.F04` — RolePrivilegeSeparation:** A directory role is not assumed to confer every corresponding clinical privilege. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B08.03.05.F05` — PrivilegeReviewTraceability:** Privilege review and change decisions are attributable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-PRAC-VERIFY, C-PRAC-AFF. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.
