## B10 — Health Information Stewardship
The healthcare enterprise can meet its responsibilities for health records, knowledge, information use and accountability.

### B10.01 — Health Record Stewardship
Establish and maintain clinically and legally meaningful records.

#### B10.01.01 — Clinically Meaningful Record Completion
The organisation can ensure required records of care are coherent and complete.

- **`B10.01.01.F01` — Required Record Content Completed:** proportion of sampled care records with applicable content completed or qualified. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.01.F02` — Relevant Record Content:** proportion of sampled clinically meaningful record completion cases with accessible, accurate evidence of relevant record content. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.01.F03` — Timely Record Finalisation:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely record finalisation outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.01.F04` — Record Authorship Clarity:** proportion of sampled clinically meaningful record completion cases with accessible, accurate evidence of record authorship clarity. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.01.F05` — Correction of Record Omissions:** proportion of independently checked clinically meaningful record completion instances satisfying the specified correction of record omissions quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.01.02 — Record Identity and Association
Records can be attributed to the correct person, service and encounter or care context.

- **`B10.01.02.F01` — CorrectRecordAssociation:** proportion of audited records associated with the intended subject and context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.02.F02` — Correct Person Association:** proportion of independently checked record identity and association instances satisfying the specified correct person association quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.02.F03` — Duplicate Record Recognition:** proportion of independently checked record identity and association instances satisfying the specified duplicate record recognition quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.02.F04` — Cross-Record Link Trace:** proportion of sampled record identity and association cases with accessible, accurate evidence of cross-record link trace. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.02.F05` — Misassociation Correction:** proportion of independently checked record identity and association instances satisfying the specified misassociation correction quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.01.03 — Record Reconciliation and Continuity
Multiple contributions can form a useful longitudinal record without hiding differences.

- **`B10.01.03.F01` — CoherentHealthRecord:** proportion of reviewed longitudinal records with material contributions correctly contextualised. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.03.F02` — Cross-Source Record Comparison:** proportion of sampled record reconciliation and continuity cases with accessible, accurate evidence of cross-source record comparison. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.03.F03` — Resolved Record Disagreement:** proportion of sampled record reconciliation and continuity cases with accessible, accurate evidence of resolved record disagreement. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.03.F04` — Historical Record Retention:** proportion of sampled record reconciliation and continuity cases with accessible, accurate evidence of historical record retention. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.01.03.F05` — Continuity across Encounters:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in record reconciliation and continuity for which continuity across encounters is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.01.04 — Person Identity & Record Association Stewardship **[NEW C014]**
Account for person identities and the evidence linking records to individuals across health information sources.

- **`B10.01.04.F01` — IdentitySourceAccountability:** Person identifiers and the source responsible for each assertion remain distinguishable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.01.04.F02` — UncertainSubjectAssociation:** An uncertain person–record match is qualified before clinical use. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.01.04.F03` — IdentityChangeAwareness:** Record stewards can identify relevant person identity changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.01.04.F04` — WrongPersonRiskHandling:** Potential wrong-person clinical record associations have an accountable escalation route. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.01.04.F05` — CrossSourceIdentityHistory:** Past and current record-to-person associations can be reconstructed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-PERSON-CORR, C-PERSON-RESOLVE. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### B10.01.05 — Longitudinal Record Assembly Stewardship **[NEW C014]**
Ensure cross-episode health record contributions are appropriately associated, qualified and discoverable without turning summaries into invented clinical truth.

- **`B10.01.05.F01` — CrossEpisodeContentAssociation:** A longitudinal record includes appropriately related episodes with attributable sources. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.01.05.F02` — KnownRecordGapsReported:** Missing/unavailable source material is not misrepresented as evidence of absence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.01.05.F03` — AggregateNotAuthoritativeOverwrite:** Summaries retain the identity and authority of contributing source records. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.01.05.F04` — ClinicalRecordVersionContext:** Historic and amended clinical contributions remain interpretable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.01.05.F05` — ConflictingRecordsNotHidden:** Materially contradictory source statements are qualified rather than silently erased. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-LHR-QUERY, C-FED-QUERY. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### B10.02 — Information Completeness & Provenance Accountability
Know whether the record has the evidence, source and history necessary for use.

#### B10.02.01 — Information Sufficiency Appraisal

- **`B10.02.01.F01` — QualifiedInformationSufficiency:** proportion of sampled decisions with known incompleteness made explicit. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.01.F02` — Required Data Coverage:** proportion of applicable information sufficiency appraisal requests or situations with verified required data coverage. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.01.F03` — Missing Information Visibility:** proportion of sampled information sufficiency appraisal cases with accessible, accurate evidence of missing information visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.01.F04` — Qualified Information Use:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed information sufficiency appraisal scenarios demonstrating qualified information use against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.01.F05` — Reassessment after Data Arrival:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed information sufficiency appraisal scenarios demonstrating reassessment after data arrival against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.02.02 — Clinical Information Source Accountability
The enterprise can establish where information came from and who contributed it.

- **`B10.02.02.F01` — AttributableBusinessInformation:** proportion of sampled information with retrievable required origin context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.02.F02` — Identifiable Information Origin:** proportion of independently checked clinical information source accountability instances satisfying the specified identifiable information origin quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.02.F03` — Source Trust Qualification:** proportion of independently checked clinical information source accountability instances satisfying the specified source trust qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.02.F04` — Transformation History Access:** proportion of sampled clinical information source accountability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of transformation history access. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.02.F05` — Source Challenge Procedure:** proportion of sampled clinical information source accountability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of source challenge procedure. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.02.03 — Information History Review
Relevant updates or interpretations can be reviewed without silently replacing prior context.

- **`B10.02.03.F01` — ReviewableInformationHistory:** proportion of selected changed records with sufficient traceable history. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.03.F02` — Prior Version Access:** proportion of independently checked information history review instances satisfying the specified prior version access quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.03.F03` — Consequential Change Recognition:** proportion of sampled information history review cases with accessible, accurate evidence of consequential change recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.03.F04` — Change Contributor Identification:** proportion of independently checked information history review instances satisfying the specified change contributor identification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.02.03.F05` — Historic Context Explanation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed information history review scenarios demonstrating historic context explanation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.02.04 — Provenance Evidence Stewardship **[NEW C012]**
Ensure that evidence about information origin, transformations and accountability remains identifiable, interpretable and protected.

*Enterprise contributions: E08.07, E08.08, E01.03; findings: R03, R04.*

- **`B10.02.04.F01` — ProvenanceChronologyFaithful:** Evidence chronology matches represented event order. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.02.04.F02` — SourceIdentityRetained:** Every governed derivative has attributable source identity where required. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.02.04.F03` — IncompleteProvenanceQualified:** Missing evidence does not silently imply full lineage. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.02.04.F04` — ProvenanceCorrectionAdditive:** Qualifications are recorded without altering immutable history. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.02.04.F05` — ReviewAccessControlled:** Only authorised reviewers can obtain protected evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### B10.02.05 — Clinical Source & Temporal Qualification Stewardship **[NEW C014]**
Help health information users assess the source, time and interpretation limitations of assembled clinical evidence.

- **`B10.02.05.F01` — OccurrenceVsRecordingClarified:** Clinically relevant chronology reflects event occurrence separately from documentation time. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.02.05.F02` — SourceConfidenceVisible:** Information provenance and known limitations can be reviewed by authorised clinicians. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.02.05.F03` — CorrectionLineageKnown:** An amended clinical statement remains linked to the prior statement. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.02.05.F04` — ContradictionReviewPath:** Inconsistent source information has a recognisable clinical review route. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.02.05.F05` — QualifiedSummaryUse:** Summaries identify when source coverage or identity association remains uncertain. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-LHR-QUERY, DRP-IU. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### B10.03 — Appropriate Information Access & Sharing
Make information available to authorised participants for valid business purposes.

#### B10.03.01 — Relevant Care Information Sharing
Necessary health information can be made available to permitted care recipients.

- **`B10.03.01.F01` — Information Available to Authorised Receiving Care:** proportion of required care transitions with authorised necessary information available. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.01.F02` — DisclosedInformationScope:** proportion of sampled exchanges that include necessary information without unnecessary disclosure. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.01.F03` — Recipient Relevance:** proportion of applicable relevant care information sharing interactions where recipient relevance reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.01.F04` — Clinical Context Preservation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in relevant care information sharing for which clinical context preservation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.01.F05` — Sharing Timeliness:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed relevant care information sharing scenarios demonstrating sharing timeliness against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.01.F06` — Sharing Receipt Awareness:** proportion of applicable relevant care information sharing interactions where sharing receipt awareness reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.03.02 — Purpose-Appropriate Information Disclosure
Information can be shared for legitimate business purposes at an appropriate scope.

- **`B10.03.02.F01` — CorrectBusinessDisclosure:** proportion of sampled disclosures matching applicable purpose and scope. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.02.F02` — Legitimate Disclosure Purpose:** proportion of sampled purpose-appropriate information disclosure cases with accessible, accurate evidence of legitimate disclosure purpose. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.02.F03` — Minimum Necessary Information:** proportion of independently checked purpose-appropriate information disclosure instances satisfying the specified minimum necessary information quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.02.F04` — Recipient Authority Check:** proportion of positive and negative purpose-appropriate information disclosure test cases meeting the applicable recipient authority check criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.02.F05` — Disclosure Accountability:** proportion of sampled purpose-appropriate information disclosure cases with accessible, accurate evidence of disclosure accountability. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.03.03 — Information Sharing Exception Handling
Barriers or disputes concerning information sharing can be recognised and resolved or escalated.

- **`B10.03.03.F01` — AccountableSharingException:** proportion of consequential blocked sharing needs with documented disposition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.03.F02` — Blocked Disclosure Explanation:** proportion of sampled information sharing exception handling cases with accessible, accurate evidence of blocked disclosure explanation. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.03.F03` — Corrective Referral Route:** proportion of independently checked information sharing exception handling instances satisfying the specified corrective referral route quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.03.F04` — Reviewable Exception Evidence:** proportion of sampled information sharing exception handling cases with accessible, accurate evidence of reviewable exception evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.03.03.F05` — Resolved Sharing Outcome:** proportion of assessed information sharing exception handling cases with observable resolved sharing outcome against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.03.04 — Disclosure Authority Revalidation **[NEW C012]**
Ensure sharing decisions reflect current consent, policy, compartment and representative authority.

*Enterprise contributions: E05.06, E05.03, E02.01; findings: R01, R02, R22.*

- **`B10.03.04.F01` — RevokedPermissionHonoured:** Withdrawn sharing authority prevents subsequent disclosure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.04.F02` — ChangedPolicyReassessed:** Previously granted access is reconsidered when policy changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.04.F03` — RecipientPurposeVerified:** Sharing is limited to intended lawful/authorised purpose. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.04.F04` — NoMetadataDisclosure:** Restricted records cannot be discovered through unprivileged metadata. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.04.F05` — DenialAccountable:** A defensible record of an authorisation refusal is available where required. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### B10.03.05 — External Health Information Receipt Stewardship **[NEW C014]**
Account for receipt, acceptance and subsequent availability of clinical information exchanged across organisations.

- **`B10.03.05.F01` — ReceivedNotNecessarilyAccepted:** Business recipients can distinguish a received submission from an accepted contribution. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.05.F02` — AcceptedInformationAccountable:** Accepted inbound clinical information has an identified custody obligation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.05.F03` — RejectedContributionExplained:** Material rejections carry an actionable qualification for the submitting party. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.05.F04` — ReceivedSourceAttribution:** Source organisation, subject and exchange time are recorded where applicable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.05.F05` — DelayedDispositionVisible:** Information pending validation or import has a known and reviewable status. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-HIE-GATE, C-DOC-INGRESS. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### B10.03.06 — Cross-Organisation Exchange Exception Resolution **[NEW C014]**
Coordinate the business handling of ambiguous, incomplete or rejected health information exchanges without silently losing care obligations.

- **`B10.03.06.F01` — ExchangeExceptionOwned:** A significant exchange exception has an accountable resolution responsibility. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.06.F02` — BusinessTechnicalStatusSeparated:** A technical receipt failure is distinguishable from clinical information rejection. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.06.F03` — ExceptionCareImpactVisible:** Clinical care impacts of an exchange problem can be escalated where appropriate. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.06.F04` — PartnerNotificationAppropriate:** The sending partner receives an applicable, actionable disposition. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.03.06.F05` — CorrectedExchangeTraceable:** A corrected submission can be related to the rejected or uncertain predecessor. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-HIE-GATE, C-DOC-INGRESS. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### B10.04 — Consent, Preference & Representation Stewardship
Maintain and respect consent, decision authority and participation preferences.

#### B10.04.01 — Consent Intent Stewardship
Applicable permissions, refusals and qualifications can be recorded and respected in business decisions.

- **`B10.04.01.F01` — CurrentConsentIntent:** proportion of qualifying decisions applying the relevant recorded consent context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.01.F02` — Consent Communication Clarity:** proportion of positive and negative consent intent stewardship test cases meeting the applicable consent communication clarity criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.01.F03` — Consent Scope Clarity:** proportion of positive and negative consent intent stewardship test cases meeting the applicable consent scope clarity criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.01.F04` — Consent Withdrawal Recognition:** proportion of positive and negative consent intent stewardship test cases meeting the applicable consent withdrawal recognition criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.01.F05` — Consent Decision History:** proportion of positive and negative consent intent stewardship test cases meeting the applicable consent decision history criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.04.02 — Participation Preference Stewardship
Communication and participation preferences can remain available to relevant service participants.

- **`B10.04.02.F01` — RespectedParticipationPreferences:** proportion of sampled applicable interactions conforming to current preferences. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.02.F02` — Current Participation Preference:** proportion of representative participation preference stewardship scenarios in which current participation preference is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.02.F03` — Context-Sensitive Preference Use:** proportion of representative participation preference stewardship scenarios in which context-sensitive preference use is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.02.F04` — Changed Preference Communication:** proportion of representative participation preference stewardship scenarios in which changed preference communication is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.02.F05` — Preference Conflict Recognition:** proportion of representative participation preference stewardship scenarios in which preference conflict recognition is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.04.03 — Decision Authority Stewardship
The enterprise can identify appropriate decision authority and its limits where required.

- **`B10.04.03.F01` — CorrectDecisionAuthorityUse:** proportion of tested decisions using a valid applicable authority basis. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.03.F02` — Authoritative Decision Scope:** proportion of positive and negative decision authority stewardship test cases meeting the applicable authoritative decision scope criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.03.F03` — Delegation Currency:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in decision authority stewardship for which delegation currency is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.03.F04` — Unauthorised Decision Detection:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing decision authority stewardship scenarios with correct unauthorised decision detection, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.04.03.F05` — Authority Change Traceability:** proportion of positive and negative decision authority stewardship test cases meeting the applicable authority change traceability criteria. *PROPOSED*.

### B10.05 — Health Knowledge Stewardship
Curate trusted clinical guidance and domain knowledge for professional use.

#### B10.05.01 — Clinical Guidance Stewardship
Trusted clinical guidance can be curated and made available to relevant professional contexts.

- **`B10.05.01.F02` — Guidance Source Authority:** proportion of positive and negative clinical guidance stewardship test cases meeting the applicable guidance source authority criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.01.F03` — Current Guidance Availability:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in clinical guidance stewardship for which current guidance availability is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.01.F04` — Retired Guidance Visibility:** proportion of sampled clinical guidance stewardship cases with accessible, accurate evidence of retired guidance visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.01.F05` — Guidance Revision Record:** proportion of sampled clinical guidance stewardship cases with accessible, accurate evidence of guidance revision record. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.05.02 — Knowledge Change Review
Changes in clinical knowledge can be assessed for their impact on practice.

- **`B10.05.02.F01` — ReviewedKnowledgeChange:** proportion of material changes with an accountable business review. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.02.F02` — Evidence-Grounded Knowledge Revision:** proportion of sampled knowledge change review cases with accessible, accurate evidence of evidence-grounded knowledge revision. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.02.F03` — Appropriate Knowledge Review:** proportion of independently checked knowledge change review instances satisfying the specified appropriate knowledge review quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.02.F04` — Change Impact Awareness:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in knowledge change review for which change impact awareness is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.02.F05` — Knowledge Release Trace:** proportion of sampled knowledge change review cases with accessible, accurate evidence of knowledge release trace. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.05.03 — Knowledge Use Support
Clinical teams can obtain useful knowledge for decisions without obscuring uncertainty.

- **`B10.05.03.F01` — UsableClinicalKnowledge:** proportion of evaluated decisions with available appropriate guidance and qualifications. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.03.F02` — Clinically Relevant Knowledge Access:** proportion of applicable knowledge use support requests or situations with verified clinically relevant knowledge access. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.03.F03` — Knowledge Applicability Clarity:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed knowledge use support scenarios demonstrating knowledge applicability clarity against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.03.F04` — Knowledge Limitations Disclosure:** proportion of sampled knowledge use support cases with accessible, accurate evidence of knowledge limitations disclosure. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.05.03.F05` — Feedback on Knowledge Use:** proportion of applicable knowledge use support interactions where feedback on knowledge use reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

### B10.06 — Retention, Disclosure & Record Accountability
Meet obligations for retention, disclosure, correction and traceability.

#### B10.06.01 — Business Record Retention Stewardship
The enterprise can meet applicable duties to retain and protect health records.

- **`B10.06.01.F01` — CompliantRecordRetention:** proportion of sampled records managed under relevant retention obligations. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.01.F02` — Applicable Retention Obligation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed business record retention stewardship scenarios demonstrating applicable retention obligation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.01.F03` — Legal Hold Recognition:** proportion of sampled business record retention stewardship cases with accessible, accurate evidence of legal hold recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.01.F04` — Scheduled Record Disposition:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the scheduled record disposition outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.01.F05` — Retention Evidence Availability:** proportion of sampled business record retention stewardship cases with accessible, accurate evidence of retention evidence availability. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.06.02 — Request for Record Disclosure Management
Requests to access or disclose records can be assessed and fulfilled where authorised.

- **`B10.06.02.F01` — AccountableRecordDisclosure:** proportion of sampled valid requests with correct outcome and traceable decision. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.02.F02` — Requester Entitlement Check:** proportion of positive and negative request for record disclosure management test cases meeting the applicable requester entitlement check criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.02.F03` — Required Record Identification:** proportion of independently checked request for record disclosure management instances satisfying the specified required record identification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.02.F04` — Timely Disclosure Disposition:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely disclosure disposition outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.02.F05` — Redaction and Scope Explanation:** proportion of positive and negative request for record disclosure management test cases meeting the applicable redaction and scope explanation criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.06.03 — Record Disclosure Accounting
Disclosures and significant record actions can be accounted for under business obligations.

- **`B10.06.03.F01` — TraceableDisclosureHistory:** proportion of sampled disclosable activities with required evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.03.F02` — Disclosure Recipient Trace:** proportion of sampled record disclosure accounting cases with accessible, accurate evidence of disclosure recipient trace. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.03.F03` — Disclosure Content Identification:** proportion of independently checked record disclosure accounting instances satisfying the specified disclosure content identification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.03.F04` — Authorised Disclosure Evidence:** proportion of positive and negative record disclosure accounting test cases meeting the applicable authorised disclosure evidence criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.06.03.F05` — Disclosure History Review:** proportion of sampled record disclosure accounting cases with accessible, accurate evidence of disclosure history review. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.06.04 — Comprehensive Evidence Disclosure **[NEW C012]**
Release requested evidentiary information only when complete, valid and authorisable as an integrated disclosure.

*Enterprise contributions: E08.08, E05.05, E09.03; findings: R04, R23.*

- **`B10.06.04.F01` — RequestedSetComplete:** The approved requested evidence set is fully assembled before release. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.06.04.F02` — NoPartialEvidenceRelease:** Any mandatory authorisation denial prevents release of the whole set. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.06.04.F03` — RawVsInterpretedDistinct:** A record export differs clearly from dereferenced explanatory evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.06.04.F04` — DisclosureBasisRetained:** Purpose, authority and release decision remain attributable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.06.04.F05` — IncompleteRequestQualified:** Missing essential evidence yields refusal/exception rather than misleading completeness. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### B10.07 — Information Correction & Reconciliation
Resolve inaccurate, inconsistent or disputed health information without obscuring history.

#### B10.07.01 — Record Error Correction
Known errors can be corrected appropriately while retaining necessary history.

- **`B10.07.01.F01` — AccountableRecordCorrection:** proportion of sampled corrections with appropriate authorisation and traceability. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.01.F02` — Correction Request Capture:** proportion of independently checked record error correction instances satisfying the specified correction request capture quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.01.F03` — Evidence-Supported Correction:** proportion of independently checked record error correction instances satisfying the specified evidence-supported correction quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.01.F04` — Corrected Record Visibility:** proportion of independently checked record error correction instances satisfying the specified corrected record visibility quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.01.F05` — Prior Statement Preservation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in record error correction for which prior statement preservation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.07.02 — Disputed Information Resolution
Conflicting or disputed statements can be reviewed without silently asserting certainty.

- **`B10.07.02.F01` — DispositionedInformationDispute:** proportion of sampled disputes with a recorded outcome or qualification. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.02.F02` — Dispute Claim Acknowledgement:** proportion of applicable disputed information resolution interactions where dispute claim acknowledgement reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.02.F03` — Independent Dispute Review:** proportion of representative disputed information resolution scenarios in which independent dispute review is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.02.F04` — Qualified Disputed Content:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed disputed information resolution scenarios demonstrating qualified disputed content against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.02.F05` — Dispute Resolution Communication:** proportion of applicable disputed information resolution interactions where dispute resolution communication reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.07.03 — Cross-Source Information Reconciliation
Material differences between information sources can be identified and appropriately addressed.

- **`B10.07.03.F01` — QualifiedCrossSourceConsistency:** proportion of tested discrepancies resolved or visibly qualified. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.03.F02` — Conflicting Source Detection:** proportion of sampled cross-source information reconciliation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of conflicting source detection. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.03.F03` — Preferred Source Rationale:** proportion of sampled cross-source information reconciliation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of preferred source rationale. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.03.F04` — Reconciliation Change Trace:** proportion of independently checked cross-source information reconciliation instances satisfying the specified reconciliation change trace quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B10.07.03.F05` — Unresolved Disagreement Disclosure:** proportion of sampled cross-source information reconciliation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of unresolved disagreement disclosure. *PROPOSED*.

#### B10.07.04 — Correction Commitment Accountability **[NEW C012]**
Distinguish working correction, review, execution and authoritative acceptance while preserving immutable prior versions.

*Enterprise contributions: E01.01, E06.07, E07.02; findings: R06, R09.*

- **`B10.07.04.F01` — PreparedCorrectionNotFinal:** Uncommitted drafts are not treated as authoritative. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.04.F02` — ValidationSeparate:** Quality checks produce findings before acceptance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.04.F03` — CommittedVersionIdentified:** Accepted correction has a resolvable new governed version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.04.F04` — RejectedCorrectionSafe:** Rejected action does not alter previous authoritative content. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.04.F05` — ConflictResolutionTraceable:** Competing corrections retain decision evidence and prior history. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### B10.07.05 — Uncertain Person Identity Association Resolution **[NEW C014]**
Provide an accountable business process for assessing ambiguous, conflicting and contested patient identity matches.

- **`B10.07.05.F01` — AmbiguousMatchReferral:** A disputed match reaches an appropriately authorised reviewer. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.05.F02` — ResolutionEvidence:** A resolution references its source evidence, decision maker and applicable time. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.05.F03` — HighRiskFalseMergeGuard:** Known wrong-person adverse cases are not accepted as unqualified linkage. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.05.F04` — ExceptionTimelinessVisible:** Pending identity cases have measurable age and escalation status. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.05.F05` — DisputedIdentityVisibility:** A dispute cannot disappear simply because one consumer selects a preferred source. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-PERSON-CORR, C-PERSON-CORRECT. *Release horizon:* R3+ full EMPI scope; earlier identity safety remains applicable.


#### B10.07.06 — Person Identity Linkage Reversal Accountability **[NEW C014]**
Ensure identity association corrections and reversals preserve prior clinical attribution and downstream impact accountability.

- **`B10.07.06.F01` — ReversalAuthority:** A wrong association is reversed only under the applicable review authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.06.F02` — AffectedClinicalRecordsKnown:** Dependent record associations can be identified for review or qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.06.F03` — HistoricalDecisionPreserved:** The prior identity link and its reason remain auditable after correction. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.06.F04` — WrongPersonExposureAssessed:** The impact of wrong-person disclosure or clinical use can be assessed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B10.07.06.F05` — CorrectedLinkDisclosedSafely:** Authorised consumers receive appropriate information about a corrected association. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-PERSON-CORRECT, DRP-IU. *Release horizon:* R3+ full EMPI scope.


## B11 — Safety, Quality & Accountability
Healthcare quality, safety, conformance and concerns can be examined, assured, improved and accounted for.

### B11.01 — Clinical Safety & Risk Management
Identify, reduce and respond to risks arising from care.

#### B11.01.01 — Clinical Hazard Identification
Risks of harm arising from the care context can be recognised before or during delivery.

- **`B11.01.01.F01` — RecognisedClinicalHazards:** proportion of prescribed clinical hazard cases identified by relevant controls. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.01.F02` — Clinical Hazard Signal Recognition:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical hazard identification scenarios with correct clinical hazard signal recognition, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.01.F03` — Hazard Severity Appraisal:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical hazard identification scenarios with correct hazard severity appraisal, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.01.F04` — Hazard Reporter Feedback:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical hazard identification scenarios with correct hazard reporter feedback, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.01.F05` — Hazard Register Currency:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical hazard identification scenarios with correct hazard register currency, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.

#### B11.01.02 — Clinical Risk Mitigation
Appropriate prevention and mitigation arrangements can be established for material risks.

- **`B11.01.02.F01` — EffectiveRiskMitigation:** proportion of sampled material risks with active and reviewed mitigation. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.02.F02` — Proportionate Risk Control:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical risk mitigation scenarios with correct proportionate risk control, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.02.F03` — Control Responsibility Assignment:** proportion of applicable clinical risk mitigation requests or situations with verified control responsibility assignment. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.02.F04` — Mitigation Progress Monitoring:** proportion of sampled clinical risk mitigation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of mitigation progress monitoring. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.02.F05` — Residual Risk Visibility:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical risk mitigation scenarios with correct residual risk visibility, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.

#### B11.01.03 — Clinical Safety Escalation
Emerging or unresolved safety concerns can reach an accountable decision-maker.

- **`B11.01.03.F01` — TimelySafetyEscalation:** proportion of qualifying high-consequence concerns escalated in the applicable interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.03.F02` — Safety Concern Escalation Route:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical safety escalation scenarios with correct safety concern escalation route, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.03.F03` — Safety Escalation Receipt Confirmation:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical safety escalation scenarios with correct safety escalation receipt confirmation, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.03.F04` — Protective Action Timing:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed clinical safety escalation scenarios demonstrating protective action timing against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.01.03.F05` — Safety Escalation Closure:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical safety escalation scenarios with correct safety escalation closure, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.

### B11.02 — Incident Reporting & Learning
Capture, investigate, respond to and learn from incidents.

#### B11.02.01 — Incident Recognition and Reporting
Significant safety or service events can be reported without unnecessary loss of context.

- **`B11.02.01.F01` — CompleteIncidentReporting:** proportion of qualifying reported events with required core information. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.01.F02` — Incident Classification Accuracy:** proportion of independently checked incident recognition and reporting instances satisfying the specified incident classification accuracy quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.01.F03` — Safe Reporting Accessibility:** proportion of representative incident recognition and reporting scenarios in which safe reporting accessibility is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.01.F04` — Prompt Incident Notification:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the prompt incident notification outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.01.F05` — Incident Report Completeness:** proportion of independently checked incident recognition and reporting instances satisfying the specified incident report completeness quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### B11.02.02 — Clinical Incident Follow-Up
Reported incidents can be investigated and acted upon to an accountable disposition.

- **`B11.02.02.F01` — Timely Closure with Documented Action:** proportion of qualifying incidents with evidenced follow-up and accountable closure. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.02.F02` — Investigation Ownership:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed clinical incident follow-up scenarios demonstrating investigation ownership against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.02.F03` — Harm and Contributor Assessment:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical incident follow-up scenarios with correct harm and contributor assessment, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.02.F04` — Timely Follow-Up Action:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely follow-up action outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.02.F05` — Affected Party Communication:** proportion of applicable clinical incident follow-up interactions where affected party communication reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### B11.02.03 — Incident Learning Application
Lessons from incidents can inform changes to care and services.

- **`B11.02.03.F01` — DemonstratedIncidentLearning:** proportion of sampled significant findings with evaluated improvement action. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.03.F02` — Evidence-Based Improvement Lesson:** proportion of sampled incident learning application cases with accessible, accurate evidence of evidence-based improvement lesson. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.03.F03` — Learning Dissemination:** proportion of applicable incident learning application interactions where learning dissemination reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.03.F04` — Corrective Practice Adoption:** proportion of independently checked incident learning application instances satisfying the specified corrective practice adoption quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.02.03.F05` — Learning Effect Verification:** proportion of assessed incident learning application cases with observable learning effect verification against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.

### B11.03 — Clinical Governance & Practice Assurance

#### B11.03.03 — Clinical Accountability Management
Material practice issues can be assigned to appropriate oversight and disposition.

- **`B11.03.03.F01` — AccountableClinicalGovernance:** proportion of material practice exceptions with recorded responsible review. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B11.03.03.F02` — Clear Clinical Responsibility:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed clinical accountability management scenarios demonstrating clear clinical responsibility against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### B11.03.04 — Auditable Clinical Governance Evidence **[NEW C012]**
Obtain adequate, immutable and interpretable evidence to review clinical governance and material practice events.

*Enterprise contributions: E08.07, E08.08, E09.04; findings: R03, R04.*

- **`B11.03.04.F01` — MandatoryActionsEvidenced:** Relevant clinical activity generates required audit evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B11.03.04.F02` — GovernanceEvidenceImmutable:** Previously accepted audit/provenance records cannot be rewritten. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B11.03.04.F03` — EvidenceCorrelatable:** Clinician, subject, event, decision and information version can be related. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B11.03.04.F04` — ReviewPackageAuthorised:** Evidence assemblies are disclosed only after whole-set authorisation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B11.03.04.F05` — AdverseFindingVisible:** Qualified or contrary evidence is not suppressed from authorised review. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### B11.05.04 — Evidence-Based Accreditation Verification **[NEW C012]**
Demonstrate conformance through repeatable observations without asserting success solely from a complete-looking model.

*Enterprise contributions: E08.04, E08.07, E09.04; findings: R18.*

- **`B11.05.04.F01` — RequirementToEvidenceTrace:** A compliance claim links to explicit tested requirement and version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B11.05.04.F02` — UnverifiedNotCompliant:** Missing evidence cannot be recorded as passing conformance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B11.05.04.F03` — ExceptionDisclosed:** Known qualification and deferral remain visible in review. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B11.05.04.F04` — RepeatableTestAvailable:** Assurance checks can be rerun with controlled inputs. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B11.05.04.F05` — ReviewerAuthorityEstablished:** The approval and evidence-review roles are identifiable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

## B12 — Enterprise Direction & Readiness
The healthcare enterprise can set direction, steward resources, manage risk and demonstrate operational readiness.

### B12.01 — Strategic Direction & Service Investment

#### B12.04.04 — Accepted Service Obligation Continuity **[NEW C012]**

*Enterprise contributions: E06.04, E06.07, E08.06; findings: R21, R24.*

- **`B12.04.04.F01` — AcceptedWorkNotLost:** Accepted work remains recoverable after bounded failure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

## B13 — Population Health & Learning
Population health can be assessed and improved, and trustworthy research and learning can influence practice.

### B13.01 — Population Health Surveillance
Understand the health patterns, threats and needs of populations.

#### B13.01.01 — Population Health Pattern Assessment
Health patterns and material changes can be understood across defined populations.

- **`B13.01.01.F01` — QualifiedPopulationIndicators:** proportion of sampled indicators with valid scope, source and uncertainty context. *PROPOSED*.

#### B13.01.02 — Emerging Health Threat Detection

- **`B13.01.02.F04` — Threat Escalation Timing:** proportion of applicable emerging health threat detection interactions where threat escalation timing reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B13.01.02.F05` — Cross-Source Threat Confirmation:** proportion of sampled emerging health threat detection cases with accessible, accurate evidence of cross-source threat confirmation. *PROPOSED*.

#### B13.01.03 — Population Health Needs Analysis
Unmet health needs and inequities can be examined across groups and locations.

### B14.01 — Personal Goal & Preference Support
Help a participant articulate, review and change personally meaningful goals and choices.

#### B14.01.01 — Participant Goal Articulation

### B14.04 — Ongoing Support Coordination
Maintain coherence of supports across providers, settings and changes in circumstances.

#### B14.04.01 — Multi-Provider Support Continuity
Multiple support providers can coordinate without losing the person's chosen objectives.

- **`B14.04.01.F01` — CoherentSupportContinuity:** proportion of sampled shared-support cases with compatible goals and responsibilities. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.01.F02` — Shared Support Objective:** proportion of applicable multi-provider support continuity interactions where shared support objective reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.01.F03` — Provider Handover Completeness:** proportion of independently checked multi-provider support continuity instances satisfying the specified provider handover completeness quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.01.F04` — Cross-Provider Status Visibility:** proportion of sampled multi-provider support continuity cases with accessible, accurate evidence of cross-provider status visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.01.F05` — Unfilled Support Obligation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed multi-provider support continuity scenarios demonstrating unfilled support obligation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### B14.04.02 — Support Obligation Follow-Up
Agreed support commitments can be monitored until fulfilled or dispositioned.

- **`B14.04.02.F01` — VisibleSupportCommitments:** proportion of material support commitments with current accountable status. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.02.F02` — Support Action Tracking:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed support obligation follow-up scenarios demonstrating support action tracking against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.02.F03` — Obligation Owner Clarity:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed support obligation follow-up scenarios demonstrating obligation owner clarity against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.02.F04` — Overdue Support Escalation:** proportion of applicable support obligation follow-up interactions where overdue support escalation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.02.F05` — Completed Support Confirmation:** proportion of independently checked support obligation follow-up instances satisfying the specified completed support confirmation quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### B14.04.03 — Support Transition Coordination
Support can be maintained or deliberately changed across life-stage, service or location transitions.

- **`B14.04.03.F01` — SupportedLifeTransition:** proportion of sampled transitions with confirmed continuation or adjusted arrangements. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.03.F02` — Receiving Support Acceptance:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed support transition coordination scenarios demonstrating receiving support acceptance against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.03.F03` — Changing Support Plan Communication:** proportion of applicable support transition coordination interactions where changing support plan communication reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.03.F04` — Transition Continuity Check:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in support transition coordination for which transition continuity check is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B14.04.03.F05` — Transition Exception Resolution:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in support transition coordination for which transition exception resolution is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### B14.04.04 — Participant-Centred Task Coordination **[NEW C014]**
Support tasks and follow-up involving participants or carers without treating participants as patients or automatically granting proxy authority.

- **`B14.04.04.F01` — ParticipantRoleDistinct:** Participant identity and representation authority are explicit. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B14.04.04.F02` — CarerAuthorityChecked:** A carer cannot claim protected tasks solely because they are a contact. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B14.04.04.F03` — LongDurationSupportWork:** Support tasks remain accountable when fulfilled outside the digital system. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B14.04.04.F04` — SupportedParticipationEnabled:** Relevant participants can report progress through authorised pathways. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B14.04.04.F05` — TaskContentDisclosureAppropriate:** Task descriptions and attachments obey applicable compartment and disclosure limits. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: DRP-PR, DRP-SEC. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.

#### B14.06.04 — Participant Decision Authority Transparency **[NEW C012]**
Protect a participant’s own decisions while keeping supporters, representatives and automated advice in their proper roles.

*Enterprise contributions: E07.02, E05.06, E09.04; findings: R11, R15, R22.*

- **`B14.06.04.F01` — ParticipantVoicePrimary:** Participant goals and choices are recorded distinctly from supporters. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

- **`B14.06.04.F03` — AIAdviceNotDecision:** Automated recommendations do not become authoritative participant decisions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B14.06.04.F04` — DisagreementResolutionAvailable:** Conflict between participant and representative has accountable escalation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`B14.06.04.F05` — DecisionEvidenceAccessible:** Participant or authorised reviewer can examine decision basis. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

## B15 — Health System Funding & Commissioning
Services and supports can be commissioned, purchased, financed and evaluated against population need.

### B15.01 — Health Needs-Based Commissioning
Identify the services/outcomes a population or covered group requires and arrange service provision.

#### B15.01.01 — Population Service Gap Commissioning
Commissioners can arrange services in response to established unmet population needs.

- **`B15.01.01.F01` — Commissioned Capacity Addresses an Identified Gap:** proportion of sampled commissioning decisions linked to a documented service need. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B15.01.01.F02` — Unmet Population Need Evidence:** proportion of sampled population service gap commissioning cases with accessible, accurate evidence of unmet population need evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B15.01.01.F03` — Geographic Service Gap Visibility:** proportion of sampled population service gap commissioning cases with accessible, accurate evidence of geographic service gap visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B15.01.01.F04` — Commissioning Priority Trace:** proportion of sampled population service gap commissioning cases with accessible, accurate evidence of commissioning priority trace. *PROPOSED*.

#### B15.01.02 — Commissioning Outcome Specification
The intended public or service value of commissioning can be expressed and assessed.

- **`B15.01.02.F02` — Commissioned Outcome Clarity:** proportion of assessed commissioning outcome specification cases with observable commissioned outcome clarity against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B15.01.02.F03` — Measurable Service Expectations:** proportion of applicable commissioning outcome specification requests or situations with verified measurable service expectations. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B15.01.02.F04` — Provider Accountability Terms:** proportion of sampled commissioning outcome specification cases with accessible, accurate evidence of provider accountability terms. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B15.01.02.F05` — Outcome Review Schedule:** proportion of sampled commissioning outcome specification cases with accessible, accurate evidence of outcome review schedule. *PROPOSED*.

#### B15.01.03 — Provider Market and Capacity Assessment
Potential service supply can be evaluated against required coverage and capability.

- **`B15.01.03.F01` — EvidenceBasedProviderMarketView:** proportion of commissioned service types with current capability and capacity evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B15.01.03.F02` — Viable Provider Market Visibility:** proportion of sampled provider market and capacity assessment cases with accessible, accurate evidence of viable provider market visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`B15.01.03.F03` — Capacity and Capability Assessment:** proportion of applicable provider market and capacity assessment requests or situations with verified capacity and capability assessment. *PROPOSED*.

# Enterprise Capabilities
## E01 — Governed Information Custody
The system can hold, preserve, recover and account for governed information.

### E01.01 — Governed Information Persistence
Preserve accepted information reliably with its governance context.

#### E01.01.01 — Durable Information Acceptance
Accepted governed information remains retrievable following an acknowledgement.

- **`E01.01.01.F01` — AcceptedInformationRecoverable:** proportion of acknowledged units retrieved intact after interruption. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.01.F02` — PersistenceBeforeAcknowledgement:** conformance of acceptance-versus-persistence ordering under tests. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.01.F03` — Confirmed Acceptance Identity:** proportion of independently checked durable information acceptance instances satisfying the specified confirmed acceptance identity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.01.F04` — Recovery of Accepted State:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in durable information acceptance for which recovery of accepted state is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.01.F05` — Incomplete Acceptance Rejection:** proportion of independently checked durable information acceptance instances satisfying the specified incomplete acceptance rejection quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.01.F06` — Acceptance Outcome Visibility:** proportion of sampled durable information acceptance cases with accessible, accurate evidence of acceptance outcome visibility. *PROPOSED*.

#### E01.01.02 — Controlled Information Change
Changes to held information respect the required governing conditions.

- **`E01.01.02.F01` — CustodyChangeIntegrity:** proportion of in-scope changes correctly accepted or rejected. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.02.F02` — DurableChangeOutcome:** proportion of accepted changes recoverable after a prescribed disruption. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.02.F03` — Precondition-Checked Change:** proportion of positive and negative controlled information change test cases meeting the applicable precondition-checked change criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.02.F04` — Authorised Change Attribution:** proportion of positive and negative controlled information change test cases meeting the applicable authorised change attribution criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.02.F05` — Concurrent Change Conflict Detection:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in controlled information change for which concurrent change conflict detection is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.02.F06` — Rejected Change Preservation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in controlled information change for which rejected change preservation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E01.01.03 — Governed Content Custody
Content remains available in an appropriate storage context without constraining storage technology.

- **`E01.01.03.F01` — ContentAvailability:** proportion of required content returned within agreed availability conditions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.03.F02` — Content Integrity Verification:** proportion of independently checked governed content custody instances satisfying the specified content integrity verification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.03.F03` — Controlled Content Retrieval:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed governed content custody scenarios demonstrating controlled content retrieval against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.03.F04` — Content Custody Accounting:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed governed content custody scenarios demonstrating content custody accounting against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E01.01.03.F05` — Content Disposition Protection:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed governed content custody scenarios demonstrating content disposition protection against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E01.01.04 — Authoritative Commitment Outcome **[NEW C012]**
Report a uniquely interpretable acceptance, rejection, absence or indeterminate disposition for an attempted durable change.

*Demand: B10.01, B10.07, B11.05; evidence: MN, PERSIST, IU; findings: R06, R16.*

- **`E01.01.04.F01` — AcceptedStateVerified:** Every reported committed result is read back with the same governed identity and version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.01.04.F02` — RejectedStateUnchanged:** Rejected changes leave the authoritative version and integrity state unchanged. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.01.04.F03` — OutcomeUncertaintyExplicit:** Interrupted commit tests return an indeterminate outcome rather than invented success. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.01.04.F04` — ReplayCorrelationPreserved:** Repeated requests are correlated to the original disposition without false duplicate acceptance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.01.04.F05` — CommitEvidenceResolvable:** The commit response carries enough references to retrieve authoritative disposition evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E01.01.05 — Independent Persistence Custody **[NEW C012]**
Provide governed durable custody without binding authoritative identity or lifecycle to a storage provider or cache.

*Demand: B10.01, B10.06; evidence: PERSIST, DIST, IU; findings: R05, R20.*

- **`E01.01.05.F01` — ProviderIdentityIndependence:** Replacing a provider leaves governed identity and version references resolvable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.01.05.F02` — DurableBeforeAcknowledgement:** No durable-acceptance acknowledgement precedes required persistence evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.01.05.F03` — UnknownProviderOutcomeReported:** Provider timeouts produce a bounded uncertain outcome with reconciliation route. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.01.05.F04` — DomainInstanceIsolation:** A failure in one persistence domain does not silently change another domain state. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.01.05.F05` — CustodyEvidenceTraceable:** Stored unit, version and provider event can be correlated without exposing provider IDs as authoritative. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

- **`E01.05.04.F01` — MediaNotStoredInOrdinaryStore:** Large-payload scenarios use authorised media custody rather than generic relational IU storage. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.05.04.F02` — MediaReferenceSurvivesRelocation:** A media reference remains resolvable when storage location changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

- **`E01.05.05.F04` — MovedIdentityPreserved:** Relocation or sharding does not change governed coordinates. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.05.05.F05` — MissingCoordinateExplained:** Invalid, ambiguous and absent reference outcomes remain distinguishable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E01.06 — Governed Binary & Large-Media Content Management **[NEW C016]**
Manage the identity, metadata, information associations, custody arrangements, resolution, acquisition, retrieval, assembly and lifecycle of formally managed binary or large-media content. This is an **Enterprise Capability**, not a synonym for Sylloge or a storage product. Existing reference and distribution capabilities remain intact.

**Provenance:** Existing Continuum `Harmonia.Sylloge` (canonical component `feea742463b84021b66853d287d8916f`), Domain05 U17 / F31 / D15, and NFR-087; Candidate 015 `E01.05.01`–`E01.05.05` and `E02.04.03`. **Standing:** Candidate 016 proactive completeness expansion, not incorporated into the governed Continuum.

**Business enablement hypotheses:** B10.01 Health Record Stewardship, B10.02 Information Completeness & Provenance Accountability, B10.03 Appropriate Information Access & Sharing, B10.06 Retention, Disclosure & Record Accountability, B10.07 Information Correction & Reconciliation. Additional clinical demand is available through LHR, HIE, diagnostics and provider-directory service journeys; detailed links require review.

**Boundary:** A binary payload, its metadata, the Information Unit that references it, and its physical custodian are distinct. Harmonia may manage a reference to externally held content without copying the bytes. Where Harmonia has custody, suitable media storage is used rather than assuming ordinary IU/database persistence. Sylloge remains the established application responsibility; Mneme/Mnemosyne remain the governed information and persistence boundaries. Agora-native collaboration media remains distinct. References are not automatically publicly dereferenceable.

**Technology interfaces:** ICT Foundation lenses 09 (Platform & Middleware), 11 (Storage & Persistence), 12 (Network & Connectivity), 15 (Observability & Operational Management), 16 (Resilience & Continuity). This does not select object storage, PACS/VNA, DICOM representation, FHIR Binary/DocumentReference operation, or any specific media-server product.

**Release note:** NFR-087 states a *defined size threshold*; the historically discussed 64 KiB boundary should be checked against the exact currently governed release policy rather than silently hardened as a new universally fixed threshold.

#### E01.06.01 — Binary Content Registration & Description **[NEW C016]**
Register content metadata and maintain the information necessary to identify and govern an associated binary payload, without equating the payload with an Information Unit.

- **`E01.06.01.F01` — DescribedContentIdentity:** A registered payload or externally held payload can be distinguished from the associated Information Unit and from other payloads. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.01.F02` — ContentFormatDeclared:** The available type, format and representation details are recorded accurately where supplied or established. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.01.F03` — ContentProvenanceRetained:** The content registration preserves available source, origin and provenance references. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.01.F04` — DescriptiveMetadataBound:** Descriptive metadata can be retrieved without retrieving the full binary content. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.01.F05` — RegistrationExceptionsExplicit:** Unsupported, incomplete or inconsistent registration requests have explicit outcomes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E01.06.02 — Information Unit–Binary Association Management **[NEW C016]**
Associate logical Information Units with zero or more supporting binary payloads while preserving the distinct identity, version and lifecycle semantics of both.

- **`E01.06.02.F01` — AssociationTargetCorrect:** A governed Information Unit association resolves to the intended binary content identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.02.F02` — MultiplePayloadAssociation:** An information item may explicitly reference multiple associated payloads where its defined semantics require them. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.02.F03` — VersionAssociationTraceable:** An association identifies the applicable logical information version and appropriate binary-content version or standing. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.02.F04` — RelationshipChangeGoverned:** Creating, changing or retiring an association is authorised and attributable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.02.F05` — AssociationIntegrityDetectable:** Missing, mismatched or contradictory payload associations are detected or explicitly reported. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E01.06.03 — Binary Custody & Source Location Management **[NEW C016]**
Determine which party or technical service holds physical custody and how content can be resolved while preserving external source authority and storage independence.

- **`E01.06.03.F01` — CustodyKindKnown:** Harmonia-managed and externally managed physical custody are distinguishable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.03.F02` — ExternalContentNoForcedCopy:** A valid externally managed item can be governed and referenced without requiring automatic replication into Harmonia. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.03.F03` — PhysicalLocationIndependent:** Logical references remain stable through authorised storage-provider or physical-location changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.03.F04` — CustodyAvailabilityQualified:** Provider unavailability, known absence and broken reference are reported as distinct cases. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.03.F05` — SourceAuthorityPreserved:** Binary-content management does not imply ownership of originating clinical facts or the external source repository. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E01.06.04 — Large-Content Ingestion & Custody Establishment **[NEW C016]**
Accept or register large content through appropriate governed custody arrangements without using ordinary structured Information Unit persistence for bulk media bytes.

- **`E01.06.04.F01` — ThresholdPolicyApplied:** Content above the release-defined large-content threshold follows the designated media path. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.04.F02` — ReferenceCreatedOnAcceptedIngest:** Where physical custody is accepted, a governed content reference is established and correlated to the accepted payload. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.04.F03` — IngestOutcomeUnambiguous:** Accepted, rejected, incomplete and indeterminate acquisition or storage outcomes are distinguishable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.04.F04` — PayloadByteIntegrityChecked:** Accepted physical content can be verified against the applicable integrity evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.04.F05` — UncommittedMediaNotPublished:** Uncertain or incomplete content capture is not misrepresented as authoritative available content. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E01.06.05 — Governed Binary Retrieval & Delivery **[NEW C016]**
Supply authorised binary content through bounded access semantics without requiring every consumer or information exchange to carry the payload in-band.

- **`E01.06.05.F01` — AuthorisedRetrievalOnly:** Permitted and denied retrievals respect the applicable effective identity and information-protection rules. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.05.F02` — ReferenceBasedAcquisition:** Consumers can acquire the content through a governed reference independently of Information Unit structural data retrieval. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.05.F03` — ResourceBoundedTransfer:** Large-content retrieval can be delivered without unbounded application memory demand under a stated performance contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.05.F04` — AvailabilityFailureQualified:** Missing, inaccessible, corrupted and temporarily unavailable content produce distinguishable outcomes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.05.F05` — TransferIntegrityAssessed:** Received bytes can be verified against the expected governed content identity and integrity evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E01.06.06 — On-Demand Binary Assembly & Presentation **[NEW C016]**
Resolve and assemble the logical content required by a consumer from one or more governed binary payloads when authorised and required.

- **`E01.06.06.F01` — AssemblySourcesTraceable:** The payload sources and transformations contributing to assembled content remain attributable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.06.F02` — MultiPartAssemblyCorrect:** Supported multi-payload content is assembled in the defined order and structure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.06.F03` — SourceAuthorityNotRewritten:** Assembly does not silently change the authoritative status of source information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.06.F04` — PartialAssemblyNotMisrepresented:** Missing or rejected assembly components do not produce a falsely complete result. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.06.F05` — AssemblyDisclosureConstrained:** An assembled result is released only when all applicable disclosure obligations are satisfied. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E01.06.07 — Binary Content Lifecycle & Reference Continuity **[NEW C016]**
Maintain consistency of content references, retention and availability across binary-content lifecycle events, without prescribing a particular storage engine.

- **`E01.06.07.F01` — MediaRetentionRespected:** Applicable retention constraints and holds are preserved by the managed content arrangement. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.07.F02` — MediaReferenceRetained:** Valid media references remain resolvable or explicitly dispositioned across archive, relocation and provider changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.07.F03` — MediaVersionSemanticsExplicit:** Replacement, new representation and version changes do not silently rewrite prior source identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.07.F04` — MediaRetirementGoverned:** Content retirement and deletion are prohibited when retention or external custody constraints disallow them. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.07.F05` — MediaRecoveryAccountable:** Recovery/restoration exercises verify consistency between metadata, governed references and available physical payloads. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E01.06.08 — Binary Content Protection & Operational Integration **[NEW C016]**
Apply security and operational obligations to managed binary content and integrate with fit-for-purpose technical storage and external custodians without treating the platform service as the capability itself.

- **`E01.06.08.F01` — ProtectionContextCarried:** Information labels, relevant authorisation context and disclosure constraints accompany applicable media access. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.08.F02` — MediaAccessAuditable:** Material retrieval, disclosure and governed lifecycle changes are represented in the applicable audit/provenance framework. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.08.F03` — PlatformStorageIntegrationQualified:** Unavailable, degraded and restored media providers are observable through explicit application/technical status interfaces. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.08.F04` — NoGenericDatabaseFallback:** Large media is not silently redirected to ordinary structured Information Unit persistence when its configured media facility is unavailable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E01.06.08.F05` — TechnicalSubstitutionIndependent:** Changing the underlying repository or storage technology does not redefine governed media identities or consumer contract semantics. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

**Non-duplication:** E01.05 remains the general capability to manage and resolve governed references, and E02.04.03 remains authorised reference-based information sharing. E01.06 covers **the governed binary content itself and its association/custody lifecycle**. E05 retains cross-cutting identity/security obligations; E03 governs whichever explicit external API/media contracts are selected.

**Review scenarios:** external PACS or source unavailable but logical reference retained; metadata exists but binary missing; multipart document assembly missing one part; caller can retrieve IU metadata but not binary bytes; provider storage moves without a logical reference change; content exceeds media threshold; media capture fails after partial transfer; authorised historical content requested after archive; malicious substitution/corruption detected; storage unavailable with no fallback to relational IU store.

## E02 — Information Access, Discovery & Distribution
The system can make information available to appropriate consumers at the time and scope required.

### E02.01 — Governed Information Access
Provide controlled retrieval and change through governed contracts.

#### E02.01.01 — Authorised Information Retrieval
Consumers can retrieve the information they are entitled to use.

- **`E02.01.01.F01` — PolicyConformantRead:** proportion of tested retrievals with correct permission and scope outcomes. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.01.F02` — Access Decision Correctness:** proportion of independently checked authorised information retrieval instances satisfying the specified access decision correctness quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.01.F03` — Purpose-Limited Retrieval:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed authorised information retrieval scenarios demonstrating purpose-limited retrieval against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.01.F04` — Retrieval Denial Explanation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed authorised information retrieval scenarios demonstrating retrieval denial explanation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.01.F05` — Retrieved Version Integrity:** proportion of independently checked authorised information retrieval instances satisfying the specified retrieved version integrity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.01.02 — Governed Information Change
Consumers can request appropriately controlled modifications.

- **`E02.01.02.F01` — CorrectChangeDisposition:** proportion of tested changes with correct acceptance, rejection and durability outcome. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.02.F02` — Write Authority Verification:** proportion of positive and negative governed information change test cases meeting the applicable write authority verification criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.02.F03` — Change Precondition Check:** proportion of positive and negative governed information change test cases meeting the applicable change precondition check criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.02.F04` — Accepted Change Durability:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in governed information change for which accepted change durability is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.02.F05` — Governed Rejection Evidence:** proportion of positive and negative governed information change test cases meeting the applicable governed rejection evidence criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.01.03 — Scoped Information Selection
Consumers can request information relevant to an authorised subject or task.

- **`E02.01.03.F01` — ScopeCorrectRetrieval:** proportion of tested results conforming to the requested authorised scope. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.03.F02` — Subject-Scoped Result Accuracy:** proportion of positive and negative scoped information selection test cases meeting the applicable subject-scoped result accuracy criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.03.F03` — Compartment Boundary Conformance:** proportion of positive and negative scoped information selection test cases meeting the applicable compartment boundary conformance criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.03.F04` — Irrelevant Result Exclusion:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed scoped information selection scenarios demonstrating irrelevant result exclusion against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.01.03.F05` — Contextual Selection Explanation:** proportion of applicable scoped information selection requests or situations with verified contextual selection explanation. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.01.04 — Authorised Version-Specific Retrieval **[NEW C012]**
Allow a consumer to retrieve a precisely identified governed version with explicit authority and absence outcomes.

*Demand: B02.01, B10.03, B10.07; evidence: MN, IU, SEC; findings: R05, R09.*

- **`E02.01.04.F01` — VersionSelectionCorrect:** Specified historical reference returns only the designated version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.01.04.F02` — DenialNotAbsence:** Unauthorised retrieval cannot be mistaken for absence of information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.01.04.F03` — ReadEvidenceAttributed:** Disclosures requiring audit are correlated with requesting authority and source. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.01.04.F04` — RepresentationMeaningPreserved:** Returned data retains sufficient model context for interpretation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
