- **`E02.01.04.F05` — StaleReadQualified:** Where currentness is not assured the response exposes its temporal/availability qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E02.01.05 — Purpose-Limited Disclosure **[NEW C012]**
Make authorised information available only in the requested and permitted purpose, content and temporal scope.

*Demand: B01.05, B10.03, B10.04, B14.06; evidence: SEC, TH, IU; findings: R02, R22.*

- **`E02.01.05.F01` — PurposeConstraintApplied:** Purpose-incompatible information is not disclosed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.01.05.F02` — CompartmentFilterEnforced:** Compartment-restricted content does not escape filtered responses. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.01.05.F03` — DelegatedScopeValidated:** Representative permissions are checked against applicable current authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.01.05.F04` — NoUnauthorisedMetadataLeak:** Denied results expose no protected presence/identity hints. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.01.05.F05` — DisclosureDecisionAttributable:** Permit and deny decisions can be examined with effective authority context. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E02.02 — Information Discovery & Search
Locate information and return appropriate versioned references/results.

#### E02.02.01 — Version-Referenced Information Search
Search can locate governed information and identify the exact versions returned.

- **`E02.02.01.F01` — ResolvableSearchReferences:** proportion of returned version references that resolve to intended versions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.01.F02` — Correct Version Targeting:** proportion of independently checked version-referenced information search instances satisfying the specified correct version targeting quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.01.F03` — Search Result Completeness Qualification:** proportion of independently checked version-referenced information search instances satisfying the specified search result completeness qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.01.F04` — Search Reference Persistence:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed version-referenced information search scenarios demonstrating search reference persistence against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.01.F05` — Controlled Version Disclosure:** proportion of independently checked version-referenced information search instances satisfying the specified controlled version disclosure quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.02.02 — Contextual Information Discovery
Search reflects applicable business and information constraints.

- **`E02.02.02.F01` — CorrectSearchScoping:** proportion of test searches returning only in-scope permitted results. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.02.F02` — Purpose-Relevant Query Scope:** proportion of positive and negative contextual information discovery test cases meeting the applicable purpose-relevant query scope criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.02.F03` — Authorised Result Filtering:** proportion of positive and negative contextual information discovery test cases meeting the applicable authorised result filtering criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.02.F04` — Discoverability of Relevant Sources:** proportion of sampled contextual information discovery cases with accessible, accurate evidence of discoverability of relevant sources. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.02.F05` — Explainable Result Qualification:** proportion of independently checked contextual information discovery instances satisfying the specified explainable result qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.02.03 — Search Result Continuity
A discovered result collection remains interpretable for subsequent governed work.

- **`E02.02.03.F01` — ReusableSearchResultContext:** proportion of persisted result collections retaining required meaning and references. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.03.F02` — Persistent Result-Set Identity:** proportion of independently checked search result continuity instances satisfying the specified persistent result-set identity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.03.F03` — Stable Result References:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed search result continuity scenarios demonstrating stable result references against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.03.F04` — Reusable Selection Context:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in search result continuity for which reusable selection context is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.02.03.F05` — Result-Set Change Awareness:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in search result continuity for which result-set change awareness is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.02.04 — Governed Search Completeness Qualification **[NEW C012]**
Describe which authoritative search domain, versions and filters were actually evaluated.

*Demand: B02.01, B10.03, B13.05; evidence: MN, IU, RESULT; findings: R09, R18.*

- **`E02.02.04.F01` — SearchScopeDeclared:** Results identify source scope and temporal cut-off. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.04.F02` — SearchNotCacheOnly:** Complete authoritative searches are not satisfied from incomplete active-cache state. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.04.F03` — ResultVersionsPinned:** Persisted result sets retain version references used during the search. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.04.F04` — SearchDenialNotMissing:** Search restriction is distinguishable from no matching authoritative content. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.04.F05` — PartialSearchExposed:** Partial failures or inaccessible partitions are reported rather than implied complete. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E02.02.05 — Cross-Domain Discovery & Correlation **[NEW C012]**
Discover information across healthcare domains using governed identifiers while avoiding identity conflation.

*Demand: B02.01, B05.04, B13.05; evidence: IU, REF, MODEL; findings: R09.*

- **`E02.02.05.F01` — SubjectCorrelationQualified:** Cross-domain correlations report matching confidence and provenance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.05.F02` — DifferentIdentityTypesPreserved:** Person, episode and IU identities remain distinguishable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.05.F03` — DuplicateCandidatesExposed:** Ambiguous matches are visible rather than silently merged. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.05.F04` — TemporalCorrelationCorrect:** Cross-domain results retain applicable occurrence and recording times. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.05.F05` — UnauthorisedSourcesNotExposed:** Search federation does not disclose unauthorised source existence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E02.02.06 — Subject-Linked Longitudinal Information Discovery **[NEW C014]**
Discover available clinical information across encounters and sources while qualifying the governed subject association.

- **`E02.02.06.F01` — SubjectLinkageQualified:** Information is not associated with a person when the available identity evidence is insufficient. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.06.F02` — MultiEpisodeDiscovery:** Relevant information can be discovered across more than one encounter or provider without assuming co-location. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.06.F03` — HistoricVersionSelectable:** Discovery can distinguish historic information versions from current versions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.06.F04` — DistributedCompletenessQualified:** Unavailable or excluded sources are visible as coverage qualifications where lawful. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.06.F05` — SourceAndTimeRetained:** Discovered information retains its source, effective time and record-version association. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-LHR-QUERY, C-FED-QUERY, C-PERSON-CORR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E02.02.07 — Cross-Source Clinical Record Correspondence **[NEW C014]**
Recognise probable duplicate, related and conflicting clinical information without silently collapsing distinct source records.

- **`E02.02.07.F01` — CrossSourceDuplicateQualification:** Same-looking records from different sources remain distinguishable pending governed correspondence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.07.F02` — RelatedNotIdentical:** A derived clinical summary is distinguishable from the information from which it was derived. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.07.F03` — ClinicalContradictionVisible:** Conflicting clinical facts can be surfaced as qualified competing assertions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.07.F04` — ReferenceCorrelationTraceable:** Each correspondence retains references to the contributing governed information versions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.02.07.F05` — UnsafeConflationPrevented:** Adverse tests demonstrate no incorrect silent fusion of clinically distinct information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-FED-QUERY, DRP-IU. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### E02.03 — Distributed Information Availability
Maintain/use distributed or cached information where appropriate to access and performance needs.

#### E02.03.01 — Distributed Access
Consumers can access and change governed information consistently across eligible system locations and concurrent activity.

- **`E02.03.01.F01` — FastRead:** retrieval responsiveness under defined loads. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.01.F02` — GovernedWrite:** proportion of prescribed distributed changes receiving correct governed acceptance, rejection and durability outcome. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.01.F03` — ConsistentSharedAccess:** proportion of prescribed concurrent operations meeting the required consistency outcome. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.01.F04` — Distributed Read Availability:** proportion of applicable distributed access requests or situations with verified distributed read availability. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.01.F05` — Conflicting Update Recognition:** proportion of sampled distributed access cases with accessible, accurate evidence of conflicting update recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.01.F06` — Correct Cross-Node State:** proportion of independently checked distributed access instances satisfying the specified correct cross-node state quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.01.F07` — Access Recovery after Node Loss:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in distributed access for which access recovery after node loss is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.03.02 — Distributed Reference Availability
Information references remain useful when access paths cross system boundaries.

- **`E02.03.02.F01` — CrossLocationReferenceResolution:** proportion of in-scope requests resolving correctly across permitted locations. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.02.F02` — Cross-Node Reference Fidelity:** proportion of independently checked distributed reference availability instances satisfying the specified cross-node reference fidelity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.02.F03` — Reference Resolution Continuity:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in distributed reference availability for which reference resolution continuity is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.02.F04` — Invalid Reference Detection:** proportion of independently checked distributed reference availability instances satisfying the specified invalid reference detection quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.02.F05` — Distributed Access Permission:** proportion of positive and negative distributed reference availability test cases meeting the applicable distributed access permission criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.03.03 — Fresh Information Availability
Consumers can distinguish information that is suitably current from information that is stale.

- **`E02.03.03.F01` — FreshnessConformantAccess:** proportion of in-scope reads satisfying their required currency constraints. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.03.F02` — Declared Information Currency:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in fresh information availability for which declared information currency is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.03.F03` — Stale Data Recognition:** proportion of sampled fresh information availability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of stale data recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.03.F04` — Currency-Constrained Retrieval:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in fresh information availability for which currency-constrained retrieval is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.03.03.F05` — Refresh Availability:** proportion of applicable fresh information availability requests or situations with verified refresh availability. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.03.04 — Distributed Access Consistency **[NEW C012]**
Preserve consumer-visible access and update semantics across concurrent nodes and partitions.

*Demand: B05.06, B10.01, B12.04; evidence: DIST, RES, NFR; findings: R10, R21.*

- **`E02.03.04.F01` — ConcurrentVersionSafety:** Concurrent updates obey declared version policy without silent lost changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.03.04.F02` — NodeFailureAccessContinuity:** Permitted reads remain available or degrade explicitly after one node loss. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.03.04.F03` — PartitionOutcomeExplicit:** Partition uncertainty never appears as unqualified authoritative success. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.03.04.F04` — ReplicaCurrencyQualified:** Observed replica freshness is exposed where material. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.03.04.F05` — RecoveryNoIdentityChange:** After recovery no governed reference changes identity or version unexpectedly. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E02.03.05 — Accepted Clinical Information Availability **[NEW C014]**
Make accepted received clinical information available through governed access despite its source, ingest route or internal storage placement.

- **`E02.03.05.F01` — AcceptedReceiptAccessible:** Accepted information is retrievable or has an explicitly governed pending/recovery disposition. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.03.05.F02` — AccessNotRawIngressOnly:** An exchange receipt is not the sole location from which accepted clinical content may be obtained. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.03.05.F03` — NoFalseAvailability:** A transport ACK does not imply authoritative persisted availability unless the applicable acceptance contract establishes it. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.03.05.F04` — SourceLossContinuity:** Accepted information remains recoverable through specified source and infrastructure failures. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.03.05.F05` — AuthorisedAvailability:** Availability to an authorised consumer never overrides subject, compartment or disclosure controls. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-DOC-INGRESS, C-HIE-GATE, DRP-IU, DRP-MN. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### E02.04 — Information Distribution & Notification
Make appropriate information or references available across required participants.

#### E02.04.01 — Selective Information Distribution
Relevant information or references reach authorised recipients according to governing rules.

- **`E02.04.01.F01` — CorrectDistributionScope:** proportion of distributions reaching only intended recipients. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.01.F02` — Intended Recipient Selection:** proportion of applicable selective information distribution interactions where intended recipient selection reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.01.F03` — Distribution Scope Conformance:** proportion of positive and negative selective information distribution test cases meeting the applicable distribution scope conformance criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.01.F04` — Confidentiality-Constrained Delivery:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing selective information distribution scenarios with correct confidentiality-constrained delivery, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.01.F05` — Distribution Status Visibility:** proportion of sampled selective information distribution cases with accessible, accurate evidence of distribution status visibility. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.04.02 — Information Availability Notification
Consumers can be informed when relevant information becomes available or changes.

- **`E02.04.02.F01` — TimelyRelevantNotification:** proportion of qualifying updates notified within required conditions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.02.F02` — Notification Relevance:** proportion of applicable information availability notification interactions where notification relevance reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.02.F03` — Recipient Delivery Visibility:** proportion of sampled information availability notification cases with accessible, accurate evidence of recipient delivery visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.02.F04` — Notification Currency:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in information availability notification for which notification currency is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.02.F05` — Duplicate Notification Control:** proportion of independently checked information availability notification instances satisfying the specified duplicate notification control quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.04.03 — Referenced Information Sharing
Large or governed information is shared as an appropriate reference when applicable.

- **`E02.04.03.F01` — ValidSharedReference:** proportion of applicable distributions using resolvable authorised references. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.03.F02` — Recipient Reference Usability:** proportion of applicable referenced information sharing interactions where recipient reference usability reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.03.F03` — Disclosure Scope Enforcement:** proportion of positive and negative referenced information sharing test cases meeting the applicable disclosure scope enforcement criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.03.F04` — No-Unnecessary-Content Transfer:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in referenced information sharing for which no-unnecessary-content transfer is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.04.03.F05` — Shared Reference Expiration:** proportion of applicable referenced information sharing interactions where shared reference expiration reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.04.04 — Governed Subscription Matching **[NEW C012]**
Select interested consumers based on governed information and event characteristics without making internal metadata a public API.

*Demand: B05.06, B10.03, B13.01; evidence: IU, REF, SEC; findings: R05, R18.*

- **`E02.04.04.F01` — SubscriptionMatchCorrect:** Authorized subscribers receive events matching agreed criteria. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.04.04.F02` — NoRestrictedDelivery:** Subscriber filters never override local information disclosure requirements. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.04.04.F03` — ChangeNotificationCorrelation:** Notifications identify the changed governed unit and relevant version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.04.04.F04` — WithdrawalStopsDelivery:** Revoked subscription or authority stops further protected delivery. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.04.04.F05` — DeliveryQualificationKnown:** Delayed, duplicate or failed notices are distinguishable where delivery is tracked. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E02.04.05 — Selective Publication & Withdrawn Information **[NEW C012]**
Make new, superseded and withdrawn information availability visible without modifying immutable historic versions.

*Demand: B05.04, B10.03, B10.07; evidence: IU, SEC; findings: R05, R18.*

- **`E02.04.05.F01` — PublicationScopeApplied:** Only authorised target consumers see published material. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.04.05.F02` — SupersessionTraceable:** Receivers can locate the subsequent governed version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.04.05.F03` — WithdrawalCommunicated:** Impacted consumers receive a qualified withdrawal notification where required. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.04.05.F04` — HistoricViewPreserved:** Withdrawal leaves required historical version references interpretable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.04.05.F05` — DuplicateNoticeHandled:** Repeated notifications do not create conflicting authoritative change records. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E02.05 — Information Access Continuity
Preserve useful access through recoverable disruptions where architecturally supported.

#### E02.05.01 — Continuing Information Access
Governed access remains useful through supported infrastructure and service disruptions.

- **`E02.05.01.F01` — ContinuityOfAccess:** proportion of specified disruption scenarios meeting access availability conditions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.05.01.F02` — Alternate Access Path Availability:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in continuing information access for which alternate access path availability is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.05.01.F03` — Degraded Access Disclosure:** proportion of sampled continuing information access cases with accessible, accurate evidence of degraded access disclosure. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.05.01.F04` — Continuity under Node Failure:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in continuing information access for which continuity under node failure is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.05.01.F05` — Essential Read Priority:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed continuing information access scenarios demonstrating essential read priority against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.05.02 — Access Restoration
Consumers can regain correct access following interruption without violating governing controls.

- **`E02.05.02.F01` — CorrectRestoredAccess:** proportion of restored sessions/requests satisfying scope, freshness and security requirements. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.05.02.F02` — Recovery of Authorised Access:** proportion of positive and negative access restoration test cases meeting the applicable recovery of authorised access criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.05.02.F03` — Restoration Outcome Confirmation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in access restoration for which restoration outcome confirmation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.05.02.F04` — Recovery Status Visibility:** proportion of sampled access restoration cases with accessible, accurate evidence of recovery status visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E02.05.02.F05` — Post-Recovery Access Consistency:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in access restoration for which post-recovery access consistency is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E02.05.03 — Degraded Access & Restoration **[NEW C012]**
Report and restore governed access following outage without promising unverified data freshness or availability.

*Demand: B05.06, B12.04, B10.01; evidence: RES, DIST, OPS; findings: R10, R24.*

- **`E02.05.03.F01` — AccessDegradationVisible:** Users and consumers can distinguish degraded and normal access modes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.05.03.F02` — RestoredVersionVerified:** Recovered access returns an authoritative version after consistency checks. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.05.03.F03` — HIEAvailabilityProtected:** Accepted incoming clinical content remains retrievable after relevant failure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.05.03.F04` — RecoveryTimeEvidence:** Recovery sequence and relevant timing are recorded for conformance assessment. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.05.03.F05` — NeverInventedEmptyResult:** Temporary inaccessibility is not reported as no record found. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E02.06 — Entity & Service Directory Discovery **[NEW C014]**
Discover, resolve and qualify healthcare entities, organisations, service offerings and their relationships through governed directory contracts, independently of any specific directory application.

*Evidence-led candidate.* Lenses: PD,EMPI,HIE,LHR; sources: C-PRAC-DIR,C-ORG-DIR,C-SVC-DIR,C-PRAC-AFF. No solution component or release commitment implied.

#### E02.06.01 — Directory Record Discovery **[NEW C014]**
Resolve attributable, uniquely identified entries within the applicable directory domain.

- **`E02.06.01.F01` — IdentifiableDirectoryEntry:** Tested directory results distinguish a record identity from its represented person, organisation or service identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.01.F02` — PurposeBoundDiscovery:** Access-control tests show each directory search discloses only information permitted for the actual purpose. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.01.F03` — MultipleEntryDisambiguation:** Ambiguous or duplicate directory matches are returned with an explicit qualifier, never silently assumed equivalent. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.01.F04` — MissingEntryTruthfulness:** Unavailable or absent entries remain distinguishable from failed or denied directory retrieval. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.01.F05` — DirectoryReferenceContinuity:** A saved directory reference can be resolved to the intended entry or an explicit change/disposition. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-PRAC-DIR, C-ORG-DIR, C-SVC-DIR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E02.06.02 — Provider Role & Affiliation Resolution **[NEW C014]**
Obtain the context-bound roles, organisational relationships and privileges relevant to a healthcare provider.

- **`E02.06.02.F01` — AffiliationTemporality:** Past, prospective and currently effective affiliations are distinguishable in tested responses. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.02.F02` — RoleScopeExplicit:** An individual’s role is returned with the organisation or care setting in which it applies. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.02.F03` — PrivilegeNotInferredFromAuthentication:** A valid login never alone implies a current clinical privilege or professional authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.02.F04` — ConflictingAffiliationVisible:** Contradictory affiliations are attributed or qualified rather than silently overwritten. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.02.F05` — ProviderRelationshipEvidence:** Users can trace each returned relationship to its governed source and relevant effective period. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-PRAC-AFF, C-PRAC-VERIFY, C-PRAC-DIR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E02.06.03 — Service & Location Discovery **[NEW C014]**
Find current service offerings, provision locations, contact routes and operating context.

- **`E02.06.03.F01` — ServiceEligibilitySeparability:** A discovered service is not presented as guaranteed entitlement or appointment availability. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.03.F02` — LocationContextAccuracy:** Location and service-provider associations are correctly scoped at the effective time. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.03.F03` — ServiceContactUsability:** Representative service searches yield a usable and appropriately authorised contact or endpoint route. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.03.F04` — ServiceDiscontinuationVisible:** Withdrawn or suspended service offerings are not falsely presented as currently available. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.03.F05` — MultiOrganisationDiscovery:** Searches across organisations retain source attribution and distinguish unavailable providers from none matching. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-SVC-DIR, C-ORG-DIR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E02.06.04 — Directory Currency & Provenance Qualification **[NEW C014]**
Qualify directory entries by source, verified currency, lifecycle and conflicting assertions.

- **`E02.06.04.F01` — VerificationStatusVisible:** A consumer can tell verified, unverified and stale directory assertions apart. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.04.F02` — SourceResponsibilityExplicit:** The party responsible for the asserted entry or relationship is identified or its absence declared. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.04.F03` — TemporalHistoryRetained:** An affiliation or location change does not erase the interpretability of its prior effective state. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.04.F04` — ContradictionQualified:** Conflicting source assertions are explicitly marked and made reviewable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.04.F05` — UnresolvedDirectoryChangeAuditable:** An unverified change is not silently promoted to trusted effective directory data. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-PRAC-VERIFY, C-PRAC-DIR, DRP-IU. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E02.06.05 — Directory Change Notification **[NEW C014]**
Publish governed directory entry and relationship changes to eligible interested consumers.

- **`E02.06.05.F01` — RelevantChangeSubscription:** Eligible directory consumers receive or can discover changes within the declared criteria. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.05.F02` — WithdrawnAccessStopsDisclosure:** Access or subscription withdrawal prevents future unauthorised directory change delivery. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.05.F03` — ChangeEventVersionCorrelation:** A change notice links to the correct entity/relationship identity and version or status. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.05.F04` — UnreportedChangeDetected:** Delayed or undelivered change notices are discernible rather than interpreted as no change. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.05.F05` — NotificationNotAuthoritativeUpdate:** Receiving a directory change notification does not independently establish an authoritative correction. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-CT2-EVENT, C-PRAC-DIR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E02.06.06 — Federated Directory Resolution **[NEW C014]**
Resolve entities and service offerings across separately authoritative directory sources without pretending that all sources share one current truth.

- **`E02.06.06.F01` — FederatedAuthorityAttribution:** Each returned assertion has a discernible source and authority qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.06.F02` — PartialFederationQualified:** Incomplete or failing directory sources produce an explicit completeness qualifier. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.06.F03` — EntityCrossReferenceNotAutomaticMerge:** Linkable records are not silently merged solely because a search returns them together. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.06.F04` — CrossDirectorySourceIsolation:** One source’s error or unavailability does not corrupt independently valid results from another. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.06.F05` — FederatedResultSecurity:** Identity, provider and endpoint details obey each source’s applicable disclosure constraints. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-ORG-DIR, C-PRAC-DIR, C-PERSON-RESOLVE. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E02.06.07 — Directory Disclosure & Boundary Control **[NEW C014]**
Provide safe and understandable differences between public, partner, practitioner and administrative views of directory information.

- **`E02.06.07.F01` — PublicPartnerInternalSeparation:** Public, partner and internal consumers obtain only their authorised directory representations. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.07.F02` — ContactDetailRestriction:** Restricted contacts are excluded from unauthorised directory responses and notifications. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.07.F03` — DirectoryQueryAuditCoverage:** Disclosure events meeting audit criteria remain attributable to the request and effective authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.07.F04` — LegitimateMinimalResult:** A restricted query can return the usable minimum without revealing protected nonmatches. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E02.06.07.F05` — BoundaryRuleConsistency:** The same governed assertion is not disclosed inconsistently merely because a different directory access path is used. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: DRP-SEC, C-PRAC-DIR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.



## E03 — Digital Interoperability & Service Interaction
The system can communicate with external/internal services using understandable, governed contracts.

### E03.01 — External Information Exchange
Exchange information across system and organisational boundaries.

#### E03.01.01 — Standards-Based Clinical Exchange
External parties can exchange supported clinical information under authoritative contracts.

- **`E03.01.01.F01` — ContractConformantClinicalExchange:** proportion of qualified exchanges satisfying applicable exchange specifications. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.01.F02` — Contract Conformant Payloads:** proportion of positive and negative standards-based clinical exchange test cases meeting the applicable contract conformant payloads criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.01.F03` — Clinically Meaningful Content Preservation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in standards-based clinical exchange for which clinically meaningful content preservation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.01.F04` — External Acknowledgement Correctness:** proportion of independently checked standards-based clinical exchange instances satisfying the specified external acknowledgement correctness quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.01.F05` — Unsupported Variation Reporting:** proportion of sampled standards-based clinical exchange cases with accessible, accurate evidence of unsupported variation reporting. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.01.02 — Cross-Organisation Information Exchange
Information can be exchanged appropriately between separate healthcare organisations.

- **`E03.01.02.F01` — SuccessfulAuthorisedExternalExchange:** proportion of valid authorised inter-organisation exchanges completed correctly. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.02.F02` — Recipient Endpoint Trust:** proportion of applicable cross-organisation information exchange interactions where recipient endpoint trust reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.02.F03` — Cross-Boundary Context Preservation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in cross-organisation information exchange for which cross-boundary context preservation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.02.F04` — Exchange Outcome Visibility:** proportion of sampled cross-organisation information exchange cases with accessible, accurate evidence of exchange outcome visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.02.F05` — Failed Exchange Resolution:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in cross-organisation information exchange for which failed exchange resolution is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.01.03 — External Exchange Acceptance
An inbound exchange receives a clear governed acceptance or rejection outcome.

- **`E03.01.03.F01` — DefinedInboundOutcome:** proportion of qualifying inbound interactions with correct outcome and evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.03.F02` — Durable Accepted Exchange:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in external exchange acceptance for which durable accepted exchange is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.03.F03` — Validated Incoming Contract:** proportion of positive and negative external exchange acceptance test cases meeting the applicable validated incoming contract criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.03.F04` — Duplicate Exchange Recognition:** proportion of independently checked external exchange acceptance instances satisfying the specified duplicate exchange recognition quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.01.03.F05` — Rejected Exchange Explanation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in external exchange acceptance for which rejected exchange explanation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.01.04 — External Acceptance versus Receipt **[NEW C012]**
Separate transport-level receipt, validation, governed acceptance and clinical responsibility acceptance.

*Demand: B04.03, B05.03, B10.01; evidence: EXCH, MN, NFR; findings: R16.*

- **`E03.01.04.F01` — TransportReceiptClassified:** Transport receipt is never presented as authoritative durable acceptance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.01.04.F02` — ValidationRejectionExplicit:** Invalid incoming content has a distinct actionable rejection result. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.01.04.F03` — DurableAcceptanceCorrelated:** Positive authoritative acceptance corresponds to retrieved governed content. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.01.04.F04` — DuplicateSubmissionHandled:** Repeat submissions preserve idempotency or explicit duplicate meaning. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.01.04.F05` — ClinicalResponsibilityNotInferred:** Technical exchange responses do not assert clinical handover acceptance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E03.02 — Service Contract Exposure & Consumption
Offer and use controlled system services where authorised.

#### E03.02.01 — Governed Service Exposure
Authorised consumers can request published information-system services.

- **`E03.02.01.F01` — ContractConformantServiceAccess:** proportion of test calls satisfying exposure and access contracts. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.01.F02` — Authorised Interface Availability:** proportion of positive and negative governed service exposure test cases meeting the applicable authorised interface availability criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.01.F03` — Contract Version Visibility:** proportion of positive and negative governed service exposure test cases meeting the applicable contract version visibility criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.01.F04` — Request Scope Enforcement:** proportion of positive and negative governed service exposure test cases meeting the applicable request scope enforcement criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.01.F05` — Interface Failure Signalling:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed governed service exposure scenarios demonstrating interface failure signalling against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.02.02 — Governed Service Consumption
The information system can use external services within required constraints.

- **`E03.02.02.F01` — ControlledExternalServiceUse:** proportion of in-scope external calls meeting identity, contract and outcome conditions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.02.F02` — Service Endpoint Trust:** proportion of applicable governed service consumption requests or situations with verified service endpoint trust. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.02.F03` — Controlled Outbound Request:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed governed service consumption scenarios demonstrating controlled outbound request against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.02.F04` — Response Contract Validation:** proportion of positive and negative governed service consumption test cases meeting the applicable response contract validation criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.02.F05` — External Dependency Failure Handling:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed governed service consumption scenarios demonstrating external dependency failure handling against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.02.03 — Service Interaction Discoverability
Service consumers can identify the supported capabilities and interaction contracts available to them.

- **`E03.02.03.F01` — AccurateServiceContractPublication:** proportion of sampled published contracts matching actual supported behaviour. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.03.F02` — Discoverable Service Purpose:** proportion of applicable service interaction discoverability requests or situations with verified discoverable service purpose. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.03.F03` — Published Contract Currency:** proportion of positive and negative service interaction discoverability test cases meeting the applicable published contract currency criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.03.F04` — Consumer Eligibility Clarity:** proportion of positive and negative service interaction discoverability test cases meeting the applicable consumer eligibility clarity criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.02.03.F05` — Interface Deprecation Visibility:** proportion of sampled service interaction discoverability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of interface deprecation visibility. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.02.04 — Consumer-Specific Contract Negotiation **[NEW C012]**
Expose stable service contracts and their compatible versions without binding consumers to implementation topology.

*Demand: B07.02, B10.05, B12.05; evidence: MODEL, EXCH, NFR; findings: R13.*

- **`E03.02.04.F01` — ContractVersionDeclared:** Consumer sees the agreed contract and version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.04.F02` — UnsupportedContractRejected:** Unsupported protocol/version is rejected with a bounded explanation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.04.F03` — ContractChangeTraceable:** A contract change has a traceable publication and compatibility history. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.04.F04` — ImplementationTopologyHidden:** Client-facing contract is unchanged by internal provider replacement. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.04.F05` — ContractAuthnRequired:** Protected contracts cannot be invoked through anonymous fallback. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.02.05 — Service Invocation Evidence & Accountability **[NEW C012]**
Correlate service calls, consumers and outcomes without treating invocation as completed business work.

*Demand: B05.03, B10.02, B11.05; evidence: AUD, NFR, TH; findings: R16, R18.*

- **`E03.02.05.F01` — InvocationIdentityCorrelated:** Service interaction references requester and execution context. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.05.F02` — OutcomeDistinctFromRequest:** Accepted invocation is not reported as completed processing. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.05.F03` — CrossBoundaryTraceCoherent:** Distributed spans can be connected without logging protected content by default. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.05.F04` — AuthorisationEnforced:** Contract consumption revalidates applicable effective authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.05.F05` — AuditableRejectionRecorded:** Mandatory denied or rejected attempts contribute the required audit evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.02.06 — Partner Exchange Contract Resolution **[NEW C014]**
Resolve the applicable exchange profile, endpoint and acceptance terms for an external provider or system.

- **`E03.02.06.F01` — PartnerProfileVersionExplicit:** The selected external profile and version are identifiable at the exchange boundary. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.06.F02` — EndpointAuthorityChecked:** The intended delivery endpoint is associated with the properly authorised partner. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.06.F03` — ContractUnsupportedExplicit:** Unsupported partner versions or contracts yield explicit rejection or qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.06.F04` — PartnerChangeEffective:** An updated endpoint/profile follows defined effective-time rules. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.02.06.F05` — ExternalAuthorityNotAssumed:** Matching a partner endpoint does not itself prove authority to disclose patient information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-HIE-GATE, C-SVC-DIR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### E03.03 — Protocol & Format Mediation
Accommodate supported exchange formats/protocols while preserving meaning.

#### E03.03.01 — Supported Exchange Format Handling
Consumers can exchange supported information formats without needless loss of meaning.

- **`E03.03.01.F01` — CorrectFormatHandling:** proportion of applicable specimens accepted/transformed/rejected according to format contract. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.01.F02` — Format Correctness:** proportion of independently checked supported exchange format handling instances satisfying the specified format correctness quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.01.F03` — Character Set Preservation:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in supported exchange format handling for which character set preservation is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.01.F04` — Format Variant Visibility:** proportion of sampled supported exchange format handling cases with accessible, accurate evidence of format variant visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.01.F05` — Unsupported Format Rejection:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed supported exchange format handling scenarios demonstrating unsupported format rejection against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.03.02 — Protocol Boundary Adaptation
Different supported interaction mechanisms can be accommodated at their declared boundaries.

- **`E03.03.02.F01` — CorrectProtocolBoundaryOutcome:** proportion of tested protocol interactions satisfying declared boundary rules. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.02.F02` — Correct Protocol Framing:** proportion of independently checked protocol boundary adaptation instances satisfying the specified correct protocol framing quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.02.F03` — Timeout and Retry Discipline:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed protocol boundary adaptation scenarios demonstrating timeout and retry discipline against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.02.F04` — Protocol Failure Explanation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed protocol boundary adaptation scenarios demonstrating protocol failure explanation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.02.F05` — Exchange Boundary Isolation:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing protocol boundary adaptation scenarios with correct exchange boundary isolation, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.03.03 — Native Representation Preservation
Authoritative information representations remain intact where preservation is required.

- **`E03.03.03.F01` — StandardsNativeFidelity:** proportion of required preserved representations remaining conformant and retrievable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.03.F02` — Standards-Native Data Fidelity:** proportion of independently checked native representation preservation instances satisfying the specified standards-native data fidelity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.03.F03` — Original Payload Retention:** proportion of sampled native representation preservation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of original payload retention. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.03.F04` — Transformation Qualification:** proportion of independently checked native representation preservation instances satisfying the specified transformation qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.03.03.F05` — Round-Trip Representation Verification:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed native representation preservation scenarios demonstrating round-trip representation verification against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.03.04 — Standards-Native Content Preservation **[NEW C012]**
Retain incoming native content and the governed representation relationship when standards require preservation.

*Demand: B10.01, B04.03, B13.05; evidence: IU, EXCH, MODEL; findings: R09, R13.*

- **`E03.03.04.F01` — NativePayloadRetained:** Original standard-native representation is retrievable when governed preservation applies. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.03.04.F02` — DerivedMappingLinked:** Any mapped Information Unit refers to its native source provenance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.03.04.F03` — UnknownFieldsReported:** Unmapped or unsupported attributes receive explicit handling qualifications. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.03.04.F04` — RoundTripLossMeasured:** Known semantic loss is detected and reported in controlled round trips. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.03.04.F05` — NoFakeStandardsConformance:** Conformance reporting distinguishes native compliance and local mapping acceptance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.03.05 — Protocol Boundary Isolation **[NEW C012]**
Adapt network and message protocols without exposing transport implementation as a business-domain service contract.

*Demand: B07.02, B05.06; evidence: NFR, EXCH; findings: R16.*

- **`E03.03.05.F01` — ConfigurableBoundaryTimeouts:** MLLP and equivalent boundary timeout policies are tested. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.03.05.F02` — SlowPeerIsolation:** One slow or malformed peer does not block unrelated exchanges. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.03.05.F03` — AcknowledgementModeConformance:** Configured protocol acknowledgement modes match the applicable standard. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.03.05.F04` — BoundaryErrorAttribution:** A transport fault can be correlated to the affected interaction. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.03.05.F05` — MalformedPayloadContained:** Failed format handling does not create authoritative clinical information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E03.04 — Interaction Acknowledgement & Error Handling
Provide suitable receipts, rejection and error indications for interactions.

#### E03.04.01 — Exchange Receipt Confirmation
An interacting party can learn whether an exchange was received and accepted.

- **`E03.04.01.F01` — CorrectExchangeAcknowledgement:** proportion of applicable exchanges receiving the specified acknowledgement outcome. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.01.F02` — Timely Transport Acknowledgement:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the timely transport acknowledgement outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.01.F03` — Standard-Compliant Acknowledgement:** proportion of positive and negative exchange receipt confirmation test cases meeting the applicable standard-compliant acknowledgement criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.01.F04` — Acknowledgement Correlation:** proportion of applicable exchange receipt confirmation interactions where acknowledgement correlation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.01.F05` — Durable-Acceptance Signalling:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed exchange receipt confirmation scenarios demonstrating durable-acceptance signalling against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.04.02 — Actionable Exchange Rejection
Rejected interactions provide sufficient detail for an authorised consumer to respond.

- **`E03.04.02.F01` — UsableRejectionInformation:** proportion of tested rejected exchanges containing required actionable detail. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.02.F02` — Precise Rejection Cause:** proportion of independently checked actionable exchange rejection instances satisfying the specified precise rejection cause quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.02.F03` — Recoverable Rejection Classification:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in actionable exchange rejection for which recoverable rejection classification is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.02.F04` — Safe Rejection Handling:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing actionable exchange rejection scenarios with correct safe rejection handling, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.02.F05` — Rejection Notification Trace:** proportion of sampled actionable exchange rejection cases with accessible, accurate evidence of rejection notification trace. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.04.03 — Bounded Interaction Outcome
Consumers can determine the result of interactions that fail or exceed prescribed time limits.

- **`E03.04.03.F01` — DeterminateTimedOutcome:** proportion of tested timeout/failure cases yielding defined outcome or reconciliation path. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.03.F02` — Explicit Interaction Completion:** proportion of applicable bounded interaction outcome requests or situations with verified explicit interaction completion. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.03.F03` — Time-Limited Uncertainty:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed bounded interaction outcome scenarios demonstrating time-limited uncertainty against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.03.F04` — Idempotent Duplicate Handling:** proportion of independently checked bounded interaction outcome instances satisfying the specified idempotent duplicate handling quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.04.03.F05` — Outcome Correlation:** proportion of assessed bounded interaction outcome cases with observable outcome correlation against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.04.04 — End-to-End Exchange State Reconciliation **[NEW C012]**
Make uncertain exchange results discoverable after retries, lost acknowledgements or boundary failure.

*Demand: B04.03, B05.02, B10.01; evidence: EXCH, MN, PERSIST; findings: R16, R06.*

- **`E03.04.04.F01` — UnknownDispositionQuery:** A sender can obtain or reconcile an initially unknown interaction outcome. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.04.F02` — RetrySafeCorrelation:** Replays do not create unexplained duplicate authoritative content. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.04.F03` — ResponseCommitConsistency:** Positive business acceptance has durable downstream evidence where promised. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.04.F04` — FailureStageIdentifiable:** Failure can be attributed to receipt, validation, acceptance or publication stage. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.04.F05` — OutOfOrderReplyHandled:** Late acknowledgement does not override a newer authoritative result. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.04.05 — Transport & Application Acknowledgement Distinction **[NEW C014]**
Track transport receipt, application acceptance and authoritative information commitment as separately interpretable outcomes.

- **`E03.04.05.F01` — ReceiptNotProcessing:** Transport receipt never alone asserts successful application processing. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.05.F02` — ApplicationOutcomeSeparatelyKnown:** Application acceptance or rejection can be distinguished from transport status. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.05.F03` — DurableCommitNotAssumed:** An application acknowledgement cannot be mistaken for committed storage absent an explicit contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.05.F04` — CorrelatedAcknowledgements:** All applicable acknowledgements correlate to the correct request, sender and exchange instance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.05.F05` — UncertainAcknowledgementQualified:** A lost acknowledgement produces a reconciliable uncertain state rather than a silent retry assumption. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-HIE-GATE, C-DOC-INGRESS, DRP-MN. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E03.04.06 — Exchange Replay & Duplicate Disposition **[NEW C014]**
Recognise repeated clinical submissions and replay attempts while retaining legitimate event-history semantics.

- **`E03.04.06.F01` — RepeatedExchangeCorrelated:** Replayed submissions correlate to their earlier accepted or attempted exchange identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.06.F02` — IdempotencePolicyApplied:** A repeated delivery does not create unintended duplicate authoritative results under the applicable contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.06.F03` — LegitimateRepeatDistinct:** A clinically new event is not rejected solely because its content resembles a previous event. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.06.F04` — ReplayOutcomeAuditable:** The disposition of replay, duplicate and corrected submissions can be reviewed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.06.F05` — RetryContractVisible:** Senders receive the applicable outcome rather than an ambiguous generic success. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-HIE-GATE, DRP-MN. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E03.04.07 — Delayed & Partial Exchange Outcome Reconciliation **[NEW C014]**
Resolve asynchronous and partly completed external exchanges, including failures after intermediate acknowledgements.

- **`E03.04.07.F01` — PendingExchangeVisible:** An exchange pending downstream processing remains identifiable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.07.F02` — StageSpecificFailure:** Transport, transform, authorisation, persistence and distribution failures are distinguishable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.07.F03` — RecoveryDoesNotLoseAcceptance:** Accepted clinical information is recoverable following the defined processing failure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.07.F04` — PartnerOutcomeCorrelated:** The ultimate outcome can be traced to its original partner submission. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.04.07.F05` — NoPrematureCompletion:** The exchange is not reported as completed while mandatory obligations remain outstanding. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-HIE-GATE, DRP-MN. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### E03.05 — Exchange Compatibility & Conformance
Validate interactions against applicable authoritative contracts and supported versions.

#### E03.05.01 — Versioned Contract Compatibility
Supported versions of exchange contracts behave as declared.

- **`E03.05.01.F01` — SupportedVersionConformance:** proportion of supported-version test suites passed. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.01.F02` — Supported Contract Version Resolution:** proportion of positive and negative versioned contract compatibility test cases meeting the applicable supported contract version resolution criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.01.F03` — Version Upgrade Compatibility:** proportion of independently checked versioned contract compatibility instances satisfying the specified version upgrade compatibility quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.01.F04` — Unsupported Version Explanation:** proportion of independently checked versioned contract compatibility instances satisfying the specified unsupported version explanation quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.01.F05` — Consumer Compatibility Evidence:** proportion of sampled versioned contract compatibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of consumer compatibility evidence. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.05.02 — Exchange Deviation Visibility
Material deviations from external contracts can be identified and reported.

- **`E03.05.02.F01` — ReportedContractDeviation:** proportion of injected/sampled deviations correctly detected and surfaced. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.02.F02` — Nonstandard Variation Detection:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed exchange deviation visibility scenarios demonstrating nonstandard variation detection against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.02.F03` — Source Deviation Attribution:** proportion of sampled exchange deviation visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of source deviation attribution. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.02.F04` — Deviation Impact Qualification:** proportion of independently checked exchange deviation visibility instances satisfying the specified deviation impact qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.02.F05` — Deviation Trend Reporting:** proportion of sampled exchange deviation visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of deviation trend reporting. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.05.03 — Exchange Conformance Evidence
Consumers can examine evidence that supported exchanges meet their obligations.

- **`E03.05.03.F01` — AvailableExchangeEvidence:** proportion of required conformance cases supported by reviewable evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.03.F02` — Repeatable Conformance Test:** proportion of positive and negative exchange conformance evidence test cases meeting the applicable repeatable conformance test criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.03.F03` — Contract Conformance Result:** proportion of positive and negative exchange conformance evidence test cases meeting the applicable contract conformance result criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.03.F04` — Failed Requirement Identification:** proportion of independently checked exchange conformance evidence instances satisfying the specified failed requirement identification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E03.05.03.F05` — Release Conformance Trace:** proportion of positive and negative exchange conformance evidence test cases meeting the applicable release conformance trace criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### E03.05.04 — Conformance Evidence for External Interfaces **[NEW C012]**
Demonstrate that external interfaces honour authoritative standards, declared deviations and compatible releases.

*Demand: B10.05, B11.05, B07.02; evidence: MODEL, NFR, EXCH; findings: R13.*

- **`E03.05.04.F01` — KnownDeviationRecorded:** Nonconforming external input has a retained deviation record. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.05.04.F02` — ConformanceSuiteRepeatable:** A release can rerun a bounded interface conformance suite. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.05.04.F03` — ExternalContractPrecedence:** Authoritative external contract is respected over internal preference. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.05.04.F04` — VersionCompatibilityReported:** Supported and unsupported contract versions are explicit. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.05.04.F05` — FailureEvidenceReproducible:** A rejected exchange can be recreated using protected representative evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.05.05 — External Deviation Qualification & Remediation **[NEW C014]**
Maintain accountable detection, classification and disposition of nonconformant incoming or outgoing healthcare exchanges.

- **`E03.05.05.F01` — DeviationTypeExplicit:** Nonconforming messages yield an interpretable conformance category. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.05.05.F02` — ToleratedDeviationQualified:** A tolerated deviation remains visible; adaptation does not silently redefine an external standard. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.05.05.F03` — RejectionReasonActionable:** The sender or authorised operator can distinguish correctable from nonrecoverable rejection. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.05.05.F04` — DeviationStatisticsReportable:** Conformance exceptions can be grouped by partner, message type and governing profile. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.05.05.F05` — ReferenceContractAuthority:** Any authorised local deviation remains explicitly subordinate to the authoritative external contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-DOC-INGRESS, C-HIE-GATE. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### E03.06 — Standards-Native Healthcare Interaction Behaviour **[NEW C015]**
Offer standards-native healthcare information exchange with explicitly selected, versioned interaction semantics, authoritative profile precedence, governed exceptions and repeatable evidence of conformance. This is an enterprise capability, independent of transport adapter or information-system ownership.

**Architecture placement:** E03. Existing E03.01–E03.05 retain their definitions. This adds explicit standards-native obligations rather than replacing general exchange capability. E04 continues to own broader semantic meaning and model governance. No capability is allocated to Pylai or another Solution Facet here.

**Business demand:** B04.03 Diagnostic Findings Reporting; B05.06 Community & Cross-Organisation Continuity; B07.02 Service Provider & Network Participation; B10.03 Appropriate Information Access & Sharing; B11.05 Regulatory Conformance & Accreditation. Additional relationships may be discovered; these are hypotheses.

**Evidence:** Governed NFR-052, NFR-088–091, NFR-148; source Continuum EC-08 cross-boundary protocols; existing E03.03/E03.04/E03.05 capability contracts; authoritative HL7 v2.x/FHIR documentation. Candidate contractual boundaries require review.

**Standards profiles:** The selected release, partner, trigger/operation, processing mode and deviations belong in a governed versioned Standards Interaction Profile / external contract—not as fabricated blanket requirements in the capability name. No standard version, complete resource list or unsupported optional interaction is selected here.

#### E03.06.01 — Supported Healthcare Standards Contract Declaration **[NEW C015]**
Select and publish the applicable standards, versions, implementation guides, message/interaction profiles, supported operations and effective rules for each interface; an implementation's supported subset must be unambiguous.

- **`E03.06.01.F01` — SupportedStandardDeclared:** Every enabled healthcare interface exposes an attributable, versioned statement of its supported standards and profiles. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.01.F02` — UnsupportedBehaviourExplicit:** Requests or messages outside the declared support envelope produce the applicable explicit rejection or qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.01.F03` — ProfileVersionResolved:** Each interaction is evaluated against the correct effective version and partner-specific contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.01.F04` — AuthoritativeContractPrecedence:** Where a published external standard governs an interaction, a local default cannot silently replace its normative meaning. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.01.F05` — ContractChangeTraceable:** New or revised standards/profile support has a reviewable release record and an identified effective boundary. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.06.02 — HL7 v2 Message Interpretation & Structural Validation **[NEW C015]**
Interpret supported HL7 v2.x messages against their declared version, message type, trigger event and sender-specific profile, including structural validation and governed deviations.

- **`E03.06.02.F01` — VersionTriggerAndProfileResolved:** The correct message version, event/structure and effective sender profile are identified or explicitly rejected. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.02.F02` — EncodingAndSegmentInterpretation:** Applicable delimiters, escaping, repetitions, character encodings, segments and field constraints are interpreted under the selected profile. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.02.F03` — StructuralDeviationReported:** Unexpected, missing or malformed structures produce attributable conformance findings rather than silent normalisation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.02.F04` — SenderSpecificToleranceGoverned:** A configurable tolerated deviation is accepted only when meaning remains reliably interpretable; it stays classified as nonconformant. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.02.F05` — NativeMessageSemanticsPreserved:** Acceptance, mapping and retention preserve the original message's meaningful content, identifier references and source provenance under applicable policy. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.06.03 — HL7 v2 Transport & Acknowledgement Choreography **[NEW C015]**
Provide declared HL7 v2.x message transport and acknowledgement behaviour, including MLLP framing and original/enhanced acknowledgement choreography where applicable, without conflating receipt and application outcome.

- **`E03.06.03.F01` — MLLPFramingAndPeerHandling:** Supported MLLP message framing, boundaries and connection lifecycle are validated against normal and malformed peer cases. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.03.F02` — ConnectionTimeoutsConfigurable:** Connection, I/O and idle timeouts are configurable and verified where applicable without stalling unrelated peers. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.03.F03` — OriginalEnhancedACKConformant:** The chosen v2 version/profile governs original or enhanced acknowledgement obligations and timing, including relevant MSH-15/MSH-16 semantics. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.03.F04` — AcknowledgementIdentityAndErrors:** Returned HL7 acknowledgements carry the correct correlation identifiers and applicable accept/error/reject detail under the selected contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.03.F05` — AcceptanceDoesNotOverclaim:** No acknowledgement asserts more acceptance, durability or application processing than the actual declared contract and achieved state warrant. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.06.04 — FHIR RESTful Server Interaction Support **[NEW C015]**
Expose a FHIR RESTful service whose declared FHIR release, supported resource types, interactions and operations control actual request/response behaviour; this does not mandate all optional FHIR interactions.

- **`E03.06.04.F01` — FHIRCapabilitiesReflectBehaviour:** The supported FHIR API publishes an accurate CapabilityStatement for its declared release and resource interactions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.04.F02` — FHIRReadWriteInteractionConformance:** Enabled instance/type/system interactions, such as read, create, update and delete, follow their applicable FHIR/HTTP rules. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.04.F03` — FHIRSearchAndPagingContract:** Declared search parameters, result bundles, paging and filtering produce standards-conformant outcomes for supported interactions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.04.F04` — FHIRConditionalHistoryVersionPolicy:** Declared conditional requests, version-specific read/history, version metadata and concurrency behaviour are honoured where supported. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.04.F05` — FHIRResponseAndFailureSemantics:** Enabled interactions return FHIR/HTTP status, headers and error representations, including OperationOutcome when prescribed, without claiming unperformed commitment. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.06.05 — FHIR RESTful Client Interaction Support **[NEW C015]**
Consume external FHIR RESTful interfaces according to their declared capabilities, governing implementation guides and current trust/authorisation context.

- **`E03.06.05.F01` — FHIRPeerCapabilityKnown:** A client can identify or explicitly configure the external FHIR release, capability profile and supported interaction subset. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.05.F02` — FHIRClientNegotiatedFormats:** Requests and responses use compatible media types, serialization and applicable profile expectations. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.05.F03` — FHIRClientSearchContinuation:** FHIR search continuation, paging and bundle correlation are correctly handled for supported queries. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.05.F04` — FHIRClientErrorAndRetryDiscipline:** Failures, conditional conflicts, throttling and uncertain responses follow the declared contract without unsafe blind repetition. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.05.F05` — FHIRClientIdentityAndDisclosure:** Outbound requests preserve identity, effective authority and governed disclosure obligations rather than assuming endpoint possession conveys permission. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.06.06 — FHIR Resource, Profile & Terminology Semantics **[NEW C015]**
Support FHIR resource exchange without conflating resource identity/version with the Harmonia Information Unit model, and validate against applicable profiles and terminology where required.

- **`E03.06.06.F01` — FHIRResourceProfileValidated:** Incoming and outgoing resources are assessed against declared FHIR release, StructureDefinitions and relevant implementation-guide constraints. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.06.F02` — FHIRTerminologyBindingsApplied:** Applicable code-system, value-set and terminology binding expectations are validated using the governed release. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.06.F03` — FHIRIdentityVersionBoundary:** FHIR resource logical identity and meta.versionId remain distinguishable from Information Unit identity and authoritative version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.06.F04` — FHIRReferenceAndBundleConsistency:** Supported reference resolution, bundles and resource relationships preserve semantics or explicitly report uncertainty and failure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.06.F05` — FHIRRepresentationMappingFidelity:** Mapping FHIR to/from governed information preserves native source, transformation provenance and known semantic loss/limitations. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.06.07 — Standards-Specific Conformance & Partner Deviation Governance **[NEW C015]**
Demonstrate that operational behaviour conforms to the chosen external-standard/profile contract, while documenting permitted local adaptation and nonconformant counterpart behaviour.

- **`E03.06.07.F01` — NormativeAndLocalRulesSeparated:** Each tested requirement is traceable to an external normative rule, a governed profile rule or an explicit qualified deviation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.07.F02` — PositiveNegativeConformanceRepeatable:** A versioned test suite covers permitted interactions, rejected interactions, error cases and recovery paths. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.07.F03` — PartnerDeviationAttributable:** A sender/partner-specific deviation and its effective tolerance are discoverable with cause and approval provenance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.07.F04` — ReleaseConformanceGate:** A solution release records test results and explicitly declares unsupported or unverified parts of the standards contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.07.F05` — SourceVariationsNotSilentlyCanonical:** Local interpretation or tolerant handling never rewrites the meaning or claimed conformance of a normative standard. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.06.08 — Additional Healthcare Standard Interaction Profiles **[NEW C015]**
Allow other approved healthcare exchange standards, including established DICOM and secure-messaging considerations, to acquire their own versioned interaction profiles without assuming universal or immediate support.

- **`E03.06.08.F01` — AdditionalStandardSupportExplicit:** DICOM, secure messaging or another standard becomes supported only with an explicit agreed release/contract scope. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.08.F02` — AdditionalProtocolNativeRules:** An enabled additional standard has its required interaction, transport and content rules bound to a specific version/profile. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.08.F03` — AdditionalStandardFailureOutcomes:** The standard-specific rejection, error, transport acknowledgement and recovery semantics are separately assessed where applicable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.08.F04` — CrossStandardMeaningPreserved:** Any conversion between standards retains identifiable source meaning, provenance and declared mapping limitations. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.06.08.F05` — NoImplicitImplementationCommitment:** Mention of a possible standard does not imply that an adapter, endpoint, licence or full feature set already exists. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Clarification:* A supported standards profile is not itself a claim that a particular implementation conforms. Each relevant release and interface must produce and preserve its own conformance evidence.


### E03.07 — Governed Bulk Data Interconnect **[NEW C017]**
Provide an enterprise-scale, governed mechanism for the exchange of large **populations of information**, including bulk exports, applicable inbound bulk submissions and contractually supported ongoing delivery, without equating bulk transfer to large-media object management or to unrestricted database access. Domain owners retain information selection, semantics, source authority, validation and acceptance; the interconnect provides reusable exchange contracts and controlled transfer capabilities.

**Architecture placement:** Enterprise Capability `E03 — Digital Interoperability & Service Interaction`. The specialised `E08.08 — Evidence Collation, Export & Controlled Release` retains its assurance/evidence responsibility, and `E01.06 — Governed Binary & Large-Media Content Management` retains its media responsibility. E03.07 is general infrastructure-neutral enterprise capability, **not** a declaration of a new deployed subsystem, a distributed analytics engine, or a raw bulk endpoint directly into Mnemosyne.

**Governed evidence:** `enterprise-architecture/design/offline-design/DRP-Logging-AuditEvent-Provenance-Governance-and-Export.md` §§4.1–4.4, 5, 7 (non-dereferenced bulk/on-demand/time-bounded export of LogEvent/AuditEvent/Provenance; specialised Kleio/Dokimasia dereferenced export; authorised release). `DesignNote-Pylai-External-Interoperability-Architecture.md` (Pylai external boundary and Terma protocol behaviour). Existing NFR-087 applies to large individual objects but **does not substitute for bulk population exchange**. Candidate 016 media capability is preserved unchanged.

**Business enablement hypotheses:** Information Stewardship `B10`, cross-organisation sharing/HIE, operational assurance, authorised research/analytics, plus permitted administrative export or migration. These are candidate relationships, not a new authorisation to disclose all information or a commitment to implement an external analytics platform.

**Separation of responsibility:** Domain service + Mneme/Mnemosyne or other governed persistence service defines and supplies eligible records; Sylloge resolves binary content only where required; Pylai is the existing external interoperability boundary; Themis governs access and disclosure; Kleio/Dokimasia specialise evidence export. The candidate does not make an interconnect service the owner of every source record or prescribe an information-system implementation allocation.

**Release qualification:** The DRP establishes non-dereferenced bulk export for evidence-related record domains. Full generic bidirectional imports, continuous feeds and a particular bulk interface standard are *candidate expansions requiring separate R1.x/R2.x scoping*. FHIR Bulk Data (if selected) would be a specific external contract, not an automatic synonym for this general capability. Continuous streaming is not silently mandated.

**Important DRP constraints:** The assurance DRP requires dereferenced AuditEvent/Provenance exports to represent a specified historical point in time, remain interpretable if references later become unavailable, and fail without releasing partial information if any required underlying access is unauthorised. Those domain-specific invariants remain intact and are **not broadened to an unsupported universal all-or-nothing rule for every bulk transfer**.

#### E03.07.01 — Governed Bulk Exchange Contract & Scope **[NEW C017]**
Declare the content domain, provider/consumer roles, permitted modes, contractual rules, selection boundaries and applicable release support before any bulk exchange is undertaken.

- **`E03.07.01.F01` — SupportedScopeDeclared:** Every enabled bulk exchange identifies its source domain, eligible information classes and supported transfer direction. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.01.F02` — ProviderConsumerRoleKnown:** Each exchange contract identifies which participant supplies, receives and remains authoritative for the data. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.01.F03` — SelectionAuthorityPreserved:** An exchange request cannot expand a data selection beyond the scope authorised by its owning information domain. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.01.F04` — ModeSupportExplicit:** The contract states whether bulk export, bulk import, recurring delivery or incremental updates are supported; unsupported modes are rejected. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.01.F05` — ContractVersionTraceable:** A changed bulk interface contract is versioned, reviewed and tested against its declared consumers. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
