#### E03.07.02 — Governed Dataset Selection & Version Context **[NEW C017]**
Accept domain-authorised selections of records, versions and time windows; establish interpretable membership and consistency context without becoming an unrestricted information search facility.

- **`E03.07.02.F01` — DomainSelectionRetained:** The interconnect operates on a selection supplied or approved by the accountable information-domain service. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.02.F02` — VersionScopeDeclared:** Where selected information is versioned, the delivered record versions or version-selection rule are identified. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.02.F03` — TemporalBasisExplicit:** A time-bounded extract declares the applicable occurrence, creation, persistence or effective-time basis. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.02.F04` — SnapshotConsistencyQualified:** Where a historical consistency point is required, the resulting package identifies its consistency basis and exceptions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.02.F05` — OutOfScopeRecordsExcluded:** Tests verify that unrelated or unauthorised domain records cannot enter the exchange merely because they are technically discoverable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.07.03 — Bulk Extraction & Governed Ingestion **[NEW C017]**
Support governed extraction and submission of authorised information populations, with domain services retaining record interpretation, validation, authoritative acceptance and persistence responsibility.

- **`E03.07.03.F01` — BulkExtractionComplete:** An authorised export contains the declared eligible record population or reports a verifiable incomplete outcome. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.03.F02` — InboundValidationDelegated:** Inbound bulk content is evaluated by the applicable domain service and is not declared accepted solely upon transport receipt. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.03.F03` — ReferenceSemanticsPreserved:** Reference-valued data is preserved as such unless the owning export contract explicitly requires and authorises dereferencing. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.03.F04` — RecordIdentityPreserved:** Transferred records retain the source identities, source references and version markers required by the exchange contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.03.F05` — DomainAcceptanceDistinguished:** Per-domain acceptance, durable commitment and exchange completion are individually reported rather than conflated. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.07.04 — Bulk Package Manifest & Interpretable Representation **[NEW C017]**
Describe what a bulk exchange contains and how a recipient can independently identify, validate and interpret the delivered records and references.

- **`E03.07.04.F01` — PackageManifestAvailable:** Each completed bulk package identifies contract version, content scope and the constituent parts or accessible references. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.04.F02` — RepresentationDeclared:** Each part has a declared format and interpretation basis, including applicable standards profiles where used. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.04.F03` — IntegrityEvidenceAvailable:** Receivers can identify corruption, missing parts or unexpected modifications using applicable integrity metadata. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.04.F04` — PopulationCompletenessQualified:** The package reports expected and actual population/part counts or equivalent verifiable completeness evidence where applicable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.04.F05` — UnresolvedReferenceStatusVisible:** Where references cannot be resolved or were intentionally not dereferenced, the package preserves that fact without implying absent evidence was supplied. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.07.05 — Bulk Transfer Modes & External Endpoint Integration **[NEW C017]**
Provide an extensible external exchange boundary for large datasets using applicable push, pull, file, object-reference or other contractually selected patterns without coupling to one transport product.

- **`E03.07.05.F01` — PushPullModesContractual:** Configured interfaces distinguish requester-initiated retrieval from producer-initiated delivery where supported. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.05.F02` — LargeTransferFlowControlled:** Large transfers employ bounded resource use and controlled pressure rather than consuming unbounded memory or obstructing unrelated service traffic. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.05.F03` — EndpointTrustValidated:** The external endpoint identity, permitted destination and applicable transport protections are validated before exchange. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.05.F04` — ProtocolBehaviourBounded:** Transport acknowledgements, session results and final package outcomes are distinguished for each supported endpoint pattern. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.05.F05` — TransportProductReplaceable:** The information contract and outcome semantics remain intelligible if the external transfer mechanism changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.07.06 — Asynchronous Bulk Exchange Lifecycle & Recovery **[NEW C017]**
Manage the progress and exceptional outcomes of long-running bulk exchanges and support bounded recovery, restart or reconciliation according to each contract.

- **`E03.07.06.F01` — ExchangeStateObservable:** An authorised operator or requester can distinguish pending, preparing, transferring, completed, failed and indeterminate exchanges as applicable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.06.F02` — CheckpointRecoveryGoverned:** An interrupted exchange can resume or restart only within recorded, contractually permitted recovery semantics. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.06.F03` — DuplicateEffectsControlled:** Resubmission or replay is identified and handled without silently duplicating domain effects. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.06.F04` — BackpressureAndAdmissionControlled:** Overcapacity exchanges are delayed or rejected with explicit status while accepted obligations remain tracked. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.06.F05` — UncertainOutcomeRetained:** Network or recipient ambiguity is recorded as indeterminate until supported reconciliation resolves it. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.07.07 — Bulk Disclosure & Access Enforcement **[NEW C017]**
Apply authorisation, information-protection and accountable disclosure to the whole exchange and to the underlying information without creating a privileged bulk-access bypass.

- **`E03.07.07.F01` — ThemisAuthorisationApplied:** Source selection, retrieval and disclosure use applicable Themis policy enforcement and the local PEP at each controlling boundary. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.07.F02` — NoImplicitBulkPrivilege:** Possession of a bulk interconnect operation does not grant access to constituent information or across compartments. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.07.F03` — RecipientAndPurposeBounded:** Where required by policy, destination identity, use/purpose and disclosure constraints are verified prior to release. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.07.F04` — RequiredAtomicReleaseEnforced:** Where an owning export contract requires all-or-nothing release, incomplete or unauthorised packages cannot be disclosed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.07.F05` — RevocationAndFailureProtected:** A relevant authorisation failure, revocation or cancelled exchange is handled without uncontrolled release of staged information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E03.07.08 — Bulk Exchange Reconciliation, Evidence & Reuse **[NEW C017]**
Provide independent transfer evidence and reusable outcomes while preserving the separation between transport facts, information authority and domain-specific audit or assurance meaning.

- **`E03.07.08.F01` — EndToEndTransferAccountable:** The exchange records attributable origin, destination, declared scope and outcome without conflating them with clinical or assurance conclusions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.08.F02` — PartLevelReconciliationAvailable:** Where supported, source and recipient can reconcile missing, rejected, accepted and indeterminate package parts. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.08.F03` — DomainEvidenceResponsibilitiesIntact:** Kleio, Dokimasia and other owning domains retain authority over their own export interpretation and specialised evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.08.F04` — NoExternalAnalyticsDependency:** A deployment can operate without an external data lake or analytics platform unless an explicit solution contract requires one. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E03.07.08.F05` — MultipleDomainReuseDemonstrated:** Conformance tests show the same interconnect pattern can serve at least two independently governed record domains without duplicating the transport framework. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

## E04 — Semantic Meaning & Model Governance
The system can preserve, interpret, transform and evolve the meaning of managed information.

### E04.01 — Information Model Definition & Resolution
Publish and use controlled definitions of information.

#### E04.01.01 — Governed Information Model Publication
Consumers can obtain the authoritative definitions applicable to governed information.

- **`E04.01.01.F01` — AvailableModelDefinition:** proportion of valid model/version requests resolved correctly. *PROPOSED*.

### E04.03 — Semantic Transformation
Translate supported representations without hiding material meaning loss.

#### E04.03.01 — Governed Representation Translation
Information can be translated among supported representations without unreported meaning loss.

- **`E04.03.01.F01` — SemanticallyConformantTranslation:** proportion of tested mappings preserving required meaning. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E04.03.01.F02` — Meaning Preservation Evidence:** proportion of sampled governed representation translation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of meaning preservation evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E04.03.01.F03` — Translation Rule Version:** proportion of independently checked governed representation translation instances satisfying the specified translation rule version quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E04.03.01.F04` — Qualified Translation Loss:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed governed representation translation scenarios demonstrating qualified translation loss against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E04.03.01.F05` — Repeatable Translation Outcome:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in governed representation translation for which repeatable translation outcome is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E04.03.02 — Transparent Transformation Qualifications
Consumers can discover significant limitations or loss during translation.

- **`E04.03.02.F01` — VisibleTranslationQualifications:** proportion of expected qualifications identified and conveyed. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E04.03.02.F02` — Transformation Limitation Disclosure:** proportion of sampled transparent transformation qualifications cases with accessible, accurate evidence of transformation limitation disclosure. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E04.03.02.F03` — Source-to-Target Traceability:** proportion of independently checked transparent transformation qualifications instances satisfying the specified source-to-target traceability quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E04.03.02.F04` — Unsupported Mapping Recognition:** proportion of sampled transparent transformation qualifications cases with accessible, accurate evidence of unsupported mapping recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E04.03.02.F05` — Qualification Consumer Visibility:** proportion of independently checked transparent transformation qualifications instances satisfying the specified qualification consumer visibility quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### E04.03.03 — Loss-Aware Semantic Transformation **[NEW C012]**
Transform representations while exposing provenance, semantic degradation and incompatibility.

*Demand: B02.01, B04.03, B10.05; evidence: MODEL, IU, EXCH; findings: R09, R13.*

- **`E04.03.03.F01` — UnmappedConceptsReported:** No unrepresentable source semantics vanish without qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E04.03.03.F02` — TransformationVersionPinned:** Mapping rules and versions are recoverable for every governed derivative. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E04.03.03.F03` — MeaningEquivalentWhereClaimed:** Equivalence claims pass reference examples and adverse tests. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E04.03.03.F04` — OriginalRepresentationRetrievable:** Original preserved information remains referenceable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E04.03.03.F05` — TransformationProvenanceLinked:** Output IU is linked to source IU and transform evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E04.03.04 — Identity-Preserving Clinical Translation **[NEW C014]**
Preserve subject/entity references, uncertainty and source semantics through standards conversion and canonical information mapping.

- **`E04.03.04.F01` — SourceIdentifierProvenance:** A translated identifier retains its source/issuer semantics. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E04.03.04.F02` — NoInventedPatientMatch:** Transformation does not create an authoritative patient association without identity evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E04.03.04.F03` — LossQualificationReported:** Untranslatable clinically meaningful or security-related semantics are reported or explicitly qualified. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E04.03.04.F04` — NativeRepresentationTraceable:** The source-native representation remains attributable and retrievable under applicable governance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E04.03.04.F05` — TranslationVersionReproducible:** A translated output can be attributed to the model/terminology release used. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: DRP-IU, C-DOC-INGRESS. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.

Evaluate model, terminology and contextual invariants without confusing semantic validity with clinical judgement.

- **`E04.04.04.F01` — ContextRulesVersioned:** Rule results cite the applicable semantic release. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

- **`E04.04.04.F05` — ValidationEvidenceRepeatable:** Running the same rules against an unchanged snapshot repeats the outcome. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

## E05 — Identity, Trust & Information Protection
The system can establish, propagate and enforce appropriate digital trust and access boundaries.

### E05.01 — Digital Identity & Authentication
Establish the authenticated identity of an interacting principal.

#### E05.01.01 — Authenticated Digital Interaction
The system can establish authenticated identity at a required access boundary.

- **`E05.01.01.F01` — AuthenticationOutcomeCorrectness:** proportion of test interactions correctly authenticated or denied. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.01.F02` — Verified Principal Authentication:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed authenticated digital interaction scenarios demonstrating verified principal authentication against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.01.F03` — Authentication Failure Disclosure:** proportion of sampled authenticated digital interaction cases with accessible, accurate evidence of authentication failure disclosure. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.01.F04` — Authentication Session Validity:** proportion of independently checked authenticated digital interaction instances satisfying the specified authentication session validity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.01.F05` — Restricted Anonymous Access:** proportion of applicable authenticated digital interaction requests or situations with verified restricted anonymous access. *PROPOSED*.

#### E05.01.02 — Trusted System Interaction
Systems and automated principals can authenticate to the services they are authorised to use.

- **`E05.01.02.F01` — VerifiedSystemIdentity:** proportion of tested machine interactions meeting required mutual trust conditions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.02.F02` — Peer Trust Evidence:** proportion of sampled trusted system interaction cases with accessible, accurate evidence of peer trust evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.02.F03` — Mutual Identity Confirmation:** proportion of independently checked trusted system interaction instances satisfying the specified mutual identity confirmation quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.02.F04` — Expired Trust Rejection:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in trusted system interaction for which expired trust rejection is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.02.F05` — System Trust Rotation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed trusted system interaction scenarios demonstrating system trust rotation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E05.01.03 — Authentication Evidence Availability
Required authentication outcomes are attributable for subsequent oversight.

- **`E05.01.03.F01` — ReviewableAuthenticationEvidence:** proportion of in-scope outcomes with complete required evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.03.F02` — Authenticated Action Attribution:** proportion of sampled authentication evidence availability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of authenticated action attribution. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.03.F03` — Evidence Integrity:** proportion of independently checked authentication evidence availability instances satisfying the specified evidence integrity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.03.F04` — Evidence Retrieval Scope:** proportion of positive and negative authentication evidence availability test cases meeting the applicable evidence retrieval scope criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.01.03.F05` — Evidence Retention Status:** proportion of sampled authentication evidence availability cases with accessible, accurate evidence of evidence retention status. *PROPOSED*.

#### E05.01.04 — Machine-to-Machine Trust Continuity **[NEW C012]**
Authenticate services and workloads without conflating workload identity with human business authority.

*Demand: B07.02, B12.04, B10.03; evidence: TH, NFR; findings: R02, R22.*

- **`E05.01.04.F01` — WorkloadIdentityBound:** Calls carry verifiable workload identity independent of user identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.01.04.F02` — DelegatedAuthorityExplicit:** Machine identity alone does not imply human purpose-scoped permission. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.01.04.F03` — CredentialRotationContinuity:** Credential rollover does not require ungoverned anonymous fallback. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.01.04.F04` — ExpiredTrustRejected:** Old or revoked technical identities fail protected interactions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.01.04.F05` — TrustEventAccountable:** Trust failures carry suitable diagnostic/audit correlation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E05.02 — Contextual Authorisation Determination
Determine whether an action is permitted within its applicable context.

#### E05.02.01 — Context-Sensitive Access Decision
A requested action can be assessed against governing policies and circumstances.

- **`E05.02.01.F01` — CorrectContextualDecision:** proportion of authorisation test cases producing correct decisions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.02.01.F02` — Action and Compartment Evaluation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed context-sensitive access decision scenarios demonstrating action and compartment evaluation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.02.01.F03` — Context Snapshot Validity:** proportion of independently checked context-sensitive access decision instances satisfying the specified context snapshot validity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.02.01.F04` — Denial Reason Qualification:** proportion of independently checked context-sensitive access decision instances satisfying the specified denial reason qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.02.01.F05` — Determination Repeatability:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in context-sensitive access decision for which determination repeatability is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E05.02.02 — Purpose-Scoped Permission
Information access can be constrained to the authorised purpose or compartment.

- **`E05.02.02.F01` — CorrectPurposeAndCompartmentDecision:** proportion of tested purpose/compartment cases correctly determined. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.02.02.F02` — Purpose Requirement Evaluation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed purpose-scoped permission scenarios demonstrating purpose requirement evaluation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.02.02.F03` — Purpose Mismatch Rejection:** proportion of independently checked purpose-scoped permission instances satisfying the specified purpose mismatch rejection quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.02.02.F04` — Purpose Change Recognition:** proportion of sampled purpose-scoped permission cases with accessible, accurate evidence of purpose change recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E05.02.02.F05` — Granted Scope Clarity:** proportion of positive and negative purpose-scoped permission test cases meeting the applicable granted scope clarity criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### E05.02.03 — Subject-Specific Required Criteria **[NEW C012]**
Evaluate protected-subject requirements separately from the effective authority presented by the requester.

*Demand: B10.03, B10.04, B14.06; evidence: TH, IU, REF; findings: R22.*

- **`E05.02.03.F01` — CriteriaProviderEncapsulated:** Evaluation does not require consumers to parse protected-object internals. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.02.03.F02` — ActionMaskIndependent:** Action limitations are applied independently of compartment restrictions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.02.03.F03` — CompartmentMaskCorrect:** Classification/compartment conditions alter outcome as defined. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.02.03.F04` — TimeContextApplied:** Temporal and contextual changes can revoke permission to an unchanged subject. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.02.03.F05` — DeniedCriteriaExplainedSafely:** Rejection exposes safe reason classes without protected criteria leakage. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

Prohibited requests are rejected without unintended side effects.

### E05.07 — Health Entity Identity & Association **[NEW C014]**

#### E05.07.01 — Represented Entity Identity Distinction **[NEW C014]**

#### E05.07.04 — Uncertain Identity Association Review **[NEW C014]**

- **`E05.07.06.F02` — RetiredIdentifierResolvable:** A retired identifier can yield a governed disposition rather than an unexplained missing record. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.06.F03` — SourceSystemChangeSafe:** Replaced or migrated source systems do not silently break a recorded entity association. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.06.F04` — ProvenancePreservingReidentification:** Reidentification or correction preserves source evidence and the responsible actor. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.06.F05` — CrossDomainIdentityConsistency:** Permitted references to the same entity do not disagree without an explicit qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: DRP-IU, C-PERSON-RESOLVE. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E05.07.07 — Enterprise Person Identity Authority **[NEW C014]**

- **`E05.07.07.F02` — MasterAssertionTraceability:** A master identity assertion is supported by identifiable source evidence and decisions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.07.F03` — ConflictNotSilentlyResolved:** Competing enterprise identity determinations remain visible until the applicable resolution occurs. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.07.F04` — GovernedMasterIdentityChange:** Authoritative master identity change produces an accountable, reviewable lifecycle event. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.07.F05` — CrossSystemImpactQualified:** Consumers can distinguish propagating an identity update from proving that every affected record was corrected. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FULL_EMPI_GOVERNANCE; evidence keys: C-PERSON-CORR, C-PERSON-CORRECT, DRP-IU. *Release horizon:* R3+ EMPI service objective.


#### E05.07.08 — Digital Twin Referent Association **[NEW C014]**
Preserve the association between a Digital Twin and the real-world entity it models without equating the Twin with that entity or its current fulfiller.

- **`E05.07.08.F01` — TwinReferentDistinction:** A task carried by a Twin does not imply that the Twin itself physically fulfils it. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.08.F02` — ReferentAssociationStable:** The intended physical referent remains identifiable across Twin state changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.08.F03` — CorrectedAssociationGoverned:** A misidentified Twin–entity association has an accountable correction route. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.08.F04` — AuthorityDoesNotInheritBlindly:** Twin participation never automatically confers every right or privilege of the represented entity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E05.07.08.F05` — MultipleTwinContextQualified:** Multiple possible representations or contexts for one entity are distinguishable and reviewable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* AGREED_DIRECTION_WITH_INFERRED_CAPABILITY_DETAIL; evidence keys: DRP-PR, C-CT2-ACT. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.



## E06 — Digital Work, Events & Temporal Coordination
The system can carry forward and account for work, events and time-dependent execution.

### E06.01 — Task Representation & State Management
Represent actionable work, its state and resulting outcomes.

#### E06.01.01 — Actionable Work Representation
Work can be described with sufficient meaning and state for governed handling.

- **`E06.01.01.F01` — CompleteActionableWorkContext:** proportion of accepted work items with required task context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.01.F02` — Defined Work Objective:** proportion of assessed actionable work representation cases with observable defined work objective against an agreed evaluation basis. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.01.F03` — Valid Work State:** proportion of independently checked actionable work representation instances satisfying the specified valid work state quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.01.F04` — Assigned Work Responsibility:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed actionable work representation scenarios demonstrating assigned work responsibility against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.01.F05` — Actionable Work Eligibility:** proportion of positive and negative actionable work representation test cases meeting the applicable actionable work eligibility criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### E06.01.02 — Work Outcome Recording
The outcome of a work unit can be obtained and interpreted.

- **`E06.01.02.F01` — AttributableWorkOutcome:** proportion of completed work units with valid result/outcome reference. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.02.F02` — Explicit Work Completion Result:** proportion of applicable work outcome recording requests or situations with verified explicit work completion result. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.02.F03` — Outcome Version Association:** proportion of independently checked work outcome recording instances satisfying the specified outcome version association quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.02.F04` — Failed Work Disposition:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed work outcome recording scenarios demonstrating failed work disposition against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.02.F05` — Outcome Provenance:** proportion of sampled work outcome recording cases with accessible, accurate evidence of outcome provenance. *PROPOSED*.

#### E06.01.03 — Work Lifecycle Visibility
Consumers can determine the current governed state of work.

- **`E06.01.03.F01` — CorrectWorkState:** proportion of sampled work units with reconciled state. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.03.F02` — Work State Transition Trace:** proportion of sampled work lifecycle visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of work state transition trace. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.03.F03` — In-Flight Work Discoverability:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed work lifecycle visibility scenarios demonstrating in-flight work discoverability against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.03.F04` — Work Ownership Visibility:** proportion of sampled work lifecycle visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of work ownership visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.01.03.F05` — Abnormal Work State Flagging:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed work lifecycle visibility scenarios demonstrating abnormal work state flagging against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E06.01.04 — Task Obligation & Outcome Semantics **[NEW C012]**
Represent actionable task, fulfillment, reporting and outcome relationships without using transport status as task completion.

**Adjudicated FC-001 clarification:** `ActionableTask` names the abstract identifiable work obligation, and `FulfillmentTask` names an abstract identifiable undertaking. In an actionable-work context, `Pragma` is the common governed Harmonia representation from which a `Contextualiser` may produce **zero or more distinct, recipient-specific `Analipsi`**. Each `Analipsi` realises one undertaking (`FulfillmentTask`), contains its **own contextualised `Pragmaton`** and is presented for processing by its intended Ergon or equivalent recipient. Its Pragmaton state is specific to that Analipsi, **not cumulative state shared by all realisations** of the source Pragma. Each Analipsi supplies distinct identity and correlation context for progression, outputs, AuditEvent, provenance and independent assurance. Independent undertakings of a Pragma may be concurrent; their contribution to `TaskOutcome` remains governed by the parent work obligation's existing concurrency and outcome rules. This statement does not equate every kind of Pragma with an ActionableTask, and does not equate an Analipsi with a technical Ergon invocation or retry.

*Demand: B05.01, B07.04, B11.02; evidence: PRAGMA, TASK; findings: R07, R18.*

- **`E06.01.04.F01` — TaskTypeDistinguished:** Actionable, fulfillment and reported task representations remain distinguishable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.01.04.F02` — OutcomeModeApplied:** Aggregate/collection/derived outcomes follow the designated contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.01.04.F03` — CompletionPolicyApplied:** FirstToFinish, AssignedToFinish and Aggregate behave as specified. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.01.04.F04` — TaskIdentityPreserved:** Task lineage survives restart and repartitioning. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.01.04.F05` — UndeliveredNotComplete:** Queued or accepted transport never alone indicates task fulfillment. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E06.02 — Workflow & Work Progression
Coordinate work progression across actors and components.

#### E06.02.01 — Coordinated Work Progression
Related work can progress coherently across participating systems.

- **`E06.02.01.F01` — CorrectWorkflowProgression:** proportion of conformance journeys reaching correct permitted state. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.01.F02` — Required Step Ordering:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed coordinated work progression scenarios demonstrating required step ordering against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.01.F03` — Dependency-Aware Advancement:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed coordinated work progression scenarios demonstrating dependency-aware advancement against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.01.F04` — Correct Branch Progression:** proportion of independently checked coordinated work progression instances satisfying the specified correct branch progression quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.01.F05` — Workflow Completion Visibility:** proportion of sampled coordinated work progression cases with accessible, accurate evidence of workflow completion visibility. *PROPOSED*.

#### E06.02.02 — Individual Work Execution
A bounded work unit can be processed and reported distinctly from its wider workflow. Processing of an individual `Analipsi` uses that realisation's instance-specific `Pragmaton`, progression and evidence correlation; downstream independent recipients receive their own Analipsi derived from the applicable Pragma rather than inheriting an undifferentiated, cumulatively mutable execution context.

- **`E06.02.02.F01` — IsolatedWorkOutcome:** proportion of tested individual tasks yielding correct, independently accountable outcomes. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.02.F02` — Scoped Task Context:** proportion of positive and negative individual work execution test cases meeting the applicable scoped task context criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.02.F03` — Single-Task Completion Evidence:** proportion of sampled individual work execution cases with accessible, accurate evidence of single-task completion evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.02.F04` — Task Failure Qualification:** proportion of independently checked individual work execution instances satisfying the specified task failure qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.02.F05` — Recoverable Task Interruption:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in individual work execution for which recoverable task interruption is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E06.02.03 — Digital Twin Work Progression
Work concerning a governed digital twin can progress in its required ordering/context.

- **`E06.02.03.F01` — TwinWorkOrderingConformance:** proportion of prescribed Twin tests satisfying ordering and state conditions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.03.F02` — Twin-Scoped Work Sequence:** proportion of positive and negative digital twin work progression test cases meeting the applicable twin-scoped work sequence criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.03.F03` — Twin Event Context:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed digital twin work progression scenarios demonstrating twin event context against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.03.F04` — Twin-Work Isolation:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing digital twin work progression scenarios with correct twin-work isolation, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.02.03.F05` — Twin Progress Recovery:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in digital twin work progression for which twin progress recovery is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E06.02.04 — Non-Digital-Twin Workflow Progression **[NEW C012]**
Coordinate and observe non-Twin workflow obligations without equating them to individual work execution or parcel routing.

*Demand: B05.01, B07.04, B11.02; evidence: TASK, WORK, PRAGMA; findings: R08.*

- **`E06.02.04.F01` — WorkflowStepOrderPreserved:** Required predecessor and successor constraints are observed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.02.04.F02` — NonTwinWorkRecognised:** Non-Twin workflow can be expressed without fake Twin identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.02.04.F03` — FailureStatePreserved:** Unfinished workflow state remains inspectable across execution loss. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.02.04.F04` — HumanActionGateRespected:** Required clinical or administrative human decision cannot be silently automated. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.02.04.F05` — WorkflowCompletionEvidence:** The declared completion condition is evidenced independently of transport receipt. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.02.05 — Digital Twin Workflow Coordination **[NEW C012]**
Coordinate Twin-scoped work while preserving Twin-local ownership of execution and ordered activity.

*Demand: B03.03, B05.01, B07.04; evidence: TASK, PRAGMA, WORK; findings: R08.*

- **`E06.02.05.F01` — TwinQueueIsolation:** One Twin cannot consume another Twin work queue in conflict with governing scope. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.02.05.F02` — TwinOrderMaintained:** Ordered Twin actions obey selected sequencing policy. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.02.05.F03` — TwinFailureVisible:** Unavailable Twin state is not reported as finished work. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.02.05.F04` — TwinWorkRecoverable:** Accepted obligations can be reconstructed after Twin-host failure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.02.05.F05` — CrossTwinCorrelation:** Multi-Twin interactions remain correlated without falsely merging their identities. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

Direct governed work/event parcels to the appropriate destination.

- **`E06.03.03.F03` — UnavailableDestinationHandled:** No valid route produces an explicit exception, not disappearance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

Consumers can distinguish accepted, pending, delivered, failed and dispositioned delivery states.

Provide a consistent scheduling/calendar context and temporal evaluation for distributed work without component-local competing calendars.

Contextual references used during work remain meaningful at continuation.

- **`E06.06.03.F01` — ValidWorkContextReferences:** proportion of restored work contexts with valid in-scope references. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.06.03.F02` — Referenced Information Version Pinning:** proportion of independently checked preserved work reference context instances satisfying the specified referenced information version pinning quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.06.03.F03` — Governed Work Context Reuse:** proportion of positive and negative preserved work reference context test cases meeting the applicable governed work context reuse criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E06.06.03.F04` — Broken Work Reference Detection:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed preserved work reference context scenarios demonstrating broken work reference detection against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E06.06.04 — Safe Work Reconstruction Boundary **[NEW C012]**
Reconstruct missing active working context from durable references and accepted progress, never resuming in a failed Ergon.

*Demand: B07.04, B12.04, B10.01; evidence: RES-W, WORK, PRAGMATON; findings: R07, R24.*

- **`E06.06.04.F01` — NoMidErgonResume:** Post-failure execution begins at a governed restart boundary. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
