- **`E06.06.04.F03` — LostCacheNotLostWork:** Active cache failure does not erase accepted obligations. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.06.04.F04` — RecoveredInputsVersioned:** Reconstituted input references identify their intended versions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.06.04.F05` — RestartExceptionVisible:** Unreconstructable context is reported with correlated failure evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.06.05 — Pragmaton Context Integrity **[NEW C012]**
Establish and maintain bounded working information without allowing active state to masquerade as authoritative information.

*Demand: B07.04, B10.01; evidence: PRAGMATON, WORK, MN; findings: R06, R07.*

- **`E06.06.05.F01` — ContextScopeBounded:** An Ergon receives only its expected and explicitly authorised ad-hoc working context. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.06.05.F02` — WorkingStateNotAuthoritative:** Intermediate mutation cannot be mistaken for durable version progression. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.06.05.F03` — OutputNewVersusChanged:** Genuinely new outputs remain distinct from changes to loaded inputs. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.06.05.F04` — ContextAuthorityPreserved:** Requester and executor authority and information labels survive contextualisation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.06.05.F05` — ContextReleaseAccountable:** Required context disposal does not silently discard accepted authoritative information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E06.07 — Work Acceptance & Outcome Reconciliation
Distinguish work acceptance, execution outcome, durable information commitment and the reconciliation of uncertain transitions.

**New Candidate 012 CT2 — proposed for Solution Architect review.**

#### E06.07.01 — Durable Work Acceptance **[NEW C012]**
Accept externally or internally submitted work with a durable obligation independent of Information Unit commitment.

*Demand: B07.04, B11.02, B12.04; evidence: PARCEL, PRAGMA, NFR; findings: R06, R07, R21.*

- **`E06.07.01.F01` — AcceptedWorkRecordedBeforeAck:** An acceptance acknowledgment corresponds to a recoverable work obligation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.01.F02` — RejectedWorkNotQueued:** Capacity or authority rejection leaves no silently accepted obligation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.01.F03` — DuplicateWorkCorrelated:** Retries correlate to the original accepted work identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.01.F04` — WorkAcceptanceNotIUCommit:** Acceptance status does not assert authoritative information version progression. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.01.F05` — PendingWorkSurvivesRestart:** Accepted but unfulfilled work reappears after planned or unplanned restart. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.07.02 — Execution Outcome Reconciliation **[NEW C012]**
Reconcile produced work outcomes with accepted work obligations and subsequent authoritative information commitment.

*Demand: B05.03, B07.04, B11.02; evidence: PRAGMA, MN, RES-W; findings: R06, R07.*

- **`E06.07.02.F01` — ExecutionResultNotCommit:** Completed Ergon outcome remains distinct from durable information acceptance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.02.F02` — OutcomeLineageComplete:** Task, input, execution and output correlation survives recovery. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.02.F03` — UnknownCommitReconcilable:** Indeterminate write can be resolved without reexecuting clinical business logic blindly. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.02.F04` — FailureBetweenStagesVisible:** A failed commit after successful processing is visible as incomplete disposition. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.02.F05` — NoDuplicateOutcomePromotion:** Replay does not create multiple accepted outcomes for a single governed obligation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.07.03 — Staged Change & Commit Coordination **[NEW C012]**
Support Retrieve, Check, Update, ProcessSomething and Commit as distinct service intentions with independently assessable outcomes.

*Demand: B10.07, B05.03, B11.03; evidence: MN, WORK, PERSIST; findings: R06, R07.*

- **`E06.07.03.F01` — RetrievalVersionKnown:** Work begins with a known governed source version where needed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.03.F02` — CheckDoesNotCommit:** Validation changes no authoritative state without an explicit authorised mutation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.03.F03` — UpdateRemainsProvisional:** Working-state updates do not imply durable commitment. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.03.F04` — ProcessProducesQualifiedOutcome:** Execution output is separately inspectable before commitment where applicable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.07.03.F05` — CommitDispositionAuthoritative:** Final accepted state can be verified and differentiated from pre-commit stages. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E06.08 — Task Publication, Subscription & Assignment **[NEW C013]**
Provide discoverable, governed, subscription-based actionable work with explicit matching, offering, claiming, assignment and acceptance semantics. This is a consumer-relevant enterprise ability; it does not prescribe a broker, worker pool, queue topology or owning component.

**Source standing:** Candidate architectural inference, preserving existing Continuum meaning from `Event & Subscription` (`b768b129971e4bf5bccc03d33d2fcb4e`) and `Activity & Execution` (`e5ff80e655084524a8dcee8e4ded788a`); not a new governed canonical identity.

**Business-demand hypotheses:** B07.04 Service Delivery Coordination; B07.03 Demand, Capacity & Scheduling; B05.01 Multidisciplinary Care Coordination; B05.06 Community & Cross-Organisation Continuity. These are proposed enablement relationships, not satisfaction claims.

**Architecture boundaries:** E02.04.04 concerns publication/subscription of governed information and events. E06.08 concerns actionable-task offer and allocation. E06.03 determines parcel destinations, E06.04 manages delivery, E06.01 represents task semantics, E06.02 coordinates workflow and E06.07 accounts for durable work acceptance and outcomes. None may be inferred from another's acknowledgement.

#### E06.08.01 — Task Publication **[NEW C013]**
Make independently actionable work discoverable under a governed publication scope without implying that anyone has accepted or performed it.

- **`E06.08.01.F01` — EligibleTaskPublication:** Only eligible, properly formed tasks become available for subscription matching. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.01.F02` — PublicationScopeExplicit:** The task's intended subscriber scope and disclosure constraints are available to matching consumers. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.01.F03` — PublicationIsNotAcceptance:** Publication is never mistaken for dispatch receipt, work acceptance, execution or commitment. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.01.F04` — PublishedTaskIdentityStable:** Publication and subsequent notification preserve the task identity and applicable version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.01.F05` — PublicationExpiryOrWithdrawal:** Expired or withdrawn publications cease to be offered under applicable policy. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.08.02 — Task Subscription Declaration & Lifecycle **[NEW C013]**
Let prospective consumers declare, inspect, change and withdraw governed interest in categories of actionable work.

- **`E06.08.02.F01` — SubscriptionIntentExplicit:** A subscriber's declared task interest is distinguishable from work assignment or acceptance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.02.F02` — SubscriptionCriteriaGoverned:** Subscription criteria and their changes are interpretable under the applicable policy and model release. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.02.F03` — SubscriberAuthorityEvaluated:** Only authorised consumers can establish or exercise applicable subscriptions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.02.F04` — SubscriptionLifecycleVisible:** Active, suspended, expired and withdrawn subscriptions have distinguishable states. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.02.F05` — SubscriptionChangeEffective:** Changes to eligibility or subscription terms take effect according to stated temporal and security rules. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.08.03 — Task Subscription Matching & Eligibility **[NEW C013]**
Match published task requirements to interested and currently eligible consumers without yet claiming that work is assigned.

- **`E06.08.03.F01` — TaskMatchAccurate:** Eligible consumers are matched to tasks under the declared task and subscription criteria. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.03.F02` — UnmatchedWorkVisible:** A task with no eligible subscriber remains explicitly accounted for. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.03.F03` — MatchingContextCurrent:** Matches use the applicable task, subscription and authority state at the decision boundary. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.03.F04` — UnauthorisedDisclosurePrevented:** Subscription matching does not disclose protected task information to ineligible consumers. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.03.F05` — MatchEvidenceTraceable:** The basis for a match, nonmatch or exclusion can be independently assessed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.08.04 — Task Offering, Claiming & Selection **[NEW C013]**
Support the policy-defined transition from discoverable work to offers and claims, including competing or multiple interested consumers.

- **`E06.08.04.F01` — OfferDistinctFromAssignment:** An offer alone does not assert that work was accepted or assigned. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.04.F02` — ClaimEligibilityEnforced:** A claim is permitted only under the task's current authority and eligibility conditions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.04.F03` — CompetingClaimsReconciled:** Concurrent claims resolve according to the declared assignment/concurrency policy. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.04.F04` — MultiConsumerPolicySupported:** First-to-finish, assigned-to-finish, aggregate and other applicable completion policies are not silently reduced to single-consumer handling. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.04.F05` — UnclaimedWorkDisposition:** Unclaimed, declined or expired offers remain visible and are re-offered or escalated only as policy permits. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.08.05 — Task Assignment & Acceptance **[NEW C013]**
Establish an accountable work undertaking independently of its publication, offer and transport state.

- **`E06.08.05.F01` — AssignedResponsibilityAttributable:** Assigned performers and the effective undertaking are unambiguously recorded. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.05.F02` — AssignmentAuthorityValid:** Assignment and acceptance respect current eligibility, authorisation and task constraints. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.05.F03` — DeliveryNotWorkAcceptance:** Parcel or notification receipt cannot be treated as task acceptance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.05.F04` — DurableAcceptanceBeforeAcknowledgement:** Where Harmonia acknowledges accepted governed work, the corresponding recoverable obligation already exists. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.05.F05` — DeclineAndTimeoutHandled:** Decline, acceptance timeout and reassignment produce explicit, policy-conformant outcomes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.08.06 — Task Subscription Notification & State Feedback **[NEW C013]**
Inform eligible subscribers and interested parties of relevant task offers and transitions without redefining task authority.

- **`E06.08.06.F01` — TaskNotificationRelevant:** Notifications reflect applicable subscriptions and task changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.06.F02` — OfferAndTaskCorrelated:** Notifications link to the correct task, publication, offer and applicable version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.06.F03` — StaleOfferDetected:** Stale or superseded offers cannot silently create a valid new undertaking. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.06.F04` — WithdrawalStopsNewOffers:** Withdrawn subscriptions or revoked authority prevent subsequent protected offers. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.06.F05` — TaskStatusFeedbackQualified:** Interested authorised parties can distinguish offered, claimed, assigned, accepted, completed and failed work as applicable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E06.08.07 — Published Work Recovery & Reconciliation **[NEW C013]**
Preserve and reconcile task-publication and subscription obligations during subscriber loss, redelivery and partition/restart conditions.

- **`E06.08.07.F01` — AcceptedWorkSurvivesFailure:** Accepted governed work remains recoverable across the specified failure scenarios. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.07.F02` — SubscriptionRecoveryQualified:** Recovered subscriptions have explicit currency and effective-state semantics. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.07.F03` — RepeatedOfferRecognised:** Redelivery and repeated offers do not silently create duplicate accepted undertakings. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.07.F04` — LostSubscriberDisposition:** Unavailable subscribers do not cause accepted tasks to disappear or become falsely completed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.08.07.F05` — RecoveryPreservesTaskLineage:** Publication, match, claim, assignment and eventual outcome remain correlatable across recovery. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

**Minimum integrity scenarios:** publish a task with no subscriber; match competing eligible subscribers; reject an unauthorised subscription; accept work but fail before dispatch; receive a parcel without task acceptance; recover from subscriber loss; withdraw a subscription after an offer; and reconcile first-to-finish versus aggregate completion without duplicating accepted obligations.

**Open design questions:** Whether publication is available for every task archetype; how subscriptions relate to task affinity and assigned-performer modes; how claims relate to Task concurrency/completion policy; and which operational behaviours are native to the platform versus externally delegated. These do not block defining the candidate capabilities.

### E06.09 — Asynchronous Digital Twin–Entity Work Coordination **[NEW C014]**
Preserve governed work across the digital/physical boundary: a Twin may delegate or reflect a real-world undertaking, remain able to progress independent stimuli and subsequently reconcile outcomes without a continuously executing Ergon.

*Evidence-led candidate.* Lenses: DT,LHR; sources: C-CT2-ACT,DRP-PR,DRP-PG,DRP-ER. No solution component or release commitment implied.

#### E06.09.01 — Entity-Associated Work Delegation **[NEW C014]**
Establish a governed task whose undertaking may be performed by the physical entity represented by a Twin or another explicitly accountable performer.

- **`E06.09.01.F01` — DelegatedWorkAttributable:** Task and intended performer are distinct, identifiable and traceable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.01.F02` — DigitalReferentNotFulfiller:** The Twin’s digital progression does not claim the Twin physically performed human care. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.01.F03` — DelegationAuthorityVerified:** The delegating actor’s effective authority is checked for the work scope. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.01.F04` — DelegationContextSufficient:** The recipient can obtain the permitted task context needed to undertake the work. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.01.F05` — DeclinedDelegationVisible:** A declined or unaccepted delegation remains an explicitly outstanding or disposed obligation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* AGREED_DIRECTION_WITH_INFERRED_CAPABILITY_DETAIL; evidence keys: DRP-PR, C-CT2-ACT. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E06.09.02 — Deferred Task Lifecycle **[NEW C014]**
Maintain long-duration task obligations without keeping an Ergon thread, transaction or execution envelope continuously active.

- **`E06.09.02.F01` — DeferredNotCompleted:** A deferred task is never counted as fulfilled merely because an invocation returned. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.02.F02` — NoLongLivedErgonRequired:** A multi-day scenario passes without a continuously executing bounded Ergon. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.02.F03` — DeferredIdentityStable:** Task and correlation identity survive deferred periods and component restarts. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.02.F04` — PendingObligationsVisible:** Pending information, work acceptance and output obligations remain discoverable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.02.F05` — DeferredContextRestorable:** Progression can resume from governed durable state without restoring an in-flight Ergon stack. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* AGREED_DIRECTION_WITH_INFERRED_CAPABILITY_DETAIL; evidence keys: DRP-PR, DRP-PG, DRP-ER. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E06.09.03 — Effector/Stimulus Work Correlation **[NEW C014]**
Associate outgoing/incoming Digital Twin interaction events with the correct task, physical entity, authority and eventual disposition.

- **`E06.09.03.F01` — WorkInteractionCorrelation:** A later Stimulus can be matched to the intended outstanding undertaking. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.03.F02` — NonmatchingStimulusContained:** An unrelated or forged interaction cannot alter a protected task. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.03.F03` — RepeatedStimulusDisposition:** Duplicate and late responses have a policy-defined, reviewable treatment. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.03.F04` — StimulusTemporalContext:** The occurrence and receipt times of reported physical-world actions remain distinguishable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.03.F05` — SecurityContextPropagation:** The relevant effective authority and disclosure restrictions survive asynchronous correlation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* AGREED_DIRECTION_WITH_INFERRED_CAPABILITY_DETAIL; evidence keys: DRP-PR, C-CT2-EVENT. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E06.09.04 — Independent Twin Activity Progression **[NEW C014]**
Process other permitted Digital Twin activities while one or more externally fulfilled tasks remain pending, subject to actual ordering/dependency policy.

- **`E06.09.04.F01` — UnrelatedTaskNotBlocked:** A days-long Task X does not block independent Task Y progression. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.04.F02` — DeclaredOrderingRespected:** Dependent tasks cannot incorrectly pass a declared sequencing barrier. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.04.F03` — MultiplePendingObligations:** One Twin can manage several independently tracked deferred undertakings. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.04.F04` — ConcurrentStimulusConsistency:** Simultaneous permitted events produce policy-conformant, explainable outcomes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.04.F05` — NoImplicitGlobalLock:** External fulfilment does not require a Twin-wide execution lock for its entire physical duration. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* AGREED_DIRECTION_WITH_INFERRED_CAPABILITY_DETAIL; evidence keys: DRP-ER, C-CT2-ACT. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E06.09.05 — External Fulfilment Outcome Assimilation **[NEW C014]**
Capture reports of physical-world task progress, completion or failure as evidence for governed task progression, not automatic proof of authoritative data commitment.

- **`E06.09.05.F01` — ReportedNotAutomaticallyAccepted:** A human completion report can remain pending validation or approval. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.05.F02` — OutcomeSourceAttribution:** The reporter and claimed physical fulfiller can be distinguished where they differ. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.05.F03` — PartialOutcomeRepresentable:** Partial, blocked and qualified fulfilment outcomes remain interpretable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.05.F04` — InformationCommitSeparate:** An accepted task result does not automatically establish that all clinical information versions were committed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.05.F05` — PhysicalDigitalProgressionReconciled:** Final task state correctly reflects the physical-world report and associated decision evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* AGREED_DIRECTION_WITH_INFERRED_CAPABILITY_DETAIL; evidence keys: DRP-PR, DRP-MN. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E06.09.06 — External Work Temporal Obligations **[NEW C014]**
Maintain reminders, escalation, expiry and appropriate exceptional handling for physically fulfilled work over extended periods.

- **`E06.09.06.F01` — DurationWithoutFailure:** Ordinary multi-day duration does not by itself imply task failure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.06.F02` — DeadlineEvidenceRetained:** Applicable due times and deadline changes remain attributable and interpretable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.06.F03` — ReminderEligibilityEnforced:** Notifications are sent only to authorised, relevant parties. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.06.F04` — EscalationNotFalseCompletion:** Escalation changes obligation handling without silently completing the original work. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.06.F05` — TemporalBoundaryRecoverable:** Calendar and deadline obligations survive restart or service interruption. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* AGREED_DIRECTION_WITH_INFERRED_CAPABILITY_DETAIL; evidence keys: C-CT2-ACT, DRP-PR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E06.09.07 — Deferred Work Recovery & Reconciliation **[NEW C014]**
Recover outstanding real-world obligations despite lost clients, offline periods, inconsistent reports or Digital Twin restart.

- **`E06.09.07.F01` — RestartPreservesOutstandingWork:** The Twin’s accepted unresolved work survives its own restart. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.07.F02` — OfflineCompletionReconciled:** A late progress report can be matched to the correct historical task and current policy. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.07.F03` — ConflictingOutcomeVisible:** Two contradictory completion reports are surfaced rather than arbitrarily collapsed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.07.F04` — ReassignmentTraceability:** Reassignments preserve the prior responsible party and reason. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E06.09.07.F05` — NoLostAcceptedObligation:** Loss of a communication or UI session does not lose a durably accepted task. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* AGREED_DIRECTION_WITH_INFERRED_CAPABILITY_DETAIL; evidence keys: DRP-PR, DRP-MN. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.



## E07 — Information Presentation & Human Interaction
The system can expose governed information and work context to people in useful, controlled ways.

### E07.01 — Contextual Information Presentation
Present relevant information for a legitimate user task.

#### E07.01.01 — Clinical Context Presentation
Clinical consumers can view governed information relevant to a clinical question.

- **`E07.01.01.F01` — RelevantClinicalContext:** proportion of agreed clinical presentation tests exposing required contextual information. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.01.F02` — Relevant Clinical Information Selection:** proportion of applicable clinical context presentation requests or situations with verified relevant clinical information selection. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.01.F03` — Clinician-Readable Presentation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed clinical context presentation scenarios demonstrating clinician-readable presentation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.01.F04` — Clinical Risk Highlighting:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing clinical context presentation scenarios with correct clinical risk highlighting, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.01.F05` — Clinical Context Freshness:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed clinical context presentation scenarios demonstrating clinical context freshness against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.01.02 — Care Team Context Presentation
Participating care workers can view relevant shared care context.

- **`E07.01.02.F01` — SharedCareContextCompleteness:** proportion of tested care-team views providing required governed context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.02.F02` — Assigned Team Visibility:** proportion of sampled care team context presentation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of assigned team visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.02.F03` — Current Responsibility View:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in care team context presentation for which current responsibility view is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.02.F04` — Multidisciplinary Care Context:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed care team context presentation scenarios demonstrating multidisciplinary care context against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.02.F05` — Team Handover Context:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in care team context presentation for which team handover context is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.01.03 — Participant Support Context Presentation
Support settings can present information relevant to participant goals and supports.

- **`E07.01.03.F01` — AppropriateSupportContext:** proportion of support-context test cases showing required information with restrictions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.03.F02` — Participant-Appropriate Language:** proportion of representative participant support context presentation scenarios in which participant-appropriate language is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.03.F03` — Current Support Goal Visibility:** proportion of sampled participant support context presentation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of current support goal visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.03.F04` — Accessible Support Information:** proportion of representative participant support context presentation scenarios in which accessible support information is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.03.F05` — Support Preference Presentation:** proportion of representative participant support context presentation scenarios in which support preference presentation is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.01.04 — Service Operations Context Presentation
Service operators can view demand, capacity and work context relevant to service operation.

- **`E07.01.04.F01` — UsefulOperationalContext:** proportion of test scenarios exposing required operational indicators and context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.04.F02` — Live Service Demand View:** proportion of applicable service operations context presentation requests or situations with verified live service demand view. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.04.F03` — Capacity Constraint Visibility:** proportion of sampled service operations context presentation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of capacity constraint visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.04.F04` — Operational Exception Highlighting:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed service operations context presentation scenarios demonstrating operational exception highlighting against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.04.F05` — Service Status Currency:** proportion of sampled service operations context presentation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of service status currency. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.01.05 — Digital Service Administration Context
Administrators can inspect authorised service configuration and administration context.

- **`E07.01.05.F01` — AccurateAdministrationView:** proportion of sampled administrative views reflecting governed current state. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.05.F02` — Administrative Scope Visibility:** proportion of positive and negative digital service administration context test cases meeting the applicable administrative scope visibility criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.05.F03` — Configuration State Explanation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed digital service administration context scenarios demonstrating configuration state explanation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.05.F04` — Change Impact Preview:** proportion of sampled digital service administration context cases with accessible, accurate evidence of change impact preview. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.01.05.F05` — Administrative Action Confirmation:** proportion of applicable digital service administration context interactions where administrative action confirmation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.01.06 — Human Role & Context Sensitive Presentation **[NEW C012]**
Present care, participant, administrative and operational information in their own appropriate conceptual and decision contexts.

*Demand: B01.04, B05.01, B14.01, B07.04; evidence: PRESENT, TH, JOURNEY; findings: R11, R18.*

- **`E07.01.06.F01` — AudienceContextAppropriate:** Patient and participant interfaces are assessed against distinct journeys. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.01.06.F02` — ClinicalAndNonclinicalSeparated:** Clinical judgements are not silently inferred from participant support preferences. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.01.06.F03` — RestrictedFieldsOmitted:** Presentation respects field and compartment restrictions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.01.06.F04` — SourceAndCurrencyVisible:** Display includes source and relevant age of information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.01.06.F05` — ScenarioMeaningValidated:** Users can identify the correct intended action in representative domain scenarios. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E07.01.07 — Operational Assurance Presentation **[NEW C012]**
Support OA&M users with status, exceptions and service readiness without exposing clinical content unnecessarily.

*Demand: B07.06, B12.04, B12.05; evidence: OPS, LOG, PRESENT; findings: R14, R24.*

- **`E07.01.07.F01` — ReadinessStatusGrounded:** UI readiness state corresponds to observed component and dependency health. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.01.07.F02` — IncidentDrilldownAvailable:** An incident can be traced to correlated diagnostics. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.01.07.F03` — NoDefaultClinicalPayload:** OA&M displays avoid PHI unless separately authorised and necessary. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.01.07.F04` — RecoveryProgressVisible:** Restoration state differentiates in-progress and verified recovered. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.01.07.F05` — OperatorActionsAudited:** Operational actions with material consequences are attributable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E07.02 — Governed User Interaction
Allow authorised users to initiate and complete governed interactions.

#### E07.02.01 — Governed User Action
An authorised user can initiate and receive the outcome of a governed action.

- **`E07.02.01.F01` — CorrectUserActionDisposition:** proportion of tested interactions receiving correct acceptance/rejection outcomes. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.01.F02` — Eligible Action Availability:** proportion of positive and negative governed user action test cases meeting the applicable eligible action availability criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.01.F03` — Authorisation-Bound User Action:** proportion of positive and negative governed user action test cases meeting the applicable authorisation-bound user action criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.01.F04` — Action Consequence Explanation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed governed user action scenarios demonstrating action consequence explanation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.01.F05` — Action Outcome Feedback:** proportion of applicable governed user action interactions where action outcome feedback reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.02.02 — Review & Confirmation Interaction
Users can review intended changes and acknowledge significant actions where required.

- **`E07.02.02.F01` — ExplicitActionConfirmation:** proportion of required confirmed actions with valid acknowledgement evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.02.F02` — Reviewable Action Detail:** proportion of sampled review & confirmation interaction cases with accessible, accurate evidence of reviewable action detail. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.02.F03` — Explicit User Confirmation:** proportion of applicable review & confirmation interaction interactions where explicit user confirmation reaches and is understood by the intended recipient. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.02.F04` — Material Change Highlight:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in review & confirmation interaction for which material change highlight is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.02.F05` — Reversible Draft Action:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed review & confirmation interaction scenarios demonstrating reversible draft action against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.02.03 — Accessible Interaction
Information-system interaction remains usable under the supported accessibility requirements.

- **`E07.02.03.F01` — SupportedInteractionAccessibility:** proportion of defined accessibility conformance scenarios passed. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.03.F02` — Keyboard and Assistive Access:** proportion of representative accessible interaction scenarios in which keyboard and assistive access is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.03.F03` — Understandable Interaction Language:** proportion of representative accessible interaction scenarios in which understandable interaction language is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.03.F04` — Accessible Error Recovery:** proportion of representative accessible interaction scenarios in which accessible error recovery is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.03.F05` — Alternative Interaction Method:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in accessible interaction for which alternative interaction method is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.02.04 — Controlled Interaction Scope
Available actions reflect the current information and security scope.

- **`E07.02.04.F01` — ScopeAppropriateInteraction:** proportion of tested contexts exposing and enforcing only permitted actions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.04.F02` — Work-Context Action Restriction:** proportion of positive and negative controlled interaction scope test cases meeting the applicable work-context action restriction criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.04.F03` — Relevant Data Scoping:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed controlled interaction scope scenarios demonstrating relevant data scoping against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.04.F04` — Cross-Subject Leakage Prevention:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed controlled interaction scope scenarios demonstrating cross-subject leakage prevention against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.02.04.F05` — Context Change Warning:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in controlled interaction scope for which context change warning is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.02.05 — Staged Change Review & Confirmation **[NEW C012]**
Allow a user to review, check, adjust and explicitly submit a change without collapsing those intentions into commitment.

*Demand: B01.04, B10.07, B14.03; evidence: PRESENT, MN, TH; findings: R06, R11.*

- **`E07.02.05.F01` — ReviewDoesNotMutate:** Review interaction does not commit authoritative changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.05.F02` — ValidationFindingsActionable:** Check results identify correctable conditions in consumer language. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.05.F03` — PreparedChangeDiffVisible:** A user can see intended differences before submission. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.05.F04` — CommitConsentExplicit:** Where required, authoritative submission requires a distinct confirmation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.05.F05` — UnknownCommitNotSuccess:** An uncertain commitment cannot be displayed as confirmed success. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E07.02.06 — Supported & Represented Participation **[NEW C012]**
Allow appropriately authorised assistance and representation while recording the participant or patient’s own preferences and authority limits.

*Demand: B01.05, B14.01, B14.06; evidence: TH, PRESENT, JOURNEY; findings: R11, R22.*

- **`E07.02.06.F01` — RepresentativeScopeVisible:** The effective scope and duration of a representative role can be inspected. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.06.F02` — SupportedVoiceRecorded:** Participant preference is recorded separately from representative instruction. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.06.F03` — AuthorityRevocationImmediate:** Expired or revoked representation is not permitted to continue. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.06.F04` — DisagreementNotSilenced:** Conflicting preferences and instructions are distinguishable for escalation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.06.F05` — DecisionEvidenceAttributable:** The person, representative and reviewer actions remain separately attributable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E07.02.07 — Accessibility & Language Scope Transparency **[NEW C012]**
Express actual accessibility and language support without silently asserting unapproved release commitments.

*Demand: B01.04, B14.06; evidence: PRESENT, NFR, JOURNEY; findings: R11.*

- **`E07.02.07.F01` — SupportedLocaleDeclared:** Available release-language support is explicit. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.07.F02` — UnsupportedNeedEscalatable:** A user can obtain a bounded alternative when needed support is absent. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.07.F03` — NoFalseWCAGClaim:** Testing does not assert WCAG conformance when it is outside the release baseline. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.07.F04` — CriticalContentLegible:** Known accessibility-critical scenarios receive targeted review against actual contract. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.07.F05` — HelpContextRelevant:** Service guidance is expressed in the current user participation context. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E07.02.08 — Governed Collaboration Interaction **[NEW — P3/002, FC-076]**
Enable directed communication and native conversational collaboration, including governed capture of defined interactions, without confusing communication with authoritative changes to work responsibility.

*Accepted CT3 scope: FC-076. General collaboration intent applies to Agora/Omilia; E02.04 continues information distribution, E06.08 continues accountable Task assignment/acceptance/reassignment, and E07.06.04 continues task-context collaboration. The following individual Feature formulations are **proposed for semantic review**, not separately adjudicated.*

- **`E07.02.08.F01` — Directed Communication Interaction:** Governed point-to-point or addressed messaging can identify sender, recipient, permitted content and message disposition without treating delivery as acceptance of work. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.08.F02` — Managed Conversational Collaboration:** Authorised participants can engage in direct/group dialogue and managed Rooms/Spaces, with controlled membership and distinguishable native collaboration records/media. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.08.F03` — Defined Collaboration Action Capture:** Explicitly defined, meaningful interactions in managed collaboration can generate governed information or work with attributable source context; ordinary utterances do not automatically become Pragma. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.02.08.F04` — Task Reassignment Authority Separation:** A message, chat request or acknowledgement does not by itself reassign a Task; any authoritative responsibility change is established through the governed assignment/reassignment capability and remains attributable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E07.03 — Longitudinal Information Viewing
Present related information across appropriate time/encounter boundaries.

#### E07.03.01 — Longitudinal Health Record Viewing
Consumers can examine relevant information across encounters and time.

- **`E07.03.01.F01` — CorrectLongitudinalContext:** proportion of sampled timelines with correct chronological and provenance context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.01.F02` — Chronological Record Continuity:** proportion of sampled longitudinal health record viewing cases with accessible, accurate evidence of chronological record continuity. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.01.F03` — Correct Event Association:** proportion of independently checked longitudinal health record viewing instances satisfying the specified correct event association quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.01.F04` — Historical Version Accessibility:** proportion of representative longitudinal health record viewing scenarios in which historical version accessibility is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.01.F05` — Missing Period Disclosure:** proportion of sampled longitudinal health record viewing cases with accessible, accurate evidence of missing period disclosure. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.03.02 — Cross-Service Information Viewing
Related information from multiple supported services can be inspected in meaningful context.

- **`E07.03.02.F01` — CoherentCrossServiceView:** proportion of test collections presented without misattribution or silent omission. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.02.F02` — Cross-Service Context Alignment:** proportion of applicable cross-service information viewing requests or situations with verified cross-service context alignment. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.02.F03` — Source-Specific Attribution:** proportion of sampled cross-service information viewing cases with accessible, accurate evidence of source-specific attribution. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.02.F04` — Information Source Disagreement:** proportion of sampled cross-service information viewing cases with accessible, accurate evidence of information source disagreement. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.02.F05` — Combined View Relevance:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed cross-service information viewing scenarios demonstrating combined view relevance against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.03.03 — Time-Oriented Event Viewing
Consumers can understand significant events through an attributable temporal perspective.

- **`E07.03.03.F01` — AccurateEventSequencePresentation:** proportion of tested event sequences displaying correct chronology and qualifiers. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.03.F02` — Correct Event Sequence:** proportion of independently checked time-oriented event viewing instances satisfying the specified correct event sequence quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.03.F03` — Time-Zone-Aware Display:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed time-oriented event viewing scenarios demonstrating time-zone-aware display against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.03.F04` — Event Interval Recognition:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the event interval recognition outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.03.03.F05` — Late Event Qualification:** proportion of independently checked time-oriented event viewing instances satisfying the specified late event qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.03.04 — Cross-Domain Longitudinal Correlation View **[NEW C012]**
Display a person’s timeline across encounters, providers and representations without conflating occurrence, documentation and custody time.

*Demand: B02.01, B05.04, B14.04; evidence: IU, REF, PRESENT; findings: R09, R11.*

- **`E07.03.04.F01` — TimeAxisDiscriminated:** Occurrence and recorded/persisted times can be distinguished. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.04.F02` — SourceSystemRetained:** Cross-provider timeline entries show source lineage. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.04.F03` — HistoricalVersionAccessible:** A relevant past state can be retrieved without current overwrite. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.04.F04` — UncertainCorrelationMarked:** Probable but unconfirmed identity matches are qualified. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.04.F05` — RestrictedTimelineFiltered:** Protected records do not leak through timeline metadata. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E07.03.05 — Cross-Source Clinical Timeline Synthesis **[NEW C014]**
Present an ordered longitudinal view that respects event times, recording times and uncertain or conflicting chronology.

- **`E07.03.05.F01` — TemporalDimensionsVisible:** The view distinguishes occurrence and recording time when the distinction is material. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.05.F02` — CrossSourceEventOrderQualified:** Ambiguous event order is qualified instead of presented as certain chronology. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.05.F03` — EventSourceAttribution:** Each displayed event remains attributable to its source and underlying record. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.05.F04` — ClinicalGroupingWithoutDataLoss:** Episode or event grouping does not discard independently governed clinical assertions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.05.F05` — LateArrivingInformationReconciled:** Late clinical information is correctly incorporated without silently rewriting the meaning of prior views. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-LHR-QUERY, C-FED-QUERY, DRP-IU. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E07.03.06 — Encounter & Episode Context Navigation **[NEW C014]**
Navigate clinical information by patient, encounter, care episode and responsible care context without conflating these identities.

- **`E07.03.06.F01` — EncounterBoundaryVisible:** The user can distinguish encounters and episodes even when their dates overlap. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.06.F02` — PatientContextProtected:** Switching patient context cannot silently retain the previous patient’s clinical data. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.06.F03` — MultiProviderEpisodeLinkage:** Related care episodes can be discovered with explicit provenance of the relationship. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.06.F04` — NoImplicitEpisodeMerge:** Related encounters are not treated as one authoritative episode without an established basis. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.06.F05` — ScopedClinicalNavigation:** Navigation respects current consent, purpose and information restrictions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-LHR-QUERY, C-FED-QUERY. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E07.03.07 — Longitudinal Correction & Disagreement Awareness **[NEW C014]**
Make corrected, superseded, disputed and incomplete clinical assertions understandable during longitudinal review.

- **`E07.03.07.F01` — SupersededDataQualified:** A corrected observation is not displayed as current unqualified truth. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.07.F02` — DisputedAssertionVisible:** Disputed clinical information has an identifiable qualification where disclosure is permitted. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.07.F03` — PriorRecordPreserved:** A reviewer can trace an amendment to earlier governed evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.07.F04` — AbsentVersusWithheld:** No matching clinical content is distinguishable from restricted or unavailable information as permitted. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.03.07.F05` — CorrectionImpactContextual:** Relevant downstream summaries reveal when their source information has been superseded. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-LHR-QUERY, DRP-IU, DRP-AUD. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


### E07.04 — User-Context Continuity
Maintain consistent user context during relevant work where supported.

#### E07.04.01 — Persistent Work Context
A user can return to relevant unfinished digital work without losing necessary context.

- **`E07.04.01.F01` — RestoredUserWorkContext:** proportion of prescribed recovery cases returning required context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.01.F02` — Restored User Work Position:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in persistent work context for which restored user work position is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.01.F03` — Unsaved Work Warning:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed persistent work context scenarios demonstrating unsaved work warning against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.01.F04` — Context Expiry Handling:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in persistent work context for which context expiry handling is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.01.F05` — Context Recovery after Interruption:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in persistent work context for which context recovery after interruption is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.04.02 — Contextual Navigation Continuity
Navigation between related scopes preserves appropriate contextual references.

- **`E07.04.02.F01` — PreservedNavigationContext:** proportion of in-scope transitions maintaining permitted relevant context. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.02.F02` — Relevant Navigation History:** proportion of representative contextual navigation continuity scenarios in which relevant navigation history is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.02.F03` — Stable Subject Context:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed contextual navigation continuity scenarios demonstrating stable subject context against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.02.F04` — Context Preservation between Views:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in contextual navigation continuity for which context preservation between views is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.02.F05` — Back-Navigation Integrity:** proportion of representative contextual navigation continuity scenarios in which back-navigation integrity is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.04.03 — Safe Context Switching
A user can change work context without carrying unintended sensitive information into the new scope.

- **`E07.04.03.F01` — CorrectContextIsolation:** proportion of tested context switches satisfying required scope boundaries. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.03.F02` — Explicit Subject Transition:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in safe context switching for which explicit subject transition is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.03.F03` — Uncommitted Change Protection:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in safe context switching for which uncommitted change protection is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.03.F04` — Cross-Context Data Isolation:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing safe context switching scenarios with correct cross-context data isolation, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.04.03.F05` — Current Context Indicator:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in safe context switching for which current context indicator is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.04.04 — Safe Interrupted Session Continuation **[NEW C012]**
Restore a user’s work and navigation context after interruption without implying uncommitted actions were accepted.

*Demand: B01.04, B05.01, B14.04; evidence: PRAGMATON, PRESENT, TH; findings: R06, R11.*

- **`E07.04.04.F01` — UncommittedChangesLabelled:** Recovered drafts are clearly not authoritative. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.04.04.F02` — AuthorityRecheckedOnResume:** Session restoration does not bypass new authorisation conditions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.04.04.F03` — OriginalContextIdentifiable:** Restored work preserves source unit/version identifiers. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.04.04.F04` — InterruptedActionDisambiguated:** The UI identifies whether submission outcome is pending, failed or committed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.04.04.F05` — SafeContextSwitch:** Switching patient/participant context cannot carry prior restricted content across. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E07.04.05 — Review-to-Action Context Preservation **[NEW C012]**
Maintain the identity, source version and authority of information as a consumer moves from finding to action.

*Demand: B02.01, B05.03, B10.07; evidence: PRESENT, REF, TH; findings: R06, R18.*

- **`E07.04.05.F01` — SelectedObjectStable:** A selected item remains bound to the intended governed identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.04.05.F02` — VersionChangeAlerted:** Concurrent updates trigger visible context-change qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.04.05.F03` — NoCrossPersonLeakage:** Switching subjects cannot reuse the previous subject information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.04.05.F04` — ActionTargetConfirmed:** High-impact actions identify the actual target object and effective role. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.04.05.F05` — AuditContextCorrelatable:** User action evidence links to the viewed source when required. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E07.05 — Information Presentation Accountability
Show enough context about freshness, source, status and constraints to support safe interpretation.

#### E07.05.01 — Source-Attributed Information Display
Presented information carries sufficient source and provenance indicators.

- **`E07.05.01.F01` — VisibleSourceAttribution:** proportion of in-scope displayed information with required origin indicators. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.01.F02` — Visible Origin and Author:** proportion of sampled source-attributed information display cases with accessible, accurate evidence of visible origin and author. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.01.F03` — Source Trust Qualification:** proportion of independently checked source-attributed information display instances satisfying the specified source trust qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.01.F04` — Derived Versus Original Distinction:** proportion of sampled source-attributed information display cases with accessible, accurate evidence of derived versus original distinction. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.01.F05` — Source Detail Accessibility:** proportion of representative source-attributed information display scenarios in which source detail accessibility is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.05.02 — Information Currency & Status Display
Consumers can distinguish currency, provisional status and known limitations.

- **`E07.05.02.F01` — VisibleFreshnessAndStatus:** proportion of required indicators displayed correctly under tests. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.02.F02` — Last-Updated Visibility:** proportion of sampled information currency & status display cases with accessible, accurate evidence of last-updated visibility. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.02.F03` — Provisional Versus Final Status:** proportion of sampled information currency & status display cases with accessible, accurate evidence of provisional versus final status. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.02.F04` — Stale Information Warning:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed information currency & status display scenarios demonstrating stale information warning against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.02.F05` — Information Validity Context:** proportion of independently checked information currency & status display instances satisfying the specified information validity context quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.05.03 — Incomplete Information Disclosure
Known gaps or unavailable information are visible instead of silently implied complete.

- **`E07.05.03.F01` — VisibleKnownIncompleteness:** proportion of injected partial-data scenarios correctly qualified. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.03.F02` — Missing Data Notice:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed incomplete information disclosure scenarios demonstrating missing data notice against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.03.F03` — Incomplete View Qualification:** proportion of independently checked incomplete information disclosure instances satisfying the specified incomplete view qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.03.F04` — Partial Information Safe Use:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing incomplete information disclosure scenarios with correct partial information safe use, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.03.F05` — Missing Source Escalation Path:** proportion of sampled incomplete information disclosure cases with accessible, accurate evidence of missing source escalation path. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.05.04 — Presentation Restriction Disclosure
Consumers can identify where a view is purposefully restricted or redacted.

- **`E07.05.04.F01` — TransparentScopeRestriction:** proportion of tested restricted views indicating applicable limitations without leaking content. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.04.F02` — Restricted Content Indicator:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed presentation restriction disclosure scenarios demonstrating restricted content indicator against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.04.F03` — Non-Disclosure of Protected Detail:** proportion of sampled risk-bearing presentation restriction disclosure scenarios with correct non-disclosure of protected detail, including prescribed adverse cases. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.04.F04` — Restriction Reason Qualification:** proportion of positive and negative presentation restriction disclosure test cases meeting the applicable restriction reason qualification criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E07.05.04.F05` — Availability of Review Route:** proportion of sampled presentation restriction disclosure cases with accessible, accurate evidence of availability of review route. *PROPOSED*.

#### E07.05.05 — Decision Provenance Presentation **[NEW C012]**
Make visible which human and automated inputs influenced a displayed conclusion without assigning decision authority to advice.

*Demand: B02.04, B11.03, B14.06; evidence: PROV, ASSURE, PRESENT; findings: R15, R18.*

- **`E07.05.05.F01` — EvidenceLinksUsable:** A displayed recommendation links to retrievable authorised evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.05.05.F02` — AutomatedAdviceLabelled:** AI/automated observations are recognisable as non-authoritative. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.05.05.F03` — DecisionOwnerDistinct:** The human or authorised decision owner is distinguished from advice origin. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.05.05.F04` — DataGapsDisplayed:** Material gaps and outdated inputs are explicit. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.05.05.F05` — RecommendationReproducible:** Available provenance permits review of the basis used for advice. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E07.05.06 — Incomplete & Qualified Information Disclosure **[NEW C012]**
Distinguish absent, unavailable, restricted, stale and partially derived information in human-facing presentation.

*Demand: B02.01, B10.03, B14.02; evidence: PRESENT, MN, IU; findings: R09, R11.*

- **`E07.05.06.F01` — UnavailableNotAbsent:** Temporary inaccessibility is not displayed as an absent clinical fact. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.05.06.F02` — PermissionNotExistence:** Restriction notices do not leak protected existence details. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.05.06.F03` — StalenessVisible:** Known source currency is shown where relevant. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.05.06.F04` — DerivedQualificationShown:** Inferred and transformed information is visually distinguishable from native source. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.05.06.F05` — PartialResultCaveat:** Material incompleteness is shown before a consequential user decision. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E07.06 — Human–Digital Twin Work Interaction **[NEW C014]**
Provide authorised, context-sensitive work inbox, calendar, collaboration and progress interactions between human/physical-world actors and the work governed through Digital Twins, separate from administrative OA&M control.

*Evidence-led candidate.* Lenses: DT,PD; sources: C-CT2-ACT,DRP-PR,DRP-SEC. No solution component or release commitment implied.

#### E07.06.01 — Human Work Inbox **[NEW C014]**
Present task offers, assignments, deferred obligations and actionable status in a role-appropriate Twin-associated work view.

- **`E07.06.01.F01` — InboxScopeCorrect:** A practitioner sees only work appropriate to their role, patient context and effective authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.01.F02` — OfferVsAssignedVisible:** Work offered for claim is visually distinguishable from work already assigned or accepted. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.01.F03` — PendingOutcomeVisibility:** Outstanding, deferred and completed work have distinguishable dispositions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.01.F04` — MultipleTwinContextSafe:** A user acting for multiple represented entities cannot accidentally complete a task in the wrong context. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.01.F05` — InboxRecovery:** Tasks remain discoverable after client restart or a period offline. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-CT2-ACT, DRP-PR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E07.06.02 — Task Acceptance & Participation Interaction **[NEW C014]**
Enable authorised human participants to claim, accept, decline, progress and reassign work within the governing policy.

- **`E07.06.02.F01` — HumanAcceptanceAttributable:** The accepted task is linked to the participant, time and applicable authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.02.F02` — DeclineReassignmentPath:** Decline or reassignment offers a visible, valid alternative disposition. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.02.F03` — UnauthorizedActionRejected:** An unauthorised actor cannot claim, complete or reassign protected work. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.02.F04` — ConcurrencyConflictExplained:** A lost race between competing task claimants yields an intelligible disposition. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.02.F05` — NoUIFalseAcceptance:** Clicking an action is not portrayed as accepted until the governing task state confirms it. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: DRP-PR, DRP-SEC. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E07.06.03 — Integrated Work & Calendar View **[NEW C014]**
Present scheduled and unscheduled obligations, deadlines and work context as coherent but distinct aspects of the represented entity’s work.

- **`E07.06.03.F01` — SingleCalendarAuthorityRespected:** The view consumes the governed central calendar without creating a competing scheduling authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.03.F02` — TaskScheduleAssociation:** A scheduled activity can be traced to its governing work obligation. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.03.F03` — UnscheduledWorkVisible:** Important unscheduled obligations are not hidden merely because they have no calendar event. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.03.F04` — TimeZoneAndTemporalAccuracy:** Displayed appointments, due times and changes respect relevant time-zone and temporal semantics. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.03.F05` — CalendarConflictQualified:** Clashes, cancellations and reschedules are represented without silently changing task responsibility. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: C-CT2-ACT, DRP-PR. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E07.06.04 — Task-Context Collaboration **[NEW C014]**
Support contextual conversations and sharing about work without making a collaboration room authoritative for task or clinical information state.

- **`E07.06.04.F01` — ConversationTaskCorrelation:** Relevant conversations can be associated with the correct task without replacing its governed identity. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.04.F02` — CollaborationDisclosureEnforced:** Messages or attachments do not bypass clinical information disclosure restrictions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.04.F03` — CommunicationNotWorkCommit:** A message stating a task is complete does not automatically commit the task or its output. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.04.F04` — ParticipantMembershipCurrent:** Removed or newly unauthorised participants do not retain protected access without explicit authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.04.F05` — ClientTechnologyIndependent:** Capability conformance can be tested without mandating Matrix/Element or a particular UI framework. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: DRP-PR, DRP-SEC. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E07.06.05 — Asynchronous Progress & Outcome Reporting UI **[NEW C014]**
Capture human reports of in-progress, partial, complete or failed physical work with attributable evidence and understandable dispositions.

- **`E07.06.05.F01` — ProgressReportAttributable:** Reporter identity and the subject task are correctly recorded. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.05.F02` — IncompleteWorkNotClosed:** A partial report cannot inadvertently close an outstanding task. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.05.F03` — OutcomeReviewVisible:** A report awaiting review or commit is not shown as authoritative completion. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.05.F04` — LateReportHandled:** A report submitted after an offline period receives a reconciliable disposition. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.05.F05` — EvidenceAttachmentGoverned:** Any submitted clinical or supporting content remains subject to Information Unit and security governance. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: DRP-PR, DRP-MN. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.


#### E07.06.06 — Administration vs Operational Work Experience **[NEW C014]**
Maintain a clear boundary between administration/OA&M of Digital Twins and their human task, collaboration and calendar experience.

- **`E07.06.06.F01` — OAAMActionSeparation:** Operational maintenance actions are not presented as clinical or human task fulfilment. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.06.F02` — RoleScopedAdministrativeControls:** Administrative permissions do not automatically grant clinical information disclosure or task decision authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.06.F03` — TwinStateVsHumanWorkClarity:** A user can distinguish Digital Twin platform state from the physical entity’s outstanding work. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.06.F04` — UserContextIsolation:** Moving between administrative and task contexts does not leak previously viewed protected information. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E07.06.06.F05` — UsableWorkNavigation:** A relevant authorised participant can find and act on their tasks without OA&M platform knowledge. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

*Source reasoning:* INFERRED_FROM_EVIDENCE; evidence keys: DRP-SEC, C-CT2-ACT. *Release horizon:* Unscheduled.



## E08 — Operational Observability, Assurance & Resilience
The system can reveal its operational state, preserve evidence, support controlled response and recover service.

### E08.01 — Operational Observability
Provide meaningful operational health, event and performance signals.

#### E08.01.01 — Service Health Visibility
Operations can determine whether required digital services are healthy.

- **`E08.01.01.F01` — AccurateServiceHealth:** proportion of known health scenarios correctly represented. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.01.F02` — Current Health Status:** proportion of sampled service health visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of current health status. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.01.F03` — Service Dependency Awareness:** proportion of applicable service health visibility requests or situations with verified service dependency awareness. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.01.F04` — Degradation State Recognition:** proportion of sampled service health visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of degradation state recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.01.F05` — Health Status History:** proportion of sampled service health visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of health status history. *PROPOSED*.

#### E08.01.02 — Operational Performance Visibility
Operations can inspect response, queue, throughput and pressure indicators.

- **`E08.01.02.F01` — ActionablePerformanceSignals:** proportion of defined performance conditions represented by appropriate measures. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.02.F02` — Workload and Throughput View:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed operational performance visibility scenarios demonstrating workload and throughput view against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.02.F03` — Latency Distribution Visibility:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the latency distribution visibility outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.02.F04` — Resource Saturation Awareness:** proportion of sampled operational performance visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of resource saturation awareness. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.02.F05` — Performance Trend History:** proportion of sampled operational performance visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of performance trend history. *PROPOSED*.

#### E08.01.03 — Dependency Status Visibility
Operations can inspect relevant supporting-service status for diagnosis.

- **`E08.01.03.F01` — VisibleDependencyHealth:** proportion of injected dependency faults surfaced at appropriate scope. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.03.F02` — Dependency Availability Recognition:** proportion of sampled dependency status visibility cases with accessible, accurate evidence of dependency availability recognition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.01.03.F03` — Upstream Failure Propagation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed dependency status visibility scenarios demonstrating upstream failure propagation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

### E08.04 — Conformance & Assurance Support
Supply evidence for evaluating whether system behaviour meets required constraints.

**Adjudicated FC-016 / FC-033 / FC-074 clarification — Independent Assurance Execution:** Assurance assessment **SHALL execute as a separately governed activity, logically independent** of the service, Praxis, Journey or processing activity being assessed. It SHALL evaluate applicable assurance criteria against available evidence and produce its own findings, qualifications and conclusions. Assurance execution SHALL NOT form part of the assessed activity's fulfilment or determine its operational outcome, and SHALL NOT recursively assure itself. This is logical/execution separation, **not** a mandate for separate JVMs or physical deployment. Evidence capture during ordinary processing is distinct from independent assessment through Dokimasia. R1.x/R2.x assurance conclusions remain observational/advisory, not autonomous operational or release authority.

#### E08.04.01 — Release Conformance Evidence
Reviewers can obtain evidence of required conformance for a released information system.

- **`E08.04.01.F01` — CompleteReleaseConformanceEvidence:** proportion of applicable controls with required verified release evidence. *PROPOSED*.

- **`E08.04.01.F04` — Independent Evidence Access:** proportion of representative release conformance evidence scenarios in which independent evidence access is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E08.04.01.F05` — Conformance Finding Qualification:** proportion of positive and negative release conformance evidence test cases meeting the applicable conformance finding qualification criteria. *PROPOSED*.

- **`E08.08.03.F01` — ProvenanceSetPrecise:** Selected lineage roots and temporal context are explicit. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E08.08.03.F02` — ChronologyPreservedInExport:** Evidence remains in agreed chronological semantic order. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
