- **`E08.08.03.F04` — MissingEvidenceQualified:** Unavailable but relevant evidence is not silently invented. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E08.08.03.F05` — CompleteReleaseGate:** No assembled package is released before whole-set authorisation succeeds. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E08.08.04 — All-or-Nothing Governed Release **[NEW C012]**
Withhold incomplete or partly unauthorised evidence products and account for staging, refusal and disposition.

*Demand: B10.06, B11.05, B13.04; evidence: PROV, TH, SEC; findings: R04, R23.*

- **`E08.08.04.F01` — ReleaseCompletenessVerified:** Released set contains every required requested element or a justified scoped exclusion. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E08.08.04.F02` — NoPartialReleaseAfterDenial:** Any mandatory denied item prevents any partial output delivery. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E08.08.04.F03` — StagedPayloadProtected:** Intermediate assembled information is never publicly addressable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E08.08.04.F04` — ReleaseTimeAuthorityCurrent:** Authorisation is valid at actual release boundary. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E08.08.04.F05` — FailureNoSensitiveLeak:** Failure status excludes protected evidence content while identifying remedial path. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

## E09 — Information Insight & Evidence Use
The system can organise information for queries, analysis and decision-supporting evidence without acquiring the decision authority itself.

### E09.01 — Information Set & Result Organisation
Establish referenced result sets with explicit meaning and context.

#### E09.01.01 — Version-Referenced Result Set
Consumers can use a governed collection of identified information versions.

- **`E09.01.01.F01` — ValidResultSetReferences:** proportion of sampled set references resolving to correct governed versions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.01.F02` — Version-Identified Search Membership:** proportion of independently checked version-referenced result set instances satisfying the specified version-identified search membership quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.01.F03` — Result-Set Identity Stability:** proportion of independently checked version-referenced result set instances satisfying the specified result-set identity stability quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.01.F04` — Source Version Resolution:** proportion of independently checked version-referenced result set instances satisfying the specified source version resolution quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.01.F05` — Result Membership Explanation:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed version-referenced result set scenarios demonstrating result membership explanation against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.01.02 — Preserved Result Set Context
Work can resume from an understandable previously identified result set.

- **`E09.01.02.F01` — ReusableResultSetContext:** proportion of persisted result sets retaining required context across resumption. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.02.F02` — Stored Query Intent:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed preserved result set context scenarios demonstrating stored query intent against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.02.F03` — Reusable Selection Parameters:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in preserved result set context for which reusable selection parameters is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.02.F04` — Result Context Integrity:** proportion of independently checked preserved result set context instances satisfying the specified result context integrity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.02.F05` — Context Expiration Qualification:** proportion of independently checked preserved result set context instances satisfying the specified context expiration qualification quality condition. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.01.03 — Result Set Provenance
Consumers can tell how and under which conditions a result set was formed.

- **`E09.01.03.F01` — AttributableResultSetFormation:** proportion of sampled sets with required query/formation provenance. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.03.F02` — Query Origin Attribution:** proportion of sampled result set provenance cases with accessible, accurate evidence of query origin attribution. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.03.F03` — Collection Assembly Trace:** proportion of sampled result set provenance cases with accessible, accurate evidence of collection assembly trace. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.03.F04` — Result Version Provenance:** proportion of independently checked result set provenance instances satisfying the specified result version provenance quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.01.03.F05` — Result-Set Review History:** proportion of sampled result set provenance cases with accessible, accurate evidence of result-set review history. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.01.04 — Stable Versioned Information Set **[NEW C012]**
Define a governed collection of IU version references with reproducible selection and work-context preservation.

*Demand: B13.05, B10.02, B11.03; evidence: IU, RESULT, PRAGMATON; findings: R23, R09.*

- **`E09.01.04.F01` — IUVersionRefsOnly:** Search result sets retain governed IU version references rather than uncontrolled payload copies. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.01.04.F02` — SetIdentityRepeatable:** A fixed set reference reproduces identical membership. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.01.04.F03` — SelectionProvenanceKnown:** Query criteria, requester and time context can be inspected. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.01.04.F04` — SetContextDurablyPreserved:** Relevant Ergon context can recover result-set membership. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.01.04.F05` — RestrictedMembersNotLeaked:** Results are re-evaluated for applicable access before disclosure. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E09.02 — Information Aggregation & Analysis
Combine information appropriately for analytical use.

#### E09.02.01 — Governed Information Aggregation
Information from multiple authorised sources can be assembled for an analytical purpose.

- **`E09.02.01.F01` — CorrectAggregatedScope:** proportion of test aggregates meeting applicable scope and source constraints. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.02.01.F02` — Aggregation Scope Conformance:** proportion of positive and negative governed information aggregation test cases meeting the applicable aggregation scope conformance criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.02.01.F03` — Aggregate Source Lineage:** proportion of sampled governed information aggregation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of aggregate source lineage. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.02.01.F04` — Completeness of Contributing Inputs:** proportion of independently checked governed information aggregation instances satisfying the specified completeness of contributing inputs quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.02.01.F05` — Governed Aggregate Disclosure:** proportion of positive and negative governed information aggregation test cases meeting the applicable governed aggregate disclosure criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.02.02 — Repeatable Information Analysis
Consumers can reproduce supported analysis from appropriately preserved inputs and methods.

- **`E09.02.02.F01` — ReproducibleAnalysisOutcome:** proportion of selected analyses repeatable within declared tolerances. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.02.02.F02` — Reproducible Analysis Inputs:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed repeatable information analysis scenarios demonstrating reproducible analysis inputs against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.02.02.F03` — Analytical Method Version:** proportion of independently checked repeatable information analysis instances satisfying the specified analytical method version quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.02.02.F04` — Deterministic Result Reproduction:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed repeatable information analysis scenarios demonstrating deterministic result reproduction against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.02.02.F05` — Analysis Limitation Disclosure:** proportion of sampled repeatable information analysis cases with accessible, accurate evidence of analysis limitation disclosure. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.02.03 — Temporal & Provenance Aware Aggregation **[NEW C012]**
Aggregate information while retaining source versions, definition changes and temporal applicability.

*Demand: B13.01, B11.04, B15.05; evidence: IU, PROV, INSIGHT; findings: R23.*

- **`E09.02.03.F01` — SourceVersionTraceable:** Reported aggregate can identify contributing governed versions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.02.03.F02` — TemporalWindowCorrect:** The declared occurrence/recording time basis is applied consistently. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.02.03.F03` — DefinitionChangeQualified:** Model changes affecting comparability are declared. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.02.03.F04` — RestrictedContributionControlled:** Security limits apply to aggregate disclosure or suppressions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.02.03.F05` — CalculationReproducible:** A fixed dataset and transformation definition reproduce an aggregate. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E09.02.04 — Population Insight Without Identity Leakage **[NEW C012]**
Produce allowed population-level metrics with defensible suppression, scope and data quality checks.

*Demand: B13.01, B13.03, B15.05; evidence: INSIGHT, TH, IU; findings: R12, R23.*

- **`E09.02.04.F01` — SuppressionPolicyEnforced:** Disallowed small-cell or sensitive breakdown release is blocked where policy applies. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.02.04.F02` — PopulationDenominatorKnown:** Reported rates state inclusion criteria and denominator version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.02.04.F03` — MissingDataBiasFlagged:** Material incompleteness is visible rather than hidden. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.02.04.F04` — TimeComparisonComparable:** Series identify source and definition changes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.02.04.F05` — NoIndividualDisclosure:** Aggregate pathways do not reveal protected individual records. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E09.03 — Operational & Business Information Reporting
Produce timely, provenance-aware information products and reports.

#### E09.03.01 — Operational Information Reporting
Operators can receive reports about digital service state and trends.

- **`E09.03.01.F01` — CorrectOperationalReport:** proportion of sampled reports matching accepted source evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.01.F02` — Relevant Operational Indicators:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed operational information reporting scenarios demonstrating relevant operational indicators against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.01.F03` — Current Reporting Period:** proportion of sampled operational information reporting cases with accessible, accurate evidence of current reporting period. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.01.F04` — Report Exception Highlight:** proportion of sampled operational information reporting cases with accessible, accurate evidence of report exception highlight. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.01.F05` — Scheduled Report Availability:** elapsed time from the applicable trigger to the scheduled report availability outcome; proportion within a context-defined interval. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.03.02 — Business Information Reporting
Authorised users can receive coherent information products for enterprise decisions.

- **`E09.03.02.F01` — TraceableBusinessReport:** proportion of sampled reports traceable to governed input versions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.02.F02` — Decision-Relevant Business Measure:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed business information reporting scenarios demonstrating decision-relevant business measure against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.02.F03` — Business Context Attribution:** proportion of sampled business information reporting cases with accessible, accurate evidence of business context attribution. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.02.F04` — Business Report Comparability:** proportion of sampled business information reporting cases with accessible, accurate evidence of business report comparability. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.02.F05` — Governed Business Disclosure:** proportion of positive and negative business information reporting test cases meeting the applicable governed business disclosure criteria. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.03.03 — Controlled Information Export
Authorised report outputs can be shared in supported forms.

- **`E09.03.03.F01` — GovernedReportDelivery:** proportion of tested deliveries satisfying scope, format and information rights. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.03.F02` — Authorised Export Purpose:** proportion of positive and negative controlled information export test cases meeting the applicable authorised export purpose criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.03.F03` — Minimum Necessary Export Content:** proportion of independently checked controlled information export instances satisfying the specified minimum necessary export content quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.03.F04` — Export Format Fidelity:** proportion of independently checked controlled information export instances satisfying the specified export format fidelity quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.03.03.F05` — Export Recipient Accountability:** proportion of sampled controlled information export cases with accessible, accurate evidence of export recipient accountability. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.03.04 — Controlled Business Evidence Reporting **[NEW C012]**
Provide accountable business and service reports without implying operation of the underlying business transaction engines.

*Demand: B07.06, B12.03, B15.04; evidence: INSIGHT, PROV, TH; findings: R12, R23.*

- **`E09.03.04.F01` — ReportingSourceKnown:** Report measures link to governed sources and calculation definitions. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.03.04.F02` — NoImplicitClaimsAdjudication:** A funding report is not represented as a claims decision service. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.03.04.F03` — ReportScopeAuthorised:** Recipient receives only data within authorised purpose. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.03.04.F04` — ReportQualificationVisible:** Known coverage and completeness limitations are explicit. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.03.04.F05` — ReportVersionReproducible:** A historical report can be reconstructed from pinned inputs. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E09.03.05 — Commissioning & Funding Accountability Data **[NEW C012]**
Expose trusted information supporting commissioning and funding review without presuming an internal payer platform.

*Demand: B15.01, B15.04, B15.05; evidence: INSIGHT, TH, PROV; findings: R12.*

- **`E09.03.05.F01` — CommissioningContextTraceable:** Recommendations identify source population/service need evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.03.05.F02` — EntitlementNotDigitalGrant:** Funding eligibility is distinguished from information access permission. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.03.05.F03` — ClaimsStatusNotAdjudication:** Status reports do not assert payment/claims processing authority. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.03.05.F04` — FundingReportReproducible:** Versioned inputs support independent audit of reported amount/status. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.03.05.F05` — ScopeOfSolutionExplicit:** Unimplemented or external financial functions remain documented as dependencies. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E09.04 — Evidence & Insight Presentation
Expose patterns, measures and supporting evidence with their limitations.

#### E09.04.01 — Evidence-Linked Insight Presentation
Analytical findings can be presented with source evidence and limitations.

- **`E09.04.01.F01` — SupportedInsightClaim:** proportion of sampled insights with accessible supporting evidence. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.04.01.F02` — Insight Source Traceability:** proportion of sampled evidence-linked insight presentation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of insight source traceability. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.04.01.F03` — Supporting Evidence Accessibility:** proportion of representative evidence-linked insight presentation scenarios in which supporting evidence accessibility is demonstrably available and usable. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.04.01.F04` — Qualified Insight Confidence:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed evidence-linked insight presentation scenarios demonstrating qualified insight confidence against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.04.01.F05` — Evidence-Insight Consistency:** proportion of sampled evidence-linked insight presentation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of evidence-insight consistency. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.04.02 — Trend & Change Interpretation
Users can inspect observed change through meaningful time and population context.

- **`E09.04.02.F01` — QualifiedTrendPresentation:** proportion of test trends with requisite time/scope/quality qualifications. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.04.02.F02` — Valid Time-Series Comparison:** proportion of independently checked trend & change interpretation instances satisfying the specified valid time-series comparison quality condition. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.04.02.F03` — Meaningful Baseline Selection:** proportion of applicable trend & change interpretation requests or situations with verified meaningful baseline selection. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.04.02.F04` — Trend Uncertainty Disclosure:** proportion of sampled trend & change interpretation cases with accessible, accurate evidence of trend uncertainty disclosure. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.04.02.F05` — Changed Pattern Detection:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in trend & change interpretation for which changed pattern detection is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.04.03 — Advisory Evidence & Confidence Presentation **[NEW C012]**
Expose recommendations and interpretations as qualified guidance with evidence and without unapproved decision authority.

*Demand: B02.04, B11.04, B13.06; evidence: ASSURE, INSIGHT, PROV; findings: R15.*

- **`E09.04.03.F01` — AdviceNotAuthoritative:** AI recommendations never write an authoritative business decision without authorised action. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.04.03.F02` — ConfidenceAndLimitsShown:** Known uncertainty and model limitations accompany advice. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.04.03.F03` — SourceEvidenceAvailable:** Recommendations link to available relevant evidence. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.04.03.F04` — HumanDecisionAttributable:** Acceptance/rejection by an authorised decision maker is separate and auditable. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.04.03.F05` — AdverseAdviceDetected:** Tests include plausible but unsupported recommendations and require appropriate qualification. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

#### E09.04.04 — Journey Outcome & Integrity Evidence **[NEW C012]**
Correlate stakeholder Journey value stages with separately evidenced business outcomes and IT contributions.

*Demand: B11.04, B12.03, B14.05; evidence: JOURNEY, ASSURE, PROV; findings: R18.*

- **`E09.04.04.F01` — StageValueIndependentlyTested:** Each scoped Journey stage has a value assertion not merely a technical call. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.04.04.F02` — ContributionNotDelivery:** An EC Service test is not reported as business-outcome fulfilment. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.04.04.F03` — StageExceptionBounded:** Failure and alternative paths carry explicit expected outcomes. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.04.04.F04` — EvidenceAcrossStagesCorrelated:** Identity, authority, output and commitment can be traced end to end. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.04.04.F05` — UnverifiedStageVisible:** Missing or untested Journey stage cannot be silently marked covered. *PROPOSED — unverified*.

### E09.05 — Governed Analytical Access
Ensure analytical access respects information rights, purpose and provenance.

#### E09.05.01 — Purpose-Bound Analytical Access
Analytical information use respects the declared authorised purpose.

- **`E09.05.01.F01` — AnalyticalPurposeConformance:** proportion of test accesses respecting purpose and compartment controls. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.01.F02` — Authorised Analysis Purpose:** proportion of positive and negative purpose-bound analytical access test cases meeting the applicable authorised analysis purpose criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.01.F03` — Dataset Eligibility Filtering:** proportion of positive and negative purpose-bound analytical access test cases meeting the applicable dataset eligibility filtering criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.01.F04` — Purpose Change Enforcement:** proportion of relevant changes or disruptions in purpose-bound analytical access for which purpose change enforcement is maintained or correctly recovered. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.01.F05` — Access Decision Trace:** proportion of sampled purpose-bound analytical access cases with accessible, accurate evidence of access decision trace. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.05.02 — Controlled Analytical Disclosure
Analytical products can be disclosed only to authorised audiences at permitted granularity.

- **`E09.05.02.F01` — CorrectAnalyticalDisclosure:** proportion of disclosure tests conforming to governing restrictions. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.02.F02` — Aggregate Disclosure Protection:** proportion of sampled controlled analytical disclosure cases with accessible, accurate evidence of aggregate disclosure protection. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.02.F03` — Sensitive Small-Cell Protection:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed controlled analytical disclosure scenarios demonstrating sensitive small-cell protection against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.02.F04` — Approved Analysis Output:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed controlled analytical disclosure scenarios demonstrating approved analysis output against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.02.F05` — Disclosure Audit Evidence:** proportion of sampled controlled analytical disclosure cases with accessible, accurate evidence of disclosure audit evidence. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.05.03 — Advisory Insight Identification
Automatically generated observations or recommendations remain explicitly advisory where required.

- **`E09.05.03.F01` — IdentifiedAdvisoryOutput:** proportion of applicable automated outputs marked and handled as non-authoritative. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.03.F02` — Explicit Advisory Label:** proportion of relevant, evidence-backed advisory insight identification scenarios demonstrating explicit advisory label against an explicitly defined assessment rubric. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.03.F03` — No Autonomous Authority:** proportion of positive and negative advisory insight identification test cases meeting the applicable no autonomous authority criteria. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.03.F04` — Recommendation Provenance:** proportion of sampled advisory insight identification cases with accessible, accurate evidence of recommendation provenance. *PROPOSED*.
- **`E09.05.03.F05` — Human Review Visibility:** proportion of sampled advisory insight identification cases with accessible, accurate evidence of human review visibility. *PROPOSED*.

#### E09.05.04 — Research Purpose & Data Release Controls **[NEW C012]**
Enforce approved research purpose, participant protections and evidence access without assuming a whole research solution is in Harmonia.

*Demand: B13.04, B13.05, B10.03; evidence: TH, PROV, INSIGHT; findings: R23, R12.*

- **`E09.05.04.F01` — ApprovedPurposeVerified:** Research information retrieval checks permitted purpose and applicable grant. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.05.04.F02` — ParticipantRestrictionsHonoured:** Consent, restrictions and representation conditions are applied. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.05.04.F03` — DataSnapshotPinned:** Study dataset references preserve a reproducible authorised version. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.05.04.F04` — ExtractAllOrNothingWhereRequired:** High-risk export checks reject unauthorised partial release. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
- **`E09.05.04.F05` — ResearchScopeQualified:** Capabilities are labelled contribution/optional where a full research runtime is not committed. *PROPOSED — unverified*.
